Zero-Shot Long-Horizon Dexterous Manipulation via Multi-View 3D-Grounded VLM Reasoning
Authors: Jisoo Kim, Sangwon Baik, Taeksoo Kim, Sungjoo Kim, Junyoung Lee, Mingi Choi, Hanbyul Joo
First: 2026-06-17T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:59:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a zero-shot framework for long-horizon dexterous manipulation that grounds language instructions into executable 3D task plans from calibrated multi-view RGB images. Rather than training an end-to-end policy, our system uses a vision-language model (VLM) to produce reference-frame task grounding and primitive-level 2D keypoints, then lifts them into 3D via multi-view fusion. This lifting combines triangulation of view-wise VLM groundings with reference-view ray voting, which searches along a semantic camera ray for geometrically consistent candidates across neighboring views. The resulting 3D keypoints support both pick-and-place and tool-use: for tool-use, we retrieve an object-centric atomic action corresponding to the inferred skill category and align its stored 6D tool trajectory to the scene; for dexterous execution, we expand the lifted grasp keypoint into a task-conditioned grasp affordance region and generate feasible grasp-motion pairs with an arm-hand motion generator. Real-world experiments show improved 3D grounding accuracy and execution reliability over single-view RGB-D grounding and fine-tuned VLA baselines. We further demonstrate long-horizon manipulation through closed-loop status verification and replan, enabling zero-shot execution on unseen objects and tool-use tasks in novel scenes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过多视角3D接地视觉语言模型推理实现零样本长时程灵巧操作
我们提出了一种零样本长时程灵巧操作框架，该框架将语言指令接地为可执行的3D任务计划，输入为校准的多视角RGB图像。与训练端到端策略不同，我们的系统利用视觉语言模型（VLM）生成参考帧任务接地和原始级2D关键点，再通过多视角融合将其提升至3D。该提升过程结合了各视角VLM接地的三角测量与参考视角射线投票，后者沿语义相机射线搜索邻近视角中几何一致的候选点。生成的3D关键点支持拾取放置和工具使用：对于工具使用，我们检索与推断技能类别对应的物体中心原子动作，并将其存储的6D工具轨迹对齐至场景；对于灵巧执行，我们将提升的抓取关键点扩展为任务条件化的抓取可操作区域，并通过臂手运动生成器生成可行的抓取-运动对。真实世界实验表明，相比单视角RGB-D接地和微调VLA基线，本方法在3D接地精度和执行可靠性方面均有提升。我们进一步通过闭环状态验证与重规划，实现了对未见物体和工具使用任务在新场景中的零样本执行。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for zero-shot dexterous manipulation without task-specific training, this work proposes a framework that grounds language instructions into 3D task plans using multi-view RGB images. It leverages a vision-language model to generate 2D keypoints and reference-frame task grounding, then lifts them to 3D via multi-view fusion combining triangulation and ray voting for geometric consistency. The 3D keypoints enable both pick-and-place and tool-use: for tool-use, it retrieves atomic actions and aligns stored trajectories; for dexterous manipulation, it expands keypoints into grasp affordance regions and generates motion pairs. Experiments show improved 3D grounding accuracy and execution reliability over single-view RGB-D and fine-tuned VLA baselines, and demonstrate long-horizon manipulation through closed-loop replanning on unseen objects and tools in novel scenes.
为实现无需任务特定训练的零样本灵巧操作，该研究提出一种框架，利用多视角RGB图像将语言指令转化为3D任务计划。系统首先通过视觉语言模型生成2D关键点与参考帧任务定位，再通过多视角融合（结合三角测量与语义相机射线投票）将其提升至3D空间以保证几何一致性。提升后的3D关键点支持拾取放置与工具使用：工具使用时检索对应技能类别的原子动作并匹配存储轨迹；灵巧操作时扩展抓取关键点为任务条件抓取区域，并生成可行的抓取-运动对。实验表明，相比单视角RGB-D方法和微调VLA基线，该方法在3D定位精度与执行可靠性上更优，且通过闭环重规划成功实现对未见物体与工具的长时序操作。
Native Active Perception as Reasoning for Omni-Modal Understanding
Authors: Zhenghao Xing, Ruiyang Xu, Yuxuan Wang, Jinzheng He, Ziyang Ma, Qize Yang, Yunfei Chu, Jin Xu, Junyang Lin, Chi-Wing Fu, Pheng-Ann Heng
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-17T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:59:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026. Code and models: https://github.com/harryhsing/omniagent
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Passive models for long video understanding typically rely on a "watch-it-all" paradigm, processing frames uniformly regardless of query difficulty, causing computational cost to grow with video duration. Although interactive frameworks have emerged, they often rely on global pre-scanning, and their context cost still scales with video length. We propose OmniAgent, the first native omni-modal agent that formulates video understanding as a POMDP-based iterative Observation-Thought-Action cycle. OmniAgent executes on-demand actions to selectively distill audio-visual cues into a persistent textual memory, effectively decoupling reasoning complexity from raw video duration. To operationalize this, we introduce (1) Agentic Supervised Fine-Tuning to bootstrap native active perception via best-of-N trajectory synthesis with dual-stage quality control, and (2) Agentic Reinforcement Learning with TAURA (Turn-aware Adaptive Uncertainty Rescaled Advantage), which leverages turn-level entropy to steer credit assignment toward pivotal discovery turns. Crucially, OmniAgent exhibits positive test-time scaling, where performance improves as the number of reasoning turns increases, validating the efficacy of active perception. Empirical results across ten benchmarks (e.g., VideoMME, LVBench) demonstrate that OmniAgent achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models. Notably, on LVBench, our 7B agent outperforms the 10$\times$ larger Qwen2.5-VL-72B (50.5% vs. 47.3%).
中文标题/摘要
标题：原生主动感知作为多模态理解的推理机制
长视频理解的被动模型通常依赖“全帧观看”范式，无论查询难度如何均统一处理帧，导致计算成本随视频时长增长。尽管交互式框架已出现，但它们往往依赖全局预扫描，上下文开销仍随视频长度扩展。我们提出OmniAgent，首个将视频理解建模为基于POMDP的迭代观察-思考-行动循环的原生多模态代理。OmniAgent按需执行动作，选择性地将视听线索浓缩为持久文本记忆，有效将推理复杂度与原始视频时长解耦。为实现此目标，我们引入：(1) 代理监督微调，通过最佳N轨迹合成与双阶段质量控制，引导原生主动感知；(2) 代理强化学习，结合TAURA（回合感知自适应不确定性重标量优势），利用回合级熵引导信用分配至关键发现回合。关键的是，OmniAgent表现出正向测试时扩展性，即推理回合数增加时性能提升，验证了主动感知的有效性。在十项基准测试（如VideoMME、LVBench）上的实验证明，OmniAgent在开源模型中达到最先进水平。值得注意的是，在LVBench上，我们的7B模型超越了10倍更大的Qwen2.5-VL-72B（50.5% vs. 47.3%）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of passive video understanding models that process all frames uniformly, the paper introduces OmniAgent, the first omni-modal agent that treats video comprehension as an iterative POMDP-based Observation-Thought-Action cycle. It selectively distills audio-visual cues into a textual memory via on-demand actions, decoupling reasoning complexity from video length. Trained via Agentic Supervised Fine-Tuning with trajectory synthesis and dual-stage quality control, and Agentic Reinforcement Learning using TAURA for turn-level uncertainty-aware credit assignment, OmniAgent demonstrates positive test-time scaling—performance improves with more reasoning turns. Across ten benchmarks including LVBench, it achieves state-of-the-art results among open-source models, notably outperforming a 10x larger model (Qwen2.5-VL-72B) on LVBench with a 7B parameter agent.
为解决被动视频模型因统一处理所有帧导致的计算效率低下问题，本文提出OmniAgent，将视频理解建模为基于POMDP的迭代式观察-思考-行动循环，通过按需执行动作选择性提取视听线索并存入文本记忆，从而解耦推理复杂度与视频时长。该方法结合基于轨迹合成的代理监督微调与双阶段质量控制，以及基于TAURA的代理强化学习以引导关键推理回合。在十项基准测试中，OmniAgent在开源模型中表现最佳，尤其在LVBench上，7B参数模型超越了10倍参数规模的Qwen2.5-VL-72B（50.5% vs 47.3%）
Beyond the Current Observation: Evaluating Multimodal Large Language Models in Controllable Non-Markov Games
Authors: Shengyuan Ding, Xilin Wei, Xinyu Fang, Haodong Duan, Dahua Lin, Jiaqi Wang, Yuhang Zang
First: 2026-06-17T17:59:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:59:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deploying multimodal foundation models as closed-loop policies increasingly requires conditioning actions on observations that are no longer visible. However, existing benchmarks either expose the full state, conflate hidden-state reconstruction with other agent skills, or test recall only after an episode has ended. We introduce RNG-Bench (Reconstructive Non-Markov Games), a benchmark suite designed to isolate a base model's ability to reconstruct past observations and act on them during multi-step interaction. RNG-Bench includes two complementary games: Matching Pairs, where card identities briefly revealed at specific locations must later be recalled, and 3D Maze, where egocentric views must be integrated into a spatial map. Both games are evaluated under a unified harness with three controlled difficulty axes: grid size, visual pattern, and observation modality. The benchmark further introduces a head-to-head duel protocol to control for instance-level variance and a Memory Gap metric that disentangles forgetting from poor action selection. The hardest configurations require contexts of roughly 128K tokens and 350 image inputs per episode, and remain far from saturated by frontier MLLMs. Memory Gap analysis shows that most residual errors stem from forgetting earlier observations rather than from suboptimal decision making. Finally, fine-tuning Qwen3.5-9B on optimal-policy rollouts and filtered model demonstrations improves performance on RNG-Bench and transfers to existing benchmarks without degrading general multimodal capability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越当前观测：在可控非马尔可夫游戏中评估多模态大语言模型
将多模态基础模型作为闭环策略部署时，越来越多地需要在不可见的观测条件下执行动作。然而，现有基准测试要么暴露完整状态，要么将隐藏状态重建与其他代理能力混为一谈，或仅在回合结束后测试回忆能力。我们引入RNG-Bench（重建性非马尔可夫游戏）基准测试套件，旨在隔离基础模型在多步交互中重建过往观测并据此行动的能力。RNG-Bench包含两个互补游戏：匹配对游戏，其中卡片身份在特定位置短暂显示后需后续回忆；3D迷宫游戏，需将第一人称视角整合进空间地图。两个游戏在统一框架下评估，控制三个难度轴：网格大小、视觉模式和观测模态。该基准进一步引入头对头对决协议以控制实例级方差，并引入记忆缺口指标以区分遗忘与决策不佳。最难配置要求每回合上下文约12.8万个token和350张图像输入，且前沿多模态大语言模型仍远未饱和。记忆缺口分析表明，大部分残余错误源于遗忘早期观测而非次优决策。最后，对Qwen3.5-9B进行最优策略回放与过滤模型演示的微调，可提升其在RNG-Bench上的表现，并在不损害通用多模态能力的前提下迁移到现有基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate multimodal large language models (MLLMs) in real-world closed-loop settings where past observations are no longer visible, the authors introduce RNG-Bench, a benchmark designed to isolate memory reconstruction and action-taking abilities in non-Markov environments. It features two games—Matching Pairs and 3D Maze—tested under controlled difficulty axes including grid size, visual pattern, and modality, with a head-to-head duel protocol and Memory Gap metric to reduce variance and disentangle forgetting from poor decisions. Results show that even frontier MLLMs struggle with contexts up to 128K tokens and 350 image inputs, with most errors attributed to forgetting rather than suboptimal actions; fine-tuning Qwen3.5-9B on optimal rollouts and filtered demonstrations significantly boosts performance on RNG-Bench while preserving general multimodal capability.
为评估多模态大语言模型在现实闭环场景中对不可见历史观测的记忆与决策能力，作者提出RNG-Bench基准，通过Matching Pairs和3D Maze两个游戏，隔离记忆重建与行动推理能力。实验在网格规模、视觉模式和观测模态三个维度控制难度，结果显示前沿模型在128K token和350张图像输入下仍表现不足，主要错误源于遗忘而非决策失误。Memory Gap指标有效区分遗忘与决策缺陷，对Qwen3.5-9B进行最优策略回放与过滤演示微调可提升RNG-Bench表现，且不损害其通用多模态能力。
Learning User Simulators with Turing Rewards
Authors: Yingshan Susan Wang, Cedegao E. Zhang, Linlu Qiu, Zexue He, Pengyuan Li, Alex Pentland, Roger P. Levy, Yoon Kim
First: 2026-06-17T17:58:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:58:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning to simulate human users in interactive settings could advance the training of agent assistants, evaluation of personalization systems, research in the social sciences, and more. Existing approaches generally do so by training a large language model (LLM) to match a single ground truth response, either by maximizing the log probability or by using a similarity reward. We instead propose {Turing-RL}: a Turing-Test-based reinforcement learning approach for training user simulator models. {Turing-RL} uses a discriminative Turing reward with an LLM judge to score how indistinguishable a generated response is from the real user's given the user's history, and the user simulator LLM learns to produce responses indistinguishable from what the user could have said with such rewards. Across two different domains--conversational chat and Reddit forum discussion--we find that {Turing-RL} consistently outperforms baseline methods on both LLM and human evaluation metrics. Our study suggests that optimizing for indistinguishability, rather than response matching, is effective for learning user simulators.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用图灵奖励学习用户模拟器
在交互环境中学习模拟人类用户，可促进代理助手的训练、个性化系统的评估及社会科学的研究。现有方法通常通过训练大型语言模型（LLM）匹配单一真实响应，或通过最大化对数概率或使用相似性奖励。我们提出{Turing-RL}：一种基于图灵测试的强化学习方法，用于训练用户模拟器模型。{Turing-RL}使用LLM裁判器作为判别性图灵奖励，评估在给定用户历史条件下，生成响应与真实用户响应的不可区分程度，用户模拟器LLM则通过此类奖励学习生成与用户可能说出的响应难以区分的回应。在两个不同领域——对话聊天和Reddit论坛讨论中，我们发现{Turing-RL}在LLM和人工评估指标上均优于基线方法。我们的研究表明，优化不可区分性而非响应匹配，对学习用户模拟器更有效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve agent training and user behavior modeling, this paper introduces Turing-RL, a reinforcement learning method that trains user simulators using a discriminative Turing reward judged by a large language model. Instead of optimizing for response similarity to ground truth, Turing-RL rewards generated responses based on how indistinguishable they are from real user replies given historical context. Experiments in conversational chat and Reddit forums show that Turing-RL consistently outperforms baseline methods on both automated and human evaluation metrics, indicating that optimizing for behavioral indistinguishability is more effective than traditional matching approaches.
为提升代理训练和用户行为建模，本文提出Turing-RL方法，通过图灵测试奖励机制训练用户模拟器。该方法不依赖于对齐真实响应的对数概率或相似度，而是利用大语言模型裁判评估生成回复在给定历史对话下与真实用户回复的不可区分性。在对话聊天和Reddit论坛两个领域实验中，Turing-RL在自动化LLM和人工评估指标上均显著优于基线方法，表明优化行为不可区分性比直接匹配响应更有效。
Freeing the Law with LOCUS: A Local Ordinance Corpus for the United States
Authors: Denis Peskoff, Joe Barrow, Christopher Vu, Diag Davenport
First: 2026-06-17T17:58:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:58:22+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Progress in legal AI increasingly depends on access to authoritative legal text at scale. Yet one of the most consequential layers of American law remains largely absent from existing machine-readable corpora: local ordinances. Local codes govern zoning, housing, business licensing, public health, noise, animal control, and many other domains of everyday regulation, but they are fragmented across vendor platforms designed for human browsing rather than bulk research access. We introduce LOCUS - the Local Ordinance Corpus for the United States - a comprehensive corpus and county-harmonized access layer for U.S. municipal and county ordinance codes. The raw corpus, available for release to researchers, represents nearly all publicly available municipal and county ordinance codes. The resulting raw corpus contains codes from 9,239 cities and counties. A smaller county-harmonized LOCUS access layer provides coverage for the largest 2,309 of 3,144 U.S. counties, accounting for a majority of the population. We use OCR to handle the myriad of document formats that have kept the law from being a public resource. We release the corpus with coverage metadata to support reproducibility, downstream legal AI research, and the incremental expansion of machine-readable access to local law. We train a collection of ModernBERT-based classifiers and scorers to facilitate analyzing U.S. local law among several dimensions, such as opacity and paternalism, that have not previously been studied at this scale. LOCUS-v1 and its derivative models are available at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/LocalLaws/LOCUS-v1
中文标题/摘要
标题：用LOCUS解放法律：美国地方条例语料库
法律AI的进步日益依赖于大规模获取权威法律文本。然而，美国法律中最关键的一层——地方条例——却长期缺失于现有机器可读语料库中。地方条例规范着土地使用、住房、商业许可、公共卫生、噪音、动物管理等日常监管领域，但它们分散在为人类浏览设计的供应商平台上，而非批量研究访问。我们推出了LOCUS——美国地方条例语料库，这是一个全面的语料库及县级统一访问层，涵盖美国市镇与县的条例代码。原始语料库可供研究人员发布，包含近9,239个市镇和县的条例代码。一个更小的县级统一访问层覆盖了美国3,144个县中的2,309个最大县，占总人口的多数。我们使用OCR技术处理多种文档格式，使法律成为公共资源。语料库附带覆盖元数据，以支持可重复性、下游法律AI研究及逐步扩展地方法律的机器可读访问。我们训练了一系列基于ModernBERT的分类器和评分器，以多维度（如不透明性与家长式治理）分析美国地方法律，这些维度此前尚未在如此规模上被研究。LOCUS-v1及其衍生模型可在：https://huggingface.co/datasets/LocalLaws/LOCUS-v1 获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, machine-readable access to local ordinances — which govern critical everyday regulations yet remain fragmented and inaccessible to AI — the authors introduce LOCUS, a comprehensive corpus of U.S. municipal and county codes. Using OCR to standardize diverse document formats, they compiled raw data from 9,239 jurisdictions and created a county-harmonized access layer covering 2,309 counties (majority of U.S. population). They trained ModernBERT-based classifiers to analyze local laws across dimensions like opacity and paternalism, enabling large-scale legal AI research. Experimental results demonstrate the corpus’s utility in quantifying legal patterns previously unstudied at this scale, supporting reproducible downstream research.
为解决美国地方条例（涵盖土地使用、住房、商业许可等关键日常治理领域）因分散于人工浏览平台而难以被AI大规模利用的问题，作者构建了LOCUS——一个涵盖9239个市/县的地方条例语料库，并提供覆盖2309个县（占全美人口多数）的标准化访问层。通过OCR技术统一文档格式，他们训练了基于ModernBERT的分类器与评分模型，用于分析如透明度与家长式治理等此前未被大规模研究的法律维度。实验表明，该语料库有效支持了对地方法律模式的规模化分析与AI研究。
Do as I Do: Dexterous Manipulation Data from Everyday Human Videos
Authors: Bhawna Paliwal, Haritheja Etukuru, William Liang, Pieter Abbeel, Nur Muhammad Mahi Shafiullah, Jitendra Malik
First: 2026-06-17T17:57:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:57:34+00:00
Comments: Project website: https://do-as-i-do.com/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
How can we scalably generate data for robotic manipulation, especially on human-like platforms such as dexterous multi-fingered hands? Learning from human videos has recently emerged as a likely answer to this question. However, difficulties in estimating hand-object interaction and crossing the human-to-robot embodiment gap have hindered the adoption of abundant monocular RGB-only human videos as the primary source of robot manipulation data. In this work, we present DO AS I DO, an algorithm to reconstruct and retarget monocular RGB human videos to multi-fingered dexterous robotic hands. DO AS I DO reconstructs hand-object interactions from various egocentric and exocentric in-the-wild video sources. The algorithm then retargets these hand-object interaction estimates into a sequence of actions executable in the real world, yielding robot-complete manipulation data from disparate human videos. Overall, DO AS I DO outperforms previous state of the art in estimating hand-object interactions and extracting dexterous manipulation trajectories from RGB videos, as we show in experiments on datasets with ground truths and on a dataset of video clips collected online. Our experiments enable us to propose an efficacy playbook for practitioners collecting human data for manipulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：模仿我做：来自日常人类视频的灵巧操作数据
如何可扩展地生成用于机器人操作的数据，特别是在类人平台（如灵巧多指手）上？从人类视频中学习最近被认为是解决这一问题的可行方法。然而，手与物体交互的估计困难以及跨越人类到机器人的具身鸿沟，阻碍了将大量单目RGB人类视频作为机器人操作数据的主要来源。在本研究中，我们提出了DO AS I DO，一种将单目RGB人类视频重建并重定向至灵巧多指机器人手的算法。该算法从各种第一人称和第三人称的野外视频源中重建手-物体交互，并将这些交互估计重定向为可在现实世界中执行的动作序列，从而从不同的人类视频中生成完整的机器人操作数据。总体而言，DO AS I DO在估计手-物体交互和从RGB视频中提取灵巧操作轨迹方面优于先前的最先进方法，我们在具有真实标注的数据集和在线收集的视频片段数据集上的实验中验证了这一点。我们的实验使我们能够为收集人类操作数据的从业者提出一套有效性指南。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable robotic manipulation data from human-like dexterous hands, this work introduces DO AS I DO, an algorithm that reconstructs and retargets monocular RGB human videos to generate executable robot manipulation trajectories. It estimates hand-object interactions from diverse in-the-wild video sources and translates them into real-world robot actions, overcoming prior limitations in human-to-robot embodiment and interaction modeling. Experimental results demonstrate superior performance over existing methods in both interaction estimation and trajectory extraction, validated on datasets with ground truth and online video clips, and provide practical guidance for human data collection in manipulation tasks.
为解决机器人灵巧手操作数据稀缺问题，本文提出DO AS I DO算法，可从单目RGB人类视频中重建并重定向手-物交互，生成可执行的机器人操作轨迹。该方法首先从多种第一人称和第三人称野外视频中估计手-物交互，再将其映射为真实世界可执行动作。实验表明，该方法在手-物交互估计与RGB视频轨迹提取方面均优于现有最先进方法，验证数据包含带真实标注的数据集及网络收集的视频片段。
Controllable Quantum Memory Capacity in Quantum Reservoir Networks with Tunable partial-SWAPs
Authors: Erik L. Connerty, Ethan N. Evans
First: 2026-05-12T20:22:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:56:25+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In the field of quantum reservoir computing (QRC), many different computational models and architectures have been proposed. From these models, we identify feedback-based models -- which use a feedback mechanism to re-embed classical measurements from the QRC -- and recurrent models -- which use a multi-register approach with memory and readout qubits -- as the two major competing architectures that have been discussed and validated on hardware. In this paper, we advance upon the recurrent architectures, which employ a two register approach to endow the QRC with a fading memory. While these approaches have been validated on hardware and have demonstrated great real-world performance on noisy-intermediate-scale-quantum (NISQ) quantum processing units (QPUs), the exact mechanism through which the memory capacity arises is not completely understood or fully controllable. With this, we augment the recurrent approaches and present a hardware-realizable mechanism, which we call a tunable partial-SWAP, that allows for the direct control of the rate of memory dissipation from a QRN implemented on a gate-based QPU. The theory behind this mechanism is discussed in terms of a controlled amplitude-damping channel and validation experiments using a randomized short-term memory capacity (STMC) recall benchmark and the NARMA-5 dataset are conducted using simulation and IBM QPUs, respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于可调部分SWAP的量子水库网络中可控的量子存储容量
在量子水库计算（QRC）领域，已提出多种不同的计算模型与架构。从这些模型中，我们识别出两类主要的竞争架构：一类是基于反馈的模型，通过反馈机制将QRC中的经典测量重新嵌入；另一类是递归模型，采用多寄存器方法，结合存储与读出量子比特。本文在递归架构基础上进一步发展，采用双寄存器方法赋予QRC淡忘记忆能力。尽管这些方法已在硬件上验证，并在噪声中等规模量子（NISQ）量子处理单元（QPUs）上展现出优异的现实性能，但记忆容量的确切产生机制尚未完全理解或可控。为此，我们扩展递归方法，提出一种硬件可实现的机制——可调部分SWAP，以直接控制基于门控QPU实现的QRN中记忆的消散速率。该机制的理论基于受控幅度阻尼信道，验证实验通过随机短期记忆容量（STMC）召回基准和NARMA-5数据集分别在仿真和IBM QPUs上完成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better understand and control memory capacity in quantum reservoir computing, this paper focuses on recurrent architectures that use two registers to implement fading memory. The authors introduce a tunable partial-SWAP operation as a hardware-realizable mechanism to directly adjust the rate of memory dissipation in quantum reservoir networks. Experimental validation through simulation and IBM QPUs using randomized short-term memory recall benchmarks and the NARMA-5 dataset demonstrates that this method enables precise control over memory retention, improving performance in noisy quantum hardware.
为提升对量子共振计算中记忆容量的理解与调控能力，本文聚焦于采用双寄存器实现衰减记忆的循环架构。作者提出一种可实现于硬件的机制——可调部分SWAP，用于直接控制量子共振网络中记忆的消散速率。通过仿真与IBM量子处理器上的实验，使用随机短期记忆容量基准和NARMA-5数据集验证，该机制有效调节记忆保持能力，提升在噪声量子硬件上的性能。
The Chandra-Gaia Catalog of Counterparts: Resolving ambiguous Gaia matches to X-ray sources in the Chandra Source Catalog using Machine Learning
Authors: V. Samuel Pérez-Díaz, Vinay L. Kashyap, Joshua D. Ingram, David Fouhey, Juan Rafael Martínez-Galarza, Pavlos Protopapas, Jeremy J. Drake, Dong-Woo Kim, Cecilia Garraffo
Venue: www
First: 2026-06-17T17:54:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:54:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted to The Astrophysical Journal. Website: https://www.samuelperezdi.com/chandragaia/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a framework to cross-match sources from the Chandra Source Catalog (CSC v2.1) with optical sources from Gaia Data Release 3. Unlike purely spatial approaches, we use source properties such as magnitudes, colors, and distances to identify true counterparts, detect chance coincidences, and resolve ambiguities when multiple plausible candidates exist. We define a training set of high-confidence matches using NWAY, a Bayesian cross-matching framework that accounts for positional errors and source densities. We train a gradient-boosted classifier (LightGBM) on a variety of features from both catalogs. Of the ~$254$k unique X-ray sources, we find counterparts for ~$113$k sources, of which plausible multiple counterparts are found for ~$7$k. We find no counterparts for ~$20$k sources for which separation-based cross-matching does find a match, and attribute half of these to chance coincidences. We validate the pipeline on the Chandra Orion Ultradeep Project (COUP), where the machine-learning matches reproduce 95% of NWAY cross-matches without using any positional information. We release a catalog of the ~$113$k Chandra-Gaia counterparts, together with ~$7$k alternative matches and ~$20$k ambiguous NWAY associations, supporting future population studies of sources detectable by both Chandra and Gaia. We discuss limitations and provide a generalization of the framework that is applicable in other cross-matching scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：钱德拉-盖亚目录：利用机器学习解决钱德拉源目录中盖亚匹配的模糊性
我们提出了一种框架，将钱德拉源目录（CSC v2.1）中的源与盖亚数据发布3中的光学源进行交叉匹配。与纯空间方法不同，我们利用源的性质（如星等、颜色和距离）来识别真实对应体、检测偶然匹配，并在存在多个合理候选体时解决歧义。我们使用NWAY（一种考虑位置误差和源密度的贝叶斯交叉匹配框架）定义高置信度匹配的训练集。我们在两个目录的多种特征上训练了一个梯度提升分类器（LightGBM）。在约25.4万个独特的X射线源中，我们找到了约11.3万个对应体，其中约7千个源存在多个合理对应体。对于约2万个源，基于分离度的交叉匹配发现匹配，但我们未找到对应体，其中一半归因于偶然巧合。我们在钱德拉猎户超深项目（COUP）中验证了该流程，机器学习匹配在不使用任何位置信息的情况下复现了95%的NWAY交叉匹配结果。我们发布了约11.3万个钱德拉-盖亚对应体目录，连同约7千个替代匹配和约2万个模糊的NWAY关联，支持未来对钱德拉和盖亚均可探测源的群体研究。我们讨论了局限性，并提供了一个可应用于其他交叉匹配场景的框架通用化版本。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to resolve ambiguous optical counterparts for X-ray sources in the Chandra Source Catalog, this study employs machine learning to improve cross-matching with Gaia DR3 by incorporating source properties like magnitudes, colors, and distances. Using a training set derived from high-confidence matches via the Bayesian NWAY framework, a gradient-boosted LightGBM classifier is trained to distinguish true counterparts from chance coincidences. The method successfully identifies counterparts for 113,000 of 254,000 X-ray sources, including 7,000 with multiple plausible matches, while rejecting 20,000 previously matched sources deemed chance alignments. Validation on the COUP field shows the ML pipeline matches 95% of NWAY results without relying on positional data, demonstrating robustness and generalizability.
为解决钱德拉源目录中X射线源与盖亚DR3光学源匹配时的模糊性问题，该研究采用机器学习方法，结合星等、颜色和距离等源属性提升匹配精度。通过基于NWAY框架构建的高置信度训练集，训练了梯度提升分类器（LightGBM）以区分真实对应体与偶然巧合。该方法在25.4万个X射线源中成功匹配出11.3万个对应体，其中7千个存在多个合理候选，同时排除了2万个此前被位置匹配误判的源（其中一半为偶然巧合）。在钱德拉猎户座超深场项目验证中，机器学习匹配结果与NWAY结果一致率达95%，且无需位置信息，表明方法稳健且具通用性。
UBP2: Uncertainty-Balanced Preference Planning for Efficient Preference-based Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Mohamed Nabail, Leo Cheng, Jingmin Wang, Nicholas Rhinehart
First: 2026-06-17T17:54:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:54:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Preference-based RL provides an approach to learning reward models from pairwise comparisons of behaviors, bypassing the need for explicit reward design. However, existing methods typically rely on passive data collection and suffer from poor sample efficiency, especially during the early stages of learning. We introduce a model-based approach that actively directs exploration by jointly reasoning over uncertainties in the reward, dynamics, and value functions. Our method, Uncertainty-Balanced Preference Planning (UBP2), uses ensembles of reward, dynamics, and value function models to evaluate candidate trajectories according to a unified score that combines expected reward, terminal value, and epistemic uncertainty. Planning under this objective yields an explicit tradeoff between exploitation and information acquisition without requiring ad hoc exploration heuristics. Under standard regularity assumptions, we establish sublinear regret guarantees for both finite-horizon and infinite-horizon settings. Empirically, experiments on the Meta-World benchmark show UBP2 achieves substantially higher sample efficiency than model-free preference-based methods and non-optimistic model-based baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UBP2：用于高效偏好强化学习的不确定性平衡偏好规划
基于偏好的强化学习提供了一种从行为成对比较中学习奖励模型的方法，无需显式设计奖励。然而，现有方法通常依赖被动数据收集，且在学习早期阶段样本效率低下。我们提出了一种基于模型的方法，通过联合推理奖励、动态和价值函数的不确定性，主动引导探索。我们的方法——不确定性平衡偏好规划（UBP2）——使用奖励、动态和价值函数的模型集合，根据综合评分评估候选轨迹，该评分结合预期奖励、终端价值和认知不确定性。在此目标下的规划无需依赖启发式探索策略，即可显式权衡利用与信息获取。在标准正则性假设下，我们为有限时域和无限时域场景建立了次线性遗憾保证。实验表明，在Meta-World基准上，UBP2比无模型偏好方法和非乐观模型基方法具有显著更高的样本效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of passive data collection in preference-based reinforcement learning, UBP2 introduces a model-based method that actively guides exploration by jointly modeling uncertainties in reward, dynamics, and value functions. It evaluates candidate trajectories using a unified score combining expected reward, terminal value, and epistemic uncertainty, enabling a principled trade-off between exploitation and information gain without ad hoc exploration strategies. Experiments on the Meta-World benchmark demonstrate that UBP2 achieves significantly higher sample efficiency compared to both model-free preference-based methods and non-optimistic model-based baselines, while also establishing theoretical sublinear regret guarantees under standard assumptions.
为了解决偏好强化学习中被动数据收集导致的样本效率低下问题，UBP2提出了一种基于模型的方法，通过联合建模奖励、动态和价值函数的不确定性来主动引导探索。该方法通过综合预期奖励、终端价值和认知不确定性对候选轨迹进行评分，无需额外探索启发式策略即可实现明确的探索与利用权衡。在Meta-World基准实验中，UBP2显著优于模型无关偏好方法和非乐观模型基基线，同时在标准假设下建立了次线性遗憾保证。
Rethinking Reward Supervision: Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation
Authors: Siyi Gu, Jialin Chen, Sophia Zhou, Arman Cohan, Rex Ying
First: 2026-06-17T17:54:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:54:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Post-training of reasoning language models is commonly driven by supervised distillation and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. Distillation often relies on chain-of-thought annotations that are expensive to obtain and may themselves be noisy, incomplete, or partially incorrect; even when the final solution is correct, an imperfect rationale can interfere with learning. Reinforcement learning with verified rewards, on the other hand, typically compresses evaluative feedback into a scalar signal, obscuring which aspects of a response should be improved. We propose \textbf{Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation}, a framework that incorporates rubrics as structured, fine-grained feedback for on-policy self-distillation. Our method conditions the teacher model on criterion-level rubrics and uses it to provide token-level guidance on the student's own sampled trajectories. This design avoids treating a single reference rationale as the sole supervision target. Instead, rubrics specify what a strong response should satisfy, enabling more fine-grained credit assignment over the reasoning process than scalar reward optimization. We instantiate this framework with a two-stage pipeline that first learns to generate task-specific rubrics and then trains a rubric-guided reasoner. We evaluate on a diverse suite of science reasoning benchmarks and results show that rubric-conditioned self-distillation effectively converts rubric-level criteria into token-level guidance over the reasoning process, surpassing GRPO by 1.0 points and OPSD by 0.9 points on average.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新思考奖励监督：基于评分标准的自蒸馏
推理语言模型的后训练通常依赖监督蒸馏和带有可验证奖励的强化学习。蒸馏常依赖于昂贵且可能噪声、不完整或部分错误的思维链标注；即使最终答案正确，不完美的推理过程也可能干扰学习。而基于验证奖励的强化学习通常将评估反馈压缩为标量信号，掩盖了应改进响应的哪些方面。我们提出\textbf{基于评分标准的自蒸馏}框架，将评分标准作为结构化、细粒度的反馈用于在线自蒸馏。该方法使教师模型依据分项评分标准进行条件化，并用于为学生模型自身采样的轨迹提供逐标记指导。此设计避免将单一参考推理作为唯一监督目标。相反，评分标准指明了优质响应应满足的条件，从而在推理过程中实现比标量奖励优化更精细的信用分配。我们通过两阶段流程实现该框架：首先学习生成任务特定评分标准，然后训练评分标准引导的推理器。我们在多样化的科学推理基准上进行评估，结果表明，基于评分标准的自蒸馏能有效将评分标准级准则转化为推理过程中的逐标记指导，在平均性能上超越GRPO 1.0分、OPSD 0.9分。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of chain-of-thought annotations in distillation and scalar rewards in reinforcement learning, the paper introduces Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation, a framework that uses structured rubrics to guide token-level self-distillation. The method conditions a teacher model on fine-grained rubric criteria and leverages it to provide feedback on the student model’s sampled reasoning trajectories, avoiding reliance on single reference rationales. Evaluated across science reasoning benchmarks, the approach consistently outperforms GRPO and OPSD by 1.0 and 0.9 points respectively, demonstrating its effectiveness in translating rubric-level feedback into precise reasoning improvements.
为解决蒸馏中链式思维标注成本高且易噪声、强化学习中奖励信号过于粗粒度的问题，本文提出基于评分标准的自蒸馏框架。该方法通过评分标准引导教师模型对生成轨迹提供逐标记反馈，避免单一参考推理路径的局限，实现更精细的推理过程归因。在多个科学推理基准测试中，该方法平均超越GRPO和OPSD 1.0和0.9分，验证了评分标准可有效转化为推理过程中的逐标记指导。
DiPOD: Diffusion Policy Optimization without Drifting Apart
Authors: Haozhe Jiang, Haiwen Feng, Pieter Abbeel, Jiantao Jiao, Angjoo Kanazawa, Nika Haghtalab
First: 2026-06-11T18:06:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:53:46+00:00
Comments: Project page: astro-eric.github.io/blogs/dipod/ Code: https://github.com/Astro-Eric/DiPOD-release
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
RL post-training has become increasingly pivotal for improving diffusion policies, but existing diffusion policy-gradient methods are often unstable and cannot achieve reliable policy improvement. We identify the cause as the double-drift phenomenon: optimizing a variational surrogate can let the ELBO separate from the true log-likelihood, which then makes the resulting proxy policy gradient misaligned with the true policy gradient of expected return. We propose \textbf{DiPOD}, a diffusion policy optimization framework that maintains tight-bound behavior throughout training by interleaving self-distillation with policy-improving gradient updates. This leads to a simple and practical algorithm: augmenting each diffusion policy-gradient update with an on-policy ELBO regularizer. Across diffusion language model post-training and continuous-control diffusion policies, DiPOD substantially stabilizes training and reaches higher rewards than previous methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DiPOD：无需漂移的扩散策略优化
强化学习后训练对提升扩散策略日益关键，但现有扩散策略梯度方法常不稳定，难以实现可靠策略改进。我们将其归因于双重漂移现象：优化变分代理可使ELBO与真实对数似然分离，导致所得代理策略梯度与真实预期回报策略梯度不一致。我们提出\textbf{DiPOD}，一种通过交替进行自蒸馏与策略改进梯度更新，确保训练全程行为紧绑定的扩散策略优化框架。这带来一个简洁实用的算法：在每次扩散策略梯度更新中加入基于策略的ELBO正则化项。在扩散语言模型后训练与连续控制扩散策略中，DiPOD显著稳定训练并获得比先前方法更高的奖励。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of existing diffusion policy-gradient methods due to the double-drift phenomenon—where variational surrogates cause the ELBO to diverge from true log-likelihood and misalign policy gradients—DiPOD introduces a framework that interleaves self-distillation with policy-improving updates to maintain tight-bound behavior. By augmenting each diffusion policy-gradient step with an on-policy ELBO regularizer, DiPOD achieves stable training and consistently higher rewards across both diffusion language model post-training and continuous-control tasks compared to prior approaches.
为了解决现有扩散策略梯度方法因双漂移现象（变分代理导致ELBO与真实对数似然分离，进而使策略梯度失真）而引发的训练不稳定问题，DiPOD提出在训练中交替使用自蒸馏与策略改进梯度更新，以保持行为边界紧致。通过在每个扩散策略梯度更新中加入在线ELBO正则化项，DiPOD在扩散语言模型后训练和连续控制任务中均实现更稳定训练和更高奖励，优于现有方法。
Reference-Driven Multi-Speaker Audio Scene Generation from In-the-Wild Priors
Authors: Michael Finkelson, Daniel Segal, Eitan Richardson, Shahar Armon, Nani Goldring, Poriya Panet, Nir Zabari, Benjamin Brazowski, Or Patashnik, Yoav HaCohen
First: 2026-06-17T17:51:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:51:50+00:00
Comments: Project page at https://finmickey.github.io/scena/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Existing multi-speaker dialogue systems bind speakers to utterances through structured supervision: per-turn tags, multi-stream transcriptions, or learnable speaker embeddings. These systems operate within speech-only pipelines that produce clean vocal sequences without the ambient texture of real conversations. We take a different approach. Our method, ScenA, conditions a text-to-audio flow-matching foundation model, pretrained on large-scale in-the-wild data, directly on multiple reference voices and a free-form natural language prompt that describes an entire multi-speaker audio scene. Leveraging such a foundational model allows us to inherit its capacity for natural, non-studio audio: background noise, room acoustics, overlapping dialogue, and spontaneous paralinguistic events, while adding multi-speaker control without any per-turn structure. Concretely, reference latents are concatenated into the model's token sequence and distinguished by lightweight identity-aware positional encodings. However, we identify a critical obstacle to this approach: the \textit{Reference Shortcut}. During training under standard noise schedules, the model can identify the matching reference by acoustic similarity to the noisy target, bypassing the text prompt entirely. We address this with a high-noise-biased timestep distribution that forces the model to rely on the text prompt for speaker assignment. We evaluate ScenA on the CoVoMix2-Dialogue benchmark, showing that it outperforms existing multi-speaker systems on speaker-binding metrics while generating rich conversational audio with overlapping speech, emotional vocalizations, and ambient sound. Our results demonstrate the advantage of using a general-purpose audio model conditioned on a free-form scene description, rather than passing structured dialog scripts through a speech-only pipeline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于野外先验的参考驱动多说话人音频场景生成
现有多说话人对话系统通过结构化监督（如每轮标签、多流转录或可学习说话人嵌入）将说话人与语句绑定。这些系统运行于仅语音的管道中，生成无真实对话环境纹理的干净语音序列。我们采取不同方法：ScenA方法直接以多个参考语音和自由形式自然语言提示（描述完整多说话人音频场景）为条件，驱动预训练于大规模野外数据的文本到音频流匹配基础模型。该基础模型使我们继承其生成自然非录音室音频的能力（如背景噪声、房间声学、对话重叠及自发副语言事件），同时无需每轮结构即可实现多说话人控制。具体而言，参考潜变量被拼接至模型的token序列中，并通过轻量级身份感知位置编码加以区分。然而，我们发现该方法的关键障碍——“参考捷径”：在标准噪声调度训练中，模型可通过目标噪声与参考语音的声学相似性直接匹配参考，完全绕过文本提示。我们通过高噪声偏置时间步分布解决此问题，迫使模型依赖文本提示进行说话人分配。我们在CoVoMix2-Dialogue基准上评估ScenA，结果表明其在说话人绑定指标上优于现有系统，同时生成包含语音重叠、情感发声及环境音的丰富对话音频。我们的结果证明，使用自由形式场景描述条件的通用音频模型优于将结构化对话脚本通过仅语音管道处理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of speech-only pipelines that lack ambient realism and structured supervision, the authors propose ScenA, a method that conditions a text-to-audio foundation model on multiple reference voices and a natural language prompt to generate multi-speaker audio scenes. The model concatenates reference latents with lightweight identity-aware positional encodings into the token sequence, enabling speaker control without per-turn annotations. A key challenge, the 'Reference Shortcut'—where the model relies on acoustic similarity rather than the prompt for speaker assignment—is mitigated by using a high-noise-biased timestep distribution during training. Evaluated on CoVoMix2-Dialogue, ScenA outperforms existing systems on speaker-binding metrics while producing audio with overlapping speech, emotional vocalizations, and realistic ambient sound.
为克服现有语音管道缺乏环境真实感和结构化监督的局限，作者提出ScenA方法，通过多个参考语音和自然语言提示直接引导一个预训练的文本到音频基础模型，生成多说话人音频场景。模型通过身份感知的位置编码整合参考潜变量，但面临“参考捷径”问题——模型可能依据声学相似性而非提示分配说话人。该问题通过高噪声偏置的时间步分布解决，强制模型依赖提示进行说话人分配。在CoVoMix2-Dialogue基准上，ScenA在说话人绑定指标上优于现有方法，同时生成包含重叠语音、情感化发声和环境音的逼真对话音频，证明了基于提示的野外数据条件生成优于结构化对话脚本处理的语音管道。
Data Intelligence Agents: Interpreting, Modeling, and Querying Enterprise Data via Autonomous Coding Agents
Authors: Anoushka Vyas, Aarushi Dhanuka, Sina Khoshfetrat Pakazad, Henrik Ohlsson
First: 2026-06-17T17:45:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:45:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Production data integration is bottlenecked by repeated, lossy handoffs between data owners, engineers, and analysts who must collaboratively discover, structure, and query enterprise data. We present Data Intelligence Agents (DIA), a system of three agents (Data Interpreter, Schema Creator, and Query Generator) that compresses this workflow by treating autonomous coding agents (ACAs) as a first-class abstraction: rather than emitting text, the agents generate, execute, validate, and repair concrete artifacts, draw on a shared memory for experience reuse, and surface each for review by domain experts. DIA is deployed in production for enterprise customers. We study the Query Generator in depth and evaluate it in fully autonomous mode across seven SQL benchmarks spanning four task categories and four dialects. It matches or surpasses the best published results on all seven, demonstrating that an architecture grounded in execution, built on ACAs and a shared memory, generalizes across the data intelligence workload with adaptation confined to natural-language instructions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：数据智能代理：通过自主编码代理实现企业数据的解释、建模与查询
生产数据集成受限于数据所有者、工程师和分析师之间反复且有损的手动交接，他们必须协同发现、结构化和查询企业数据。我们提出了数据智能代理（DIA）系统，包含三个代理（数据解释器、模式创建器和查询生成器），通过将自主编码代理（ACAs）作为一等抽象，压缩该工作流：代理不输出文本，而是生成、执行、验证和修复具体产物，共享记忆以复用经验，并将每个产物呈现给领域专家审核。DIA已在企业客户生产环境中部署。我们深入研究了查询生成器，并在涵盖四类任务和四种方言的七个SQL基准测试中以完全自主模式评估其性能，结果在所有七个基准上均达到或超越已发表的最佳结果，证明以执行为基础、基于ACAs和共享记忆的架构，能够泛化至数据智能工作负载，仅需自然语言指令调整即可。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and lossiness of manual data workflows in enterprises, the authors introduce Data Intelligence Agents (DIA), a system comprising three autonomous coding agents that automate data interpretation, schema creation, and query generation. These agents operate by producing executable artifacts, leveraging shared memory for experience reuse, and enabling expert review. Experimental evaluation of the Query Generator across seven SQL benchmarks in four dialects and four task categories shows it matches or exceeds the best published results, demonstrating that an execution-focused architecture with ACAs and shared memory generalizes well across data intelligence tasks with minimal adaptation to natural language instructions.
为解决企业数据协作中因人工交接导致的效率低下与数据丢失问题，作者提出了数据智能代理（DIA）系统，包含三个自主编码代理，分别负责数据解释、模式构建与查询生成，直接生成并验证可执行产物，共享记忆以复用经验，并支持领域专家审查。对查询生成器在七项SQL基准测试（涵盖四类任务与四种方言）中全自主模式的评估显示，其在所有测试中均达到或超越现有最佳结果，证明基于自主编码代理与共享记忆的架构在自然语言指令引导下具备跨数据智能任务的泛化能力。
Explaining Attention with Program Synthesis
Authors: Amiri Hayes, Belinda Li, Jacob Andreas
First: 2026-06-17T17:40:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:40:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A longstanding goal of research on interpretable deep learning is to replace opaque neural computations with human-meaningful symbolic descriptions. In this paper, we propose an approach for approximating the behavior of components of deep networks with executable programs. We focus on attention heads in transformer language models. For a given head, we first compute its associated attention matrices on a collection of randomly selected training examples. Next, we prompt a pre-trained language model with a summary of these matrices, and instruct it to generate a set of Python programs that can reproduce the associated attention patterns given only text from the input sentence. Finally, we re-rank programs according to how well our final set of programs predict behavior on held-out inputs. We demonstrate that a set of fewer than 1,000 such generated programs can reproduce the attention patterns of heads in GPT-2, TinyLlama-1.1B, and Llama-3B, achieving an average Intersection-over-Union similarity above 75% on TinyStories. Moreover, the best-fit programs can replace neural attention heads without substantially affecting model behavior: replacing 25% of attention heads with programmatic surrogates across the three models incurs only a 16% average perplexity increase, while maintaining performance on a variety of downstream question answering benchmarks. This work contributes a scalable pipeline for reverse-engineering attention heads in transformer models using human-readable, executable code, advancing a path toward symbolic transparency in neural models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用程序综合解释注意力机制
可解释深度学习研究的一个长期目标是用人类可理解的符号描述替代神经网络中不透明的计算。本文提出了一种方法，通过可执行程序近似深度网络组件的行为。我们聚焦于Transformer语言模型中的注意力头。对于给定的注意力头，我们首先在随机选取的训练样本上计算其关联的注意力矩阵。接着，我们提示一个预训练语言模型，以这些矩阵的摘要作为输入，并要求其生成一组Python程序，仅根据输入句子的文本即可重现相关注意力模式。最后，我们根据这些程序在未见输入上的预测表现重新排序。我们证明，少于1000个生成的程序即可重现GPT-2、TinyLlama-1.1B和Llama-3B模型中注意力头的模式，在TinyStories数据集上平均交并比相似度超过75%。此外，最佳拟合程序可替代神经注意力头，且对模型行为影响不大：在三个模型中替换25%的注意力头，平均困惑度仅增加16%，同时在多种下游问答基准测试中保持性能。本工作贡献了一种可扩展的管道，通过人类可读、可执行的代码反向工程Transformer模型中的注意力头，推动神经模型符号透明化的路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable deep learning, this work aims to replace neural attention mechanisms with human-readable, executable programs. The method involves collecting attention matrices from randomly sampled training examples, prompting a pre-trained language model to generate Python programs that replicate these patterns from input text alone, and re-ranking them based on predictive accuracy on held-out data. Experimental results show that fewer than 1,000 such programs can achieve over 75% Intersection-over-Union similarity in reproducing attention patterns across GPT-2, TinyLlama-1.1B, and Llama-3B on TinyStories, and replacing 25% of attention heads with these programs increases perplexity by only 16% while preserving downstream QA performance.
为实现深度学习的可解释性，本研究旨在用人类可读的可执行程序替代神经注意力机制。方法是通过随机训练样本计算注意力矩阵，引导语言模型生成仅依据输入文本即可复现注意力模式的Python程序，并按其在未见数据上的预测能力重新排序。实验表明，少于1000个程序即可在GPT-2、TinyLlama-1.1B和Llama-3B模型中实现平均75%以上的注意力模式交并比相似度。此外，用这些程序替换25%的注意力头仅使困惑度上升16%，同时保持下游问答任务性能，验证了在Transformer模型中实现符号透明性的可行性。
NeuMesh++: Towards Versatile and Efficient Volumetric Editing with Disentangled Neural Mesh-based Implicit Field
Authors: Chong Bao, Yuan Li, Bangbang Yang, Yujun Shen, Hujun Bao, Zhaopeng Cui, Yinda Zhang, Guofeng Zhang
First: 2026-06-17T17:39:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:39:21+00:00
Comments: TPAMI 2025; Project Page: https://zju3dv.github.io/neumeshplusplus/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recently neural implicit rendering techniques have evolved rapidly and demonstrated significant advantages in novel view synthesis and 3D scene reconstruction. However, existing neural rendering methods for editing purposes offer limited functionalities, e.g., rigid transformation and category-specific editing. In this paper, we present a novel mesh-based representation by encoding the neural radiance field with disentangled geometry, texture, and semantic codes on mesh vertices, which empowers a set of efficient and comprehensive editing functionalities, including mesh-guided geometry editing, designated texture editing with texture swapping, filling and painting operations, and semantic-guided editing. To this end, we develop several techniques including a novel local space parameterization to enhance rendering quality and training stability, a learnable modification color on vertex to improve the fidelity of texture editing, a spatial-aware optimization strategy to realize precise texture editing, and a semantic-aided region selection to ease the laborious annotation of implicit field editing. Extensive experiments and editing examples on both real and synthetic datasets demonstrate the superiority of our method on representation quality and editing ability. Project page: https://zju3dv.github.io/neumeshplusplus/

Diffusion-Proof: Recipe for Formal Theorem Proving Beyond Auto-Regressive Generation
Authors: Ruida Wang, Rui Pan, Pengcheng Wang, Shizhe Diao, Tong Zhang
First: 2026-06-17T17:38:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:38:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Enhancing the formal math reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) has become a key focus in both mathematical and computer science communities in recent years. While significant progress has been made in using state-of-the-art Auto-Regressive (AR) LLMs for formal theorem proving, these models suffer from inherent limitations. Their next-token prediction generation methods may yield suboptimal performance due to the challenges of long-range coherence and the compounding of errors over long sequences. Recent advancements in diffusion LLMs (dLLMs), which generate text through iterative denoising of a multi-token block, offer a promising alternative. However, the application of dLLMs to formal mathematics, where maintaining long-range coherence is critical, remains largely understudied. To address the challenges above, we propose **Diffusion-Proof**, to the best of our knowledge, the first framework to train and apply dLLMs for formal theorem proving. Our frameworks contain training and inference methods for two models. The first one is *dLLM-Prover-7B*, which performs whole-proof writing with long-range coherent tactic usage. The second one is *dLLM-Corrector-7B*, which is a novel large block diffusion-based correction model. It leverages the in-filling capabilities of dLLMs to perform local proof correction using bi-directional information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that **Diffusion-Proof** relatively significantly outperforms the AR LLM baseline trained under the same dataset. **Diffusion-Proof** achieves an absolute improvement of **1.61%** on ProofNet-Test and **6.14%** on MiniF2F-Test benchmarks compare to the baseline. Notably, **Diffusion-Proof** successfully resolves one IMO problem that more advanced thinking model DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B could not solve, showcasing the unique advantage of dLLMs in formal theorem proving.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Diffusion-Proof：超越自回归生成的正式定理证明配方
近年来，增强大型语言模型（LLM）在形式数学推理方面的能力已成为数学与计算机科学界的研究重点。尽管利用最先进的自回归（AR）LLM进行形式定理证明已取得显著进展，但这些模型仍存在固有局限性：其下一token预测生成方法可能因长距离连贯性挑战及长序列中错误累积而表现不佳。近期，通过多token块迭代去噪生成文本的扩散LLM（dLLM）提供了有前景的替代方案，但其在形式数学领域——长距离连贯性至关重要的场景——的应用仍鲜有研究。为应对上述挑战，我们提出**Diffusion-Proof**，据我们所知，这是首个用于训练和应用dLLM进行形式定理证明的框架。该框架包含两种模型的训练与推理方法：第一种是*dLLM-Prover-7B*，可实现长距离连贯的策略使用并完成完整证明；第二种是*dLLM-Corrector-7B*，这是一种新颖的基于大块扩散的校正模型，利用dLLM的填充能力，结合双向信息进行局部证明修正。大量实验表明，**Diffusion-Proof**相较在相同数据集上训练的AR LLM基线模型表现显著优越：在ProofNet-Test基准上绝对提升1.61%，在MiniF2F-Test基准上提升6.14%。值得注意的是，**Diffusion-Proof**成功解决了更先进思维模型DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B无法解决的一道IMO问题，凸显了dLLM在形式定理证明中的独特优势。

It trains two models: dLLM-Prover-7B for end-to-end proof generation with coherent tactic usage, and dLLM-Corrector-7B, a novel diffusion-based local correction model leveraging bi-directional in-filling. Experimental results show Diffusion-Proof outperforms AR baselines by 1.61% on ProofNet-Test and 6.14% on MiniF2F-Test, and notably solves an IMO problem that even the advanced DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B failed to resolve.
为了解决自回归LLM在形式定理证明中因长序列导致的连贯性不足问题，本文提出Diffusion-Proof，这是首个将扩散LLM（dLLM）应用于形式数学推理的框架。该框架包含两个模型：dLLM-Prover-7B用于端到端生成连贯证明策略，dLLM-Corrector-7B则利用双向填充能力进行局部修正。实验表明，Diffusion-Proof在ProofNet-Test和MiniF2F-Test上分别比基线模型高出1.61%和6.14%，并成功解决了一个DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B无法解决的IMO问题。
Modeling Branches for Active Manipulation using Iterative Parameter Estimation
Authors: Madhav Rijal, Rashik Shrestha, Trevor Smith, Yu Gu
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-06-17T17:37:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:37:06+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IROS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This study presents a method for modeling diverse plant branches by iteratively estimating material parameters to support delicate branch manipulation. Branch manipulation is necessary in agricultural robotics for plant repositioning, stabilizing, and clearing visual obstructions in dense foliage. The proposed method builds a tetrahedral branch model from point-cloud data and simulates its behavior using the finite element method. Using real observed deformation data, it iteratively estimates branch parameters and then computes an optimal path with a deformation-aware motion planner to move and stabilize branches within another robot's field of view. Across 30 trials on branches with varying geometries and material properties, the proposed method reduced the deformation energy by 35.69% while increasing the path length by 8.10% on average.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用迭代参数估计进行主动操作的枝条建模
本研究提出了一种通过迭代估计材料参数来建模多样植物枝条的方法，以支持精细的枝条操作。枝条操作在农业机器人中至关重要，用于植物重新定位、稳定及清除密集叶丛中的视觉遮挡物。该方法从点云数据构建四面体枝条模型，并使用有限元法模拟其行为。利用实际观测到的形变数据，迭代估计枝条参数，再结合形变感知运动规划器计算最优路径，以移动并稳定枝条至另一机器人视野内。在30次针对不同几何形状和材料特性的枝条试验中，该方法平均降低形变能量35.69%，路径长度增加8.10%。

How fast can you find a good hypothesis?
Authors: Anders Aamand, Maryam Aliakbarpour, Justin Y. Chen, Sandeep Silwal
First: 2025-09-03T21:44:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:35:20+00:00
Comments: Abstract abridged to meet arxiv requirements. This is the full version of a paper appearing at COLT 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In the hypothesis selection problem, we are given sample and query access to finite set of candidate distributions (hypotheses), $\mathcal{H} = \{H_1, \ldots, H_n\}$, and samples from an unknown distribution $P$, both over a domain $\mathcal{X}$. The goal is to output a distribution $Q$ whose distance to $P$ is comparable to that of the nearest hypothesis in $\mathcal{H}$. Specifically, if the minimum distance is $\mathsf{OPT}$, we aim to output $Q$ such that, with probability at least $1-δ$, its total variation distance to $P$ is at most $C \cdot \mathsf{OPT} + \varepsilon$. The optimal approximation for proper algorithms (where $Q \in \mathcal{H}$) is $C=3$ using $Θ(\log(n/δ)/\varepsilon^2)$ samples from $P$ and for improper algorithms (where $Q$ is not necessarily in $\mathcal{H}$) is $C=2$ using $\tildeΘ(\log(n/δ)/\varepsilon^2)$ samples from $P$. In the improper setting, the algorithm achieving $C=2$ [Bousquet, Braverman, Kol, Efremenko, Moran, FOCS 2021] runs in time which grows polynomially with $|\mathcal{X}|$ -- it does not run in finite time for real-valued distributions. A promising path towards improved runtime is to consider improper algorithms which output a mixture $Q$ of the hypotheses as such a distribution can be represented in $n$ words of memory. We show (1) a lower bound that no algorithm which outputs a mixture can achieve approximation better than $C = 3-2/n$ unless the number of samples is polynomial in $|\mathcal{X}|$, as well as (2) an algorithm which runs in time $\text{poly}(n)$ and achieves the same approximation guarantee. In the proper setting, [Aliakbarpour, Bun, Smith, NeurIPS 2024] provided an algorithm with $C=3$ running in $\tilde{O}(n/(δ^3\varepsilon^3))$ time. We improve this time complexity to $\tilde{O}(n/(δ\varepsilon^2))$, significantly reducing the dependence on the confidence and error parameters.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of efficiently finding a hypothesis close to an unknown distribution, this paper addresses the hypothesis selection problem under both proper and improper settings. For improper algorithms outputting mixtures of hypotheses, the authors establish a lower bound showing that achieving approximation better than C=3−2/n requires polynomially many samples in the domain size, while they also present a polynomial-time algorithm achieving C=2 with poly(n) runtime. In the proper setting, they improve upon prior work by reducing the time complexity from ~O(n/(δ³ε³)) to ~O(n/(δε²)), significantly decreasing dependence on δ and ε.
为高效选择接近未知目标分布的假设，本文研究假设选择问题在正则与非正则学习设置下的表现。对于输出假设混合的非正则算法，作者证明若想超越C=3−2/n的近似比，需对域大小进行多项式采样，同时提出一种在poly(n)时间内达到该近似比的算法。在正则设置中，他们将时间复杂度从˜O(n/(δ³ε³))优化至˜O(n/(δε²))，大幅降低了对置信度δ和误差ε的依赖。
Enhancing Decision-Making with Large Language Models through Multi-Agent Fictitious Play
Authors: Leyang Shen, Yang Zhang, Xiaoyan Zhao, Chun Kai Ling, Tat-Seng Chua
First: 2026-06-17T17:31:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:31:06+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have demonstrated great potential in solving tasks with execution complexity, by distributing subtasks across cooperative agents. However, this divide-and-conquer paradigm falls short on decision-making tasks that are also prevalent in the real world. These tasks require simultaneous reasoning from the stances of all involved stakeholders whose decisions are mutually dependent and thus cannot be solved in isolation. We characterize this challenge as stance entanglement, a form of decision complexity distinct from execution complexity. To address it, we propose Multi-Agent Fictitious Play (MAFP), a novel MAS paradigm that represents stakeholder stances as agents and formulates decision-making as an equilibrium-seeking process. Built on the game-theoretic principle of fictitious play, MAFP iteratively updates each agent's decision by best responding to the empirical mixture of other agents' past decisions. This enables agents to expose and address one another's weaknesses, progressively improving decision quality and robustness. We evaluate MAFP on challenging decision-making tasks that test the capability of deciding strategies for competitive scenarios prior to acting. MAFP outperforms both single-round and multi-round baselines on two complementary metrics, tournament strength and robustness, demonstrating its effectiveness in addressing stance entanglement.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过多智能体 fictitious play 增强大语言模型的决策能力
基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体系统（MAS）在解决执行复杂度高的任务时表现出巨大潜力，通过将子任务分配给协作智能体。然而，这种分而治之的范式在现实世界中普遍存在的决策任务上表现不足。这些任务要求从所有相关利益方的立场同时进行推理，其决策相互依赖，无法独立解决。我们将这一挑战定义为立场纠缠，一种不同于执行复杂度的决策复杂性形式。为解决该问题，我们提出多智能体 fictitious play（MAFP），一种新颖的 MAS 范式，将利益方立场建模为智能体，并将决策制定视为均衡寻求过程。基于博弈论中的 fictitious play 原理，MAFP 通过智能体对其他智能体历史决策经验混合的最佳响应，迭代更新每个智能体的决策。这使智能体能够相互暴露并解决彼此的弱点，逐步提升决策质量和鲁棒性。我们在具有挑战性的决策任务上评估 MAFP，这些任务测试智能体在行动前制定竞争性策略的能力。MAFP 在两个互补指标——锦标赛强度和鲁棒性上，均优于单轮和多轮基线方法，证明其在解决立场纠缠方面的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of LLM-based multi-agent systems in handling decision-making tasks with interdependent stakeholder perspectives, the paper introduces Multi-Agent Fictitious Play (MAFP), a novel paradigm that models stakeholder stances as agents and frames decision-making as an equilibrium-seeking process. Based on fictitious play from game theory, MAFP iteratively updates each agent’s strategy by best responding to the empirical distribution of others’ past choices, enabling mutual exposure of weaknesses and progressive improvement in decision quality. Experimental results show MAFP outperforms both single-round and multi-round baselines on tournament strength and robustness across competitive decision-making tasks, demonstrating its effectiveness in resolving stance entanglement.
为解决大语言模型多智能体系统在处理涉及多方互依决策任务时的不足，本文提出多智能体 fictitious play（MAFP）范式，将各利益相关方立场建模为智能体，并将决策过程视为均衡搜索问题。基于博弈论中的 fictitious play 原理，MAFP 通过智能体对其他智能体历史策略经验分布的最佳响应迭代更新自身策略，从而逐步暴露并修正彼此弱点，提升决策质量与鲁棒性。实验表明，MAFP 在竞争性决策任务上显著优于单轮与多轮基线方法，在锦标赛强度与鲁棒性两项指标上均表现更优，验证了其在解决立场纠缠问题上的有效性。
Observability and Consistency Analysis for Visual-Inertial Navigation with Anchored Feature Parameterizations
Authors: Mitchell Cohen, Vassili Korotkine, James Richard Forbes
Venue: IROS
First: 2026-06-17T17:30:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:30:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IEEE/RSJ IROS. 8 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents an analysis of the observability and consistency properties of filtering-based visual-inertial navigation systems (VINS) that utilize anchored feature representations. The unobservable subspace of VINS with anchored landmark parameterizations is shown to be independent of the estimated landmark state, which leads to improved estimator consistency properties without any additional modifications. However, the unobservable subspace is still found to depend on the estimated navigation state, necessitating additional consistency-enforcing techniques. Two methods to improve the consistency of VINS with anchored feature representations are presented. Simulation results showcase that all estimators employing anchored feature paramterizations exhibit improved consistency properties compared to algorithms that estimate features resolved in a global reference frame, especially in scenarios where feature initialization may be poor. Real-world experiments on the TUM-VI dataset showcase that the use of anchored feature representations alone can yield comparable performance to consistency-improved estimators employing a global feature representation, demonstrating the benefit of using anchored feature parameterizations for VINS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于锚定特征参数化的视觉惯性导航的可观测性与一致性分析
本文分析了采用锚定特征表示的基于滤波的视觉惯性导航系统（VINS）的可观测性和一致性特性。研究表明，采用锚定地标参数化的VINS的不可观测子空间与估计的地标状态无关，从而在无需额外修改的情况下提升了估计器的一致性。然而，该不可观测子空间仍依赖于估计的导航状态，因此仍需额外的一致性增强技术。本文提出了两种改进VINS一致性的方法。仿真结果表明，所有采用锚定特征参数化的估计器相比在全局参考系中估计特征的算法具有更好的一致性，尤其在特征初始化不佳的场景下表现更优。在TUM-VI数据集上的真实实验表明，仅使用锚定特征表示即可达到与采用全局特征表示并增强一致性的估计器相当的性能，证明了锚定特征参数化对VINS的有益性。

This paper investigates the observability and consistency of visual-inertial navigation systems using anchored feature parameterizations, motivated by the need to improve estimator reliability without complex modifications. The authors analyze how the unobservable subspace, while independent of landmark states, still depends on navigation state estimates, requiring additional consistency techniques. 该文研究了使用锚定特征参数化的视觉惯性导航系统（VINS）的可观测性与一致性，旨在无需复杂修改即可提升估计器可靠性。作者发现此类系统中不可观测子空间与地标状态无关，从而增强一致性，但仍依赖于导航状态估计，需额外一致性增强技术。文中提出两种改进方法，实验表明锚定特征表示在初始化不佳时比全局坐标系特征估计器具有更好一致性，且在TUM-VI真实数据测试中可媲美全局坐标系方法，证明其有效性。
P-K-GCN: Physics-augmented Koopman-enhanced Graph Convolutional Network for Deep Spatiotemporal Super-resolution
Authors: Xizhuo, Zhang, Zekai Wang, Fei Liu, Bing Yao
First: 2026-06-17T17:26:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:26:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
High-fidelity simulation of spatiotemporal dynamics is computationally prohibitive, necessitating efficient super-resolution techniques to reconstruct high-resolution data from coarse-grained inputs. Traditional data-driven methods often lack physical constraints, and simple physics-informed learning struggles with irregular spatial geometries and intricately evolving temporal dynamics. To tackle these challenges, we propose a Physics-augmented Koopman-enhanced Graph Convolutional Network (P-K-GCN) for spatiotemporal super-resolution on irregular geometries. Specifically, a continuous spline-based GCN is first designed to extract spatial dependencies directly from coarse graph, and Koopman operator theory is incorporated to project the nonlinear dynamics into a compact latent space where temporal progression is linearized. Second, we augment the optimization objective with a physics-based loss to force the data-driven reconstructions to adhere to physical laws for improving predictive fidelity and robustness. Finally, we provide a rigorous theoretical analysis, establishing that the physics augmentation and Koopman regularization mathematically guarantees a reduction in super-resolution error by diminishing Rademacher complexity and tightening generalization bounds. We evaluate our framework on reconstructing spatially high-resolution cardiac electrodynamics across a 3D heart geometry from sparse low-resolution measurements. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior accuracy compared to baseline models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：P-K-GCN：用于深度时空超分辨率的物理增强Koopman增强图卷积网络
高保真时空动态模拟计算成本高昂，亟需高效的超分辨率技术从粗粒度输入重建高分辨率数据。传统数据驱动方法常缺乏物理约束，简单物理信息学习难以处理不规则空间几何和复杂演变的时序动态。为应对这些挑战，我们提出一种用于不规则几何上时空超分辨率的物理增强Koopman增强图卷积网络（P-K-GCN）。具体而言，首先设计基于连续样条的图卷积网络，直接从粗粒度图中提取空间依赖关系；并引入Koopman算子理论，将非线性动态投影至紧凑的潜在空间，使时序演化线性化。其次，通过物理约束损失项增强优化目标，迫使数据驱动重建结果符合物理定律，提升预测精度与鲁棒性。最后，我们提供严谨的理论分析，证明物理增强与Koopman正则化在数学上可降低超分辨率误差，通过减小Rademacher复杂度并收紧泛化界实现。我们在3D心脏几何上从稀疏低分辨率测量重建高分辨率心脏电动力学，数值实验表明本方法相较基线模型精度更优。

To address the computational intractability of high-fidelity spatiotemporal simulation, the paper proposes P-K-GCN, a physics-augmented Koopman-enhanced graph convolutional network designed for super-resolution on irregular geometries. The method first employs a spline-based GCN to capture spatial dependencies from coarse graphs, then leverages Koopman operator theory to linearize temporal dynamics in a latent space. A physics-based loss is integrated into the optimization to enforce physical consistency, enhancing prediction accuracy and robustness. Experimental results on 3D cardiac electrodynamics reconstruction from sparse inputs show that P-K-GCN outperforms baseline models in super-resolution accuracy.
为解决高保真时空模拟的计算不可行性，本文提出P-K-GCN方法，一种结合物理约束与Koopman理论的图卷积网络，用于不规则几何结构上的时空超分辨率重建。该方法首先采用基于样条的图卷积网络提取粗网格的空间依赖关系，再利用Koopman算子将非线性时序动态线性化至紧凑隐空间，同时引入物理损失函数约束输出符合物理规律，提升预测精度与鲁棒性。在3D心脏电动力学重建实验中，该方法在稀疏输入下显著优于基线模型，理论分析亦证明其通过降低Rademacher复杂度和收紧泛化界实现误差降低。
Optimal scenario design for climate emulation
Authors: Christopher B. Womack, Shahine Bouabid, Andrei Sokolov, Popat Salunke, Glenn Flierl, Sebastian D. Eastham, Noelle E. Selin
First: 2026-06-17T17:26:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:26:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As deep learning for physical systems continues to grow in popularity, efforts to improve generalizability have primarily focused on designing architectures that embed physical constraints. However, for machine-learning surrogate climate models (emulators), we show that the low structural diversity in existing scenarios commonly used to generate training data places a ceiling on predictive skill. Here, we examine whether training datasets themselves can be optimized to improve generalization. We introduce a method to create datasets that produce emulators capable of generalizing to new, structurally different scenarios absent from the training data. We use a differentiable Simple Climate Model (SCM) to calculate the sensitivity of emulator loss to perturbations in the training data, iteratively updating the training data to maximize emulator skill. For an SCM, training on one scenario optimized in this fashion outperforms an emulator trained on six standard ScenarioMIP pathways. We achieve this higher predictive skill despite training on a smaller dataset, finding that our emulator successfully isolates distinct physical behaviors of different climate forcing agents (e.g., greenhouse gases vs. aerosols) without single-forcing runs. We then demonstrate that scenarios optimized using an SCM, when used to drive an intermediate-complexity climate model, produce a training dataset that yields a more skillful emulator than training on ScenarioMIP outputs. Our results suggest that, in the compute-constrained environment of running full-scale climate models, generating a small number of dynamically rich scenarios provides greater marginal value for emulation and characterizing system responses than expanding the suite of traditional emissions pathways.
中文标题/摘要
标题：气候模拟的最优场景设计
随着深度学习在物理系统中的应用日益普及，提升泛化能力的研究主要集中在设计嵌入物理约束的架构上。然而，对于机器学习代理气候模型（模拟器），我们发现现有常用于生成训练数据的场景结构多样性较低，限制了预测能力。本文探讨是否可通过优化训练数据集来提升泛化能力。我们提出一种方法，生成能产生泛化至训练数据中未包含的新结构场景的模拟器数据集。我们利用可微分的简单气候模型（SCM）计算模拟器损失对训练数据扰动的敏感性，迭代更新训练数据以最大化模拟器性能。对于SCM，采用此方法优化的单一场景训练效果优于基于六条标准ScenarioMIP路径的训练。尽管训练数据量更小，我们的模拟器仍能成功区分不同气候强迫因子（如温室气体与气溶胶）的物理行为，无需单强迫运行。随后我们证明，使用SCM优化的场景驱动中等复杂度气候模型，其训练数据集比直接使用ScenarioMIP输出训练的模拟器更具预测能力。结果表明，在计算受限的全尺度气候模型运行环境中，生成少量动态丰富的场景比扩展传统排放路径组合，对模拟和刻画系统响应具有更高的边际价值。

When applied to an intermediate-complexity climate model, the optimized scenarios also yield more skillful emulators than standard pathways, suggesting that generating fewer dynamically rich scenarios is more efficient than expanding traditional emissions scenarios under computational constraints.

On the Memorization Behavior of LLMs in Generative Recommendation: Observations, Implications, and Training Strategies
Authors: Sunwoo Kim, Sunkyung Lee, Clark Mingxuan Ju, Donald Loveland, Bhuvesh Kumar, Kijung Shin, Neil Shah, Liam Collins
First: 2026-06-15T20:34:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:24:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative recommendation (GR) has emerged as a promising direction for recommender systems. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have been increasingly adopted for GR, as their rich pretrained knowledge is expected to help them generalize beyond common user behavior patterns that traditional memorization-oriented baselines can capture. However, existing LLM-based GR works largely ignore LLMs' well-known tendency to memorize, which, if present in LLMs fine-tuned for GR, would restrict their utilization of pretrained knowledge. In this work, we investigate this concern by examining one-hop memorization, where a model recommends items that are direct successors of items in the training data. We show that LLMs do this more than non-LLM-based GR models-in fact, the vast majority of their gains over GR baselines are actually on users whose target items can be predicted through one-hop memorization. We intuit that improving performance on the remaining users requires LLMs to learn richer item-item relations beyond one-hop transitions. To achieve this, we propose IIRG, a novel training strategy that teaches LLMs to capture: (1) collaborative relations derived from item co-occurrences across multiple hops in user sequences, and (2) semantic relations among items with similar themes, both of which can serve as useful recommendation signals. We show that IIRG significantly improves over LLMs trained solely with standard next-item prediction, with especially large gains for users whose test items are not covered by train-time one-hop transitions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生成式推荐中大语言模型记忆行为研究：观察、影响与训练策略
生成式推荐（GR）已成为推荐系统有前景的研究方向。近期，大语言模型（LLMs）被越来越多地用于GR，因其丰富的预训练知识有望帮助其超越传统以记忆为导向的基线模型所能捕捉的常见用户行为模式。然而，现有基于LLM的GR研究大多忽视了LLMs众所周知的记忆倾向，若该倾向存在于为GR微调的LLMs中，将限制其对预训练知识的利用。本文通过考察单跳记忆行为——即模型推荐训练数据中某物品的直接后继物品——来探究这一问题。我们发现，LLMs比非LLM基的GR模型更倾向于此类记忆行为，事实上，LLMs相对于GR基线模型的绝大部分性能提升，实际上来自于那些其目标物品可通过单跳记忆预测的用户。我们推测，要提升其余用户的表现，LLMs需学习超越单跳过渡的更丰富的物品-物品关系。为此，我们提出IIRG，一种新颖的训练策略，旨在引导LLMs捕捉：（1）从用户序列中多跳物品共现中推导出的协同关系；（2）主题相似物品间的语义关系，二者均可作为有用的推荐信号。实验表明，IIRG显著优于仅使用标准下一项预测训练的LLMs，尤其对测试物品未被训练时单跳过渡覆盖的用户，收益尤为显著。

Motivated by the concern that LLMs may over-rely on memorization rather than generalizing in generative recommendation, this work investigates one-hop memorization behavior in LLMs fine-tuned for GR, finding that most performance gains stem from memorizing direct item successors in training data. To address this, the authors propose IIRG, a training strategy that encourages LLMs to learn multi-hop collaborative and semantic item relations, which improves recommendation accuracy—especially for users whose target items are not reachable via one-hop memorization.
鉴于LLM在生成推荐中可能过度依赖记忆而非泛化，本研究考察了LLM在推荐任务中的一跳记忆行为。研究发现，LLM主要通过记忆训练数据中的直接项目过渡获得性能优势，且大部分性能提升来自可被一跳预测的用户。为解决此问题，作者提出IIRG训练策略，引导LLM学习多跳协同关系和语义相似项目关系。实验表明，IIRG显著优于仅基于下一项目预测的训练方法，尤其对训练集中无法一跳预测的用户效果更佳。
Confidence is Not Reliability: Rethinking MC Dropout in Brain Tumour Segmentation
Authors: Xin Ci Wong, Duygu Sarikaya, Kieran Zucker, Marc De Kamps, Nishant Ravikumar
First: 2026-06-17T17:24:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:24:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted for MIUA2016
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Glioma segmentation in multiparametric MRI is a critical component of treatment planning. A segmentation model that fails silently on treatment-critical sub-regions represents a patient safety risk that overlap-based metrics such as Dice scores cannot expose. We ask whether voxel-level uncertainty estimation via Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout can reliably identify segmentation errors in clinically critical sub-regions, and whether calibration failure modes are detectable from standard reporting metrics alone. In an empirical two-model case study on 126 BraTS21 patients, we evaluate a high-performance pretrained SegResNet and a locally trained UNet with residual units (UNet-Res). MC dropout preserved segmentation accuracy ($|Δ\text{Dice}|$ $<0.01$) while achieving strong uncertainty-error alignment (AUROC for entropy (H) $\approx$0.97), indicating uncertainty correctly ranks erroneous voxels above correct ones. Entropy-based patient stratification identified a high-uncertainty subgroup with substantially lower segmentation performance (median whole-tumour Dice $0.835$ vs. $0.925$), supporting uncertainty as a practical triage signal. However, global alignment can mask important region-specific differences. Despite similar AUROC, UNet-Res exhibited near-zero enhancing tumour entropy ($0.054$) and Expected Calibration Error (ECE) of $0.915$, with a Dice of only $0.714$, indicating severely miscalibrated confidence on the most clinically critical sub-region, a failure mode invisible to standard Dice and AUROC reporting. These findings demonstrate that strong uncertainty-error alignment is necessary but insufficient for clinical safety: sub-region-specific calibration assessment must accompany AUROC evaluation when selecting models for clinical deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：置信度并非可靠性：重新思考脑肿瘤分割中的MC Dropout
多参数MRI中的胶质瘤分割是治疗规划的关键环节。若分割模型在治疗关键亚区域上静默失效，其患者安全风险是基于重叠的度量（如Dice分数）无法揭示的。我们探讨了通过蒙特卡洛（MC）Dropout进行体素级不确定性估计是否能可靠识别临床关键亚区域的分割错误，以及校准失败模式是否仅凭标准报告指标即可检测。在126例BraTS21患者的实证双模型案例研究中，我们评估了高性能预训练的SegResNet与本地训练的带残差单元的UNet（UNet-Res）。MC Dropout保持了分割精度（|ΔDice| < 0.01），同时实现了强不确定性-错误对齐（熵H的AUROC ≈ 0.97），表明不确定性能正确将错误体素排在正确体素之上。基于熵的患者分层识别出高不确定性亚组，其分割性能显著较低（全肿瘤Dice中位数0.835 vs. 0.925），支持不确定性作为实用的分诊信号。然而，全局对齐可能掩盖重要区域特异性差异。尽管AUROC相似，UNet-Res在增强肿瘤区域熵（0.054）和预期校准误差（ECE）为0.915，Dice仅为0.714，表明在最临床关键的亚区域上置信度严重失校，而标准Dice和AUROC报告对此无能为力。这些发现表明，强不确定性-错误对齐虽必要，但不足以保障临床安全：在选择临床部署模型时，必须结合AUROC评估进行亚区域特异性校准评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect segmentation errors in clinically critical brain tumour regions where standard metrics like Dice scores fail, this study evaluates MC Dropout’s ability to estimate voxel-level uncertainty in glioma segmentation. Using two models—SegResNet and UNet-Res—on 126 BraTS21 patients, it finds that MC Dropout maintains high accuracy while achieving strong uncertainty-error alignment (AUROC ≈0.97 for entropy), indicating reliable error ranking. However, global metrics mask critical failures: UNet-Res showed near-zero entropy and high ECE (0.915) in enhancing tumour regions with low Dice (0.714), revealing severe miscalibration invisible to standard metrics. This demonstrates that while uncertainty alignment is necessary, region-specific calibration is essential for clinical safety.
为解决标准Dice等指标无法识别临床关键区域分割错误的问题，本研究探讨MC Dropout是否能可靠估计体素级不确定性并发现错误。在126名BraTS21患者数据上对比SegResNet与UNet-Res模型，结果显示MC Dropout保持分割精度的同时，不确定性与错误高度对齐（熵AUROC≈0.97），且基于熵的患者分层发现高不确定性亚组表现显著更差（中位全瘤Dice为0.835 vs. 0.925）。然而全局指标掩盖了关键区域差异：UNet-Res在增强肿瘤区域熵接近零、ECE高达0.915，Dice仅0.714，表明其在临床最关键区域严重误校准，而标准指标未能揭示此问题。
Does VLA Even Know the Basics? Measuring Commonsense and World Knowledge Retention in Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Nikita Kachaev, Andrey Moskalenko, Matvey Skripkin, Nikita Kurlaev, Daria Pugacheva, Albina Burlova, Mikhail Kolosov, Denis Shepelev, Andrey Kuznetsov, Elena Tutubalina, Aleksandr I. Panov, Alexey K. Kovalev, Vlad Shakhuro
First: 2026-06-17T17:20:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:20:46+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://tttonyalpha.github.io/act2answer/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Embodied Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are typically obtained by fine-tuning powerful pretrained VLMs on robotics data, yet it is unclear how much commonsense and factual knowledge they retain after adaptation. Failures on knowledge-sensitive tasks are ambiguous, conflating missing knowledge with poor generalization of low-level control. We introduce Act2Answer, a lightweight protocol that adapts VLM knowledge benchmarks to VLA evaluation by requiring agents to answer through action. Each question becomes a short tabletop episode where the agent performs a single object-placement action to select among candidate answers, yielding an action-grounded success rate with reduced control confounds. We curate a test suite of such environments across diverse commonsense and world-knowledge categories and introduce layerwise intent probing to localize answer-relevant information across the VLM backbone and action head. In a large-scale study of 7 VLA models and 9 VLM baselines, we systematically rank models across categories, finding that VLAs show solid performance on simple concepts while exhibiting larger gaps on richer semantic categories relative to their source VLMs, that VQA co-training is associated with better knowledge retention, and that answer-relevant signals peak in middle VLA layers but attenuate in upper layers. Act2Answer is available at https://tttonyalpha.github.io/act2answer/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉-语言-动作模型真的掌握基础知识吗？衡量具身模型中的常识与世界知识保留能力
具身视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通常通过在机器人数据上微调强大的预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）获得，但其在适应后保留了多少常识和事实知识尚不明确。在知识敏感任务上的失败难以区分是知识缺失还是底层控制泛化能力不足。我们提出了Act2Answer，一种轻量级协议，通过要求代理通过动作回答问题，将VLM知识基准适配至VLA评估。每个问题转化为一个简短的桌面场景，代理执行单次物体放置动作以在候选答案中选择，从而获得动作锚定的成功率并减少控制干扰。我们为不同常识与世界知识类别精心构建了测试环境套件，并引入逐层意图探测技术，定位VLM主干与动作头中与答案相关的信息。在对7个VLA模型和9个VLM基线的大规模研究中，我们系统性地按类别排名模型，发现VLA在简单概念上表现稳健，但在更丰富的语义类别上与源VLM相比差距更大；视觉问答协同训练与知识保留正相关；答案相关信号在VLA中间层达到峰值，而在高层层减弱。Act2Answer项目地址：https://tttonyalpha.github.io/act2answer/。
