This study investigates how much commonsense and world knowledge Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models retain after fine-tuning on robotics data, addressing ambiguity in knowledge-sensitive task failures that may stem from poor control rather than missing knowledge. The authors introduce Act2Answer, a lightweight evaluation protocol that transforms knowledge benchmarks into tabletop episodes where agents select answers via single object-placement actions, reducing confounds from low-level control. They evaluate 7 VLA models and 9 VLM baselines across diverse knowledge categories, finding that VLAs perform well on simple concepts but show larger performance gaps on complex semantic tasks compared to their source VLMs. VQA co-training correlates with better knowledge retention, and layerwise probing reveals that answer-relevant signals peak in middle VLA layers and weaken in upper layers.
该研究探讨了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人数据微调后保留了多少常识与世界知识，旨在区分知识缺失与控制能力不足的混淆。作者提出Act2Answer评估协议，将知识基准转化为动作导向的桌面任务，要求代理通过放置物体选择答案，从而降低控制干扰。在7个VLA模型和9个VLM基线模型的对比中发现，VLA在简单概念上表现良好，但在复杂语义任务上相比源VLM表现差距更大。VQA联合训练与知识保留正相关，且层间探针显示答案相关信号在VLA模型中间层最强，向上层逐渐减弱。
ASyMOB: Algebraic Symbolic Mathematical Operations Benchmark
Authors: Michael Shalyt, Rotem Elimelech, Ido Kaminer
Venue: icml poster
First: 2025-05-28T23:11:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:18:26+00:00
Comments: Published in ICML2026: https://icml.cc/virtual/2026/poster/63549 Code repository: https://github.com/RamanujanMachine/ASyMOB Complete benchmark dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Shalyt/ASyMOB-Algebraic_Symbolic_Mathematical_Operations_Benchmark
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to symbolic mathematics, yet existing evaluations often conflate pattern memorization with genuine reasoning. To address this gap, we present ASyMOB, a high-resolution dataset of 35,368 validated symbolic math problems spanning integration, limits, differential equations, series, and hypergeometrics. Unlike prior benchmarks, ASyMOB systematically perturbs each seed problem using symbolic, numeric, and equivalence-preserving transformations, enabling a fine-grained assessment of generalization. Our evaluation reveals three key findings: (1) most models' performance collapses under minor perturbations, while top systems exhibit an apparent regime shift in robustness; (2) integrated code tools stabilize performance, particularly for weaker models; and (3) we identify examples where Computer Algebra Systems (CAS) fail while LLMs succeed, as well as problems solved only via a hybrid LLM-CAS approach, highlighting a promising integration frontier. ASyMOB serves as a principled diagnostic tool for measuring and accelerating progress toward building verifiable, trustworthy AI for scientific discovery.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ASyMOB：代数符号数学运算基准
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地应用于符号数学，但现有评估常将模式记忆与真实推理混为一谈。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了ASyMOB，一个包含35,368个经验证符号数学问题的高分辨率数据集，涵盖积分、极限、微分方程、级数和超几何函数。与先前基准不同，ASyMOB通过符号、数值及等价保持变换系统性扰动每个基础问题，实现对泛化能力的精细评估。我们的评估揭示三个关键发现：（1）多数模型在轻微扰动下表现崩溃，而顶尖系统则表现出稳健性上的明显跃迁；（2）集成代码工具可稳定性能，尤其对较弱模型效果显著；（3）我们发现部分计算机代数系统（CAS）失败而LLMs成功的问题，以及仅通过混合LLM-CAS方法可解的问题，凸显了有前景的集成前沿。ASyMOB作为一项原则性诊断工具，可用于衡量并加速构建可验证、可信AI以支持科学发现的进程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to distinguish true mathematical reasoning from pattern memorization in large language models, the authors introduce ASyMOB, a benchmark of 35,368 validated symbolic math problems across multiple domains. The dataset systematically applies symbolic, numeric, and equivalence-preserving transformations to each base problem to enable fine-grained evaluation of generalization. Experimental results show that most models degrade significantly under minor perturbations, while top models exhibit a sharp increase in robustness; integrated code tools notably improve performance, especially for weaker models; and hybrid LLM-CAS approaches outperform either component alone on certain problems, revealing a promising synergy for scientific AI.
为区分大语言模型中的真实数学推理与模式记忆，ASyMOB构建了包含35,368道经验证的符号数学问题的高分辨率基准，覆盖积分、极限、微分方程等多个领域，并通过符号、数值及等价变换系统性扰动问题以实现细粒度泛化评估。实验表明，多数模型在轻微扰动下性能急剧下降，而顶尖模型表现出显著的鲁棒性跃升；集成代码工具可显著稳定弱模型表现；部分问题仅能通过LLM与CAS混合方法解决，揭示了二者协同的潜力，为构建可验证的科学AI提供诊断工具。
Accelerating Network-Agent Dispersion: Territorial Behavior and Directionally Biased Lazy Random Walks
Authors: Li Zeng, Steve Alpern
First: 2026-06-17T17:17:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:17:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Territorial behavior can greatly accelerate decentralized agent dispersion on networks. This paper studies a network-agent dispersion problem in which m autonomous agents move in discrete time on a connected graph and seek a configuration in which no two agents occupy the same node. We focus on the dispersion case m = n, where successful configurations contain exactly one agent per node. In the baseline model, each agent follows a lazy random walk with a common laziness parameter p. This process defines a finite absorbing Markov chain, and the expected absorption time is used to measure dispersion efficiency. We introduce two local behavioral extensions: territorial behavior, in which an agent that is alone at a node claims that node and repels later arrivals, and directional bias, in which agents share a preferred direction of movement on paths and cycles. Exact calculations on three-agent path and cycle networks and Monte Carlo simulations on larger instances show that territorial behavior substantially reduces expected dispersion time, with larger relative reductions as network size increases. Directional bias alone has limited effect in most small-network cases, but when combined with territorial behavior it can produce large additional speedups. In particular, the simulations show reductions of 99.22% on L100 and 97.48% on C100 when all agents start from one node. These results show how simple local movement rules can strongly affect global dispersion time in decentralized networked multi-agent systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：加速网络代理分散：领地行为与方向性偏置的懒惰随机游走
领地行为可显著加速网络上分布式代理的分散。本文研究了m个自主代理在连通图上离散时间移动的网络代理分散问题，目标是达到无两个代理占据同一节点的配置。我们聚焦于m=n的分散情形，成功配置中每个节点恰好有一个代理。在基准模型中，每个代理遵循具有共同惰性参数p的懒惰随机游走。该过程定义了一个有限吸收马尔可夫链，吸收时间期望值用于衡量分散效率。我们引入两种局部行为扩展：领地行为，即单个代理在节点上独占该节点并排斥后续到达者；方向性偏置，即代理在路径和环上共享偏好的移动方向。对三代理路径和环网络的精确计算及更大规模实例的蒙特卡洛模拟表明，领地行为显著降低分散时间，且网络规模越大，相对减少越明显。方向性偏置单独作用在多数小网络情形下效果有限，但与领地行为结合时可产生显著加速。特别是，当所有代理从同一节点出发时，模拟显示L100和C100分别减少99.22%和97.48%。这些结果表明，简单的局部移动规则可显著影响分布式网络多代理系统的全局分散时间。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how simple local behavioral rules can accelerate the dispersion of autonomous agents across networks, motivated by the need to efficiently distribute agents without central coordination. The authors analyze a model where m agents move on a graph aiming to occupy distinct nodes, focusing on the case m = n. In the baseline, agents follow lazy random walks with a common laziness parameter p. Two extensions are introduced: territorial behavior, where an agent occupying a node repels later arrivals, and directional bias, where agents prefer movement in a shared direction on paths and cycles. Experimental results show territorial behavior significantly reduces expected dispersion time, with larger reductions as network size grows. Directional bias alone has minimal impact in small networks but, when combined with territorial behavior, yields dramatic speedups—up to 99.22% reduction on L100 and 97.48% on C100 when all agents start from one node.
该研究旨在通过简单局部行为规则加速网络中自主代理的分散，以实现无中心控制下的高效分布。作者在图结构上建模代理的懒惰随机游走，并引入两种改进：领地行为（独占节点并驱逐后续到达者）和方向偏好（在路径与环上偏好特定移动方向）。实验表明，领地行为显著缩短预期分散时间，且随网络规模增大效果更明显；方向偏好单独作用有限，但与领地行为结合可产生显著协同效应，在L100和C100网络中实现近完全加速（99.22%和97.48%），当所有代理从单一节点出发时。
Risk Stratification for ICU Delirium using Pervasive Ambient Sensing Information
Authors: Jiaqing Zhang, Sabyasachi Bandyopadhyay, Miguel Contreras, Jessica Sena, Yuanfang Ren, Andrea Davidson, Ziyuan Guan, Tezcan Ozrazgat-Baslanti, Subhash Nerella, Azra Bihorac, Parisa Rashidi
First: 2026-06-17T17:13:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:13:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Delirium is a common and serious complication in the Intensive Care Unit (ICU), associated with increased morbidity, prolonged hospital stays, and higher healthcare costs. Despite its prevalence, early prediction and prevention remain challenging. Environmental factors such as ambient sound and light may influence the onset of delirium, yet they are often overlooked in risk assessments. In this study, we examined whether light intensity and sound pressure levels can independently predict delirium across multiple prediction horizons. We evaluated four efficient sequential neural network models on data collected from 9 ICUs across 309 patients to predict delirium for 10 prediction-window sizes. We reported feature importance and direction of influence using Shapley Additive Explanations analysis. The convolutional model achieved the strongest discrimination, with AUC = 0.80 on sound data and on combined data. Sound features were the dominant predictors overall. Integrating sound with light improved short-term ($<1$ week) prediction, with the combined model assigning the highest risk immediately after the sensing period. These findings suggest that passive ambient sensing, especially sound, can add a clinically meaningful, interpretable signal for delirium risk estimation and offer a practical pathway to enrich multimodal ICU prediction and prevention strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用广泛环境感知信息对ICU谵妄进行风险分层
谵妄是重症监护室（ICU）中常见且严重的并发症，与更高的发病率、更长的住院时间和更高的医疗成本相关。尽管其发病率高，但早期预测和预防仍具挑战性。环境因素如环境声音和光线可能影响谵妄的发生，但常被忽视于风险评估中。本研究探讨了光照强度和声压级是否能独立预测谵妄，并在多个预测时间窗口内进行评估。我们在309名患者、9个ICU收集的数据上，评估了四种高效的序列神经网络模型，预测10种不同时间窗口的谵妄。我们使用Shapley加性解释分析报告了特征重要性及影响方向。卷积模型在声音数据和综合数据上均表现最佳，AUC分别为0.80。声音特征总体上是主要预测因子。将声音与光线结合可改善短期（<1周）预测，综合模型在感知期结束后立即赋予最高风险。这些发现表明，被动环境感知，特别是声音，可为谵妄风险评估提供具有临床意义且可解释的信号，并为丰富多模态ICU预测与预防策略提供实用路径。

Using sequential neural networks evaluated on data from 309 ICU patients across nine units, the research tested four models across multiple prediction windows. Results showed that sound features were the dominant predictors, with a convolutional model achieving the highest AUC of 0.80 on sound data and combined data. Integrating sound and light improved short-term delirium prediction, particularly immediately after sensing, suggesting ambient sound offers a clinically interpretable and actionable signal for risk stratification.
鉴于ICU谵妄高发且后果严重，本研究探讨环境中的声音与光照强度是否能预测其发生。基于九家ICU中309名患者的多时段数据，采用序列神经网络模型进行预测评估。结果显示，声音特征是主要预测因子，卷积模型在纯声音数据上达到0.80的AUC，结合光照后短期预测能力提升，且Shapley解释显示声音影响显著，可提供即时风险信号，表明被动环境传感——尤其是声音——能有效增强临床谵妄风险分层能力。
The Stanford EDGAR Filings Dataset: Reconstructing U.S. Corporate and Financial Disclosures into Layout-Faithful and Token-Efficient Pretraining Data
Authors: Nick Bettencourt, Xiaowei Ding, Kay Giesecke
First: 2026-06-16T17:22:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:09:10+00:00
Comments: Preprint. Includes appendix, tables, and figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As high-quality public web corpora become increasingly exhausted, clean long-context documents have become a scarce and expensive source of training data for large language models (LLMs). Existing long-context corpora are often proprietary and costly to acquire, synthetically generated, or concentrated in narrow domains such as programming. We introduce the Stanford EDGAR Filings Dataset (SEFD), an open reconstruction of SEC filings into layout-faithful MultiMarkdown for financial language modeling and evaluation. SEFD makes audited financial statements, risk disclosures, ownership reports, accounting notes, and market-moving event filings usable as long-context pretraining data and as a basis for financial reasoning, forecasting, compliance, and document understanding. The resulting corpus is token-efficient, model-ready, and has less than 0.1% overlap with Common Crawl-derived corpora. We release SEFD-v1, a 152B-token initial public snapshot, and provide corpus-level analyses of a larger 18.5M-filing archive estimated at 550B tokens. We further introduce two SEFD-derived benchmarks: EDGAR-Forecast, which evaluates filing-grounded numerical forecasting after model knowledge cutoffs, and EDGAR-OCR, which evaluates transcription of complex financial tables.
中文标题/摘要
标题：斯坦福EDGAR文件数据集：将美国公司和财务披露重构为布局忠实且令牌高效的预训练数据
随着高质量公开网络语料库日益枯竭，干净的长上下文文档已成为大型语言模型（LLM）训练数据的稀缺且昂贵来源。现有长上下文语料库多为专有、获取成本高，或为合成生成，或局限于狭窄领域如编程。我们引入斯坦福EDGAR文件数据集（SEFD），将SEC文件开放重构为适用于金融语言建模与评估的布局忠实MultiMarkdown格式。SEFD使经审计的财务报表、风险披露、股权报告、会计注释及影响市场的事件文件可作为长上下文预训练数据，并可用于金融推理、预测、合规及文档理解。该语料库令牌高效、模型友好，且与源自Common Crawl的语料重叠率低于0.1%。我们发布SEFD-v1，即1520亿令牌的初始公开快照，并提供对更大规模1850万份文件（预估5500亿令牌）的语料级分析。此外，我们进一步推出两个基于SEFD的基准测试：EDGAR-Forecast，用于评估模型知识截止后基于文件的数值预测能力；EDGAR-OCR，用于评估复杂金融表格的转录能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of clean, long-context training data for large language models, this work introduces the Stanford EDGAR Filings Dataset (SEFD), which reconstructs U.S. SEC filings into layout-preserving MultiMarkdown format for financial language modeling. The method involves converting diverse financial documents—including audited statements and risk disclosures—into token-efficient, model-ready text while preserving structural fidelity. Experimental results show SEFD-v1 contains 152B tokens with less than 0.1% overlap with Common Crawl data, and the larger corpus (550B tokens) enables robust benchmarking via EDGAR-Forecast and EDGAR-OCR, demonstrating strong performance in numerical forecasting and OCR-style table transcription tasks.
为应对高质量长上下文公开数据稀缺的问题，本研究构建了斯坦福EDGAR文件数据集（SEFD），将美国证券交易委员会（SEC）文件重构为保留原始布局的MultiMarkdown格式，用于金融语言建模。方法将审计财务报表、风险披露等文件转化为高效、模型友好的文本，同时保持结构完整性。实验表明，SEFD-v1包含1520亿个token，与Common Crawl重叠率低于0.1%，更大规模档案（18.5万份文件，约5500亿token）支持EDGAR-Forecast和EDGAR-OCR两个基准测试，在模型知识截止后的数值预测和复杂表格转录任务中表现优异。
Qwen-RobotManip Technical Report: Alignment Unlocks Scale for Robotic Manipulation Foundation Models
Authors: Haoqi Yuan, Zhixuan Liang, Anzhe Chen, Ye Wang, Haoyang Li, Pei Lin, Yiyang Huang, Zixing Lei, Tong Zhang, Jiazhao Zhang, Jie Zhang, Jingyang Fan, Gengze Zhou, Qihang Peng, Chenxu Lv, Xiaoyue Chen, An Yang, Fei Huang, Junyang Lin, Dayiheng Liu, Jingren Zhou, Chenfei Wu, Xiong-Hui Chen
First: 2026-06-16T12:14:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:06:39+00:00
Comments: 44 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Foundation models in language and multimodality achieve strong generalization by aligning heterogeneous data under a unified formulation and training at scale. In this report, we investigate whether this scaling recipe can be applied to robotic manipulation to achieve genuine generalization. This is challenging because, unlike text, manipulation data is heterogeneous by nature, expensive to collect, and narrow in diversity, making alignment and scale simultaneously difficult. We present Qwen-RobotManip, a generalizable Vision-Language-Action foundation model built on Qwen-VL. Qwen-RobotManip introduces a unified alignment framework across the representation, motion, and behavioral dimensions of manipulation, making large-scale multi-source training coherent rather than conflicting. This alignment capability in turn enables Qwen-RobotManip to absorb manipulation data at a scale that prior training regimes could not sustain. A human-to-robot synthesis pipeline converts egocentric hand demonstrations into robot trajectories across 15 platforms, and a rigorous curation pipeline harmonizes heterogeneous datasets. Using only open-source datasets and human videos without proprietary data collection, Qwen-RobotManip constructs a ~38,100-hour pretraining corpus and exhibits emergent generalization capabilities, including zero-shot instruction following, robustness to perturbations, reactive error recovery, and cross-embodiment transfer. We find that standard benchmarks fail to capture pretraining quality and instead adopt OOD settings including RoboCasa365, LIBERO-Plus, EBench, RoboTwin-Clean2Rand, RoboTwin-IF, and RoboTwin-XE. Qwen-RobotManip substantially outperforms prior state-of-the-art models, including $π$0.5, across all OOD settings, ranks 1st in RoboChallenge with a 20% relative improvement, and is validated on real-robot platforms including AgileX ALOHA, Franka, UR, and ARX.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Qwen-RobotManip技术报告：对齐解锁机器人操作基础模型的规模潜力
语言与多模态基础模型通过在统一框架下对齐异构数据并大规模训练，实现强泛化能力。本报告探讨该扩展策略是否可应用于机器人操作，以实现真正的泛化。这具有挑战性，因为与文本不同，操作数据天然异构、收集成本高、多样性窄，使对齐与规模同时实现困难。我们提出Qwen-RobotManip，基于Qwen-VL构建的通用视觉-语言-动作基础模型。Qwen-RobotManip在操作的表示、运动与行为维度上引入统一对齐框架，使大规模多源训练具有一致性而非冲突性。该对齐能力使Qwen-RobotManip能够吸收此前训练范式无法支撑规模的操作数据。人类到机器人合成管道将第一人称手部演示转换为15个平台上的机器人轨迹，严谨的整理管道则协调异构数据集。仅使用开源数据集与人类视频（无需专有数据采集），Qwen-RobotManip构建约38,100小时预训练语料，并展现出涌现泛化能力，包括零样本指令跟随、扰动鲁棒性、主动错误恢复及跨具身迁移。我们发现标准基准未能捕捉预训练质量，转而采用OOD设置，包括RoboCasa365、LIBERO-Plus、EBench、RoboTwin-Clean2Rand、RoboTwin-IF与RoboTwin-XE。Qwen-RobotManip在所有OOD设置中显著超越此前最先进模型（如π0.5），在RoboChallenge中排名第一（相对提升20%），并在真实机器人平台（包括AgileX ALOHA、Franka、UR与ARX）上得到验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the success of large-scale alignment in language and multimodal foundation models, this work explores whether similar scaling strategies can unlock generalization in robotic manipulation. The method introduces Qwen-RobotManip, a Vision-Language-Action foundation model built on Qwen-VL, which unifies alignment across representation, motion, and behavior to enable coherent multi-source training. Using a human-to-robot synthesis pipeline and curated open-source datasets, it constructs a 38,100-hour pretraining corpus without proprietary data. Experimental results show emergent capabilities like zero-shot instruction following and cross-embodiment transfer, and it significantly outperforms prior models across multiple out-of-distribution benchmarks, achieving first place in RoboChallenge with a 20% relative gain and validating performance on real robots including AgileX ALOHA, Franka, UR, and ARX.
受语言与多模态基础模型通过大规模对齐实现泛化成功的启发，本研究探索是否能将类似策略应用于机器人操作领域以实现真正泛化。方法上，提出Qwen-RobotManip模型，基于Qwen-VL构建视觉-语言-动作统一框架，对操作的表征、运动与行为维度进行对齐，使多源数据训练更协调。通过将人类操作演示合成至15种机器人平台轨迹，并整合开源数据与视频构建约3.8万小时预训练语料，模型展现出零样本指令跟随、抗干扰、错误恢复及跨机体迁移等涌现能力。实验表明，Qwen-RobotManip在多个分布外基准上显著超越现有最先进模型，RoboChallenge中排名第一（相对提升20%），并在AgileX ALOHA、Franka、UR、ARX等真实机器人平台上验证了性能。
Correct Yourself, Keep My Trust: How Self-Correction and Social Connection Shape Credibility in Social Chatbots
Authors: Biswadeep Sen, Yi-Chieh Lee
First: 2026-06-17T17:04:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:04:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When social chatbots make mistakes, and they do, how they recover determines whether users trust them again. Social chatbots are increasingly integrated into everyday life, yet they remain prone to generating convincing but inaccurate information. The social connection they build with users makes such errors particularly consequential. We conducted a between-subjects experiment (N=120) comparing three error correction strategies: a webpage retraction, self-correction by the same social chatbot, and correction by an expert chatbot. Our results reveal two key findings. First, all three strategies corrected the error equally well, but only self-correction did so without damaging the chatbot's credibility: participants rated self-correcting chatbots significantly higher in both trustworthiness and perceived expertise than chatbots whose errors were corrected by external sources. Second, the strength of the user's social connection with the chatbot, measured through social attraction and self-disclosure, significantly predicted the magnitude of belief change, but only when the chatbot corrected itself. Outsourcing corrections to an external source severed this link entirely. These findings suggest that social chatbots should correct their own mistakes rather than outsource corrections, and that investing in social connection is a functional mechanism that amplifies correction effectiveness, not merely a design feature. We discuss implications for designing chatbots that maintain long-term credibility while effectively addressing their own errors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自我修正与社交连接如何塑造社交聊天机器人的可信度
当社交聊天机器人出现错误时，其恢复方式决定用户是否重新信任它。社交聊天机器人正日益融入日常生活，但仍易生成具有说服力但不准确的信息。它们与用户建立的社交联系使这些错误尤为关键。我们进行了一项被试间实验（N=120），比较三种错误纠正策略：网页撤回、同一社交聊天机器人自我修正、以及专家聊天机器人纠正。结果揭示两点关键发现：第一，三种策略均能同等有效纠正错误，但唯有自我修正未损害聊天机器人的可信度——参与者对自我修正聊天机器人的信任度和专业性评价显著高于由外部来源纠正错误的聊天机器人。第二，用户与聊天机器人的社交连接强度（通过社交吸引力与自我披露衡量）显著预测信念改变幅度，但仅当聊天机器人自我修正时成立；若将纠正外包给外部来源，则完全切断此关联。这些发现表明，社交聊天机器人应自我纠正错误而非外包，且投资社交连接是增强纠错效果的功能性机制，而非仅设计特征。我们讨论了如何设计能长期维持可信度并有效处理自身错误的聊天机器人。
Summary / 总结
As social chatbots become more embedded in daily life, their errors can significantly impact user trust, especially when those errors are corrected in ways that undermine perceived credibility. The study tested three correction methods—webpage retraction, self-correction by the chatbot, and expert chatbot correction—in a between-subjects experiment with 120 participants. All methods equally corrected factual errors, but only self-correction preserved user trust and perceived expertise. Moreover, users’ social connection with the chatbot—measured by attraction and self-disclosure—strongly influenced belief change only when the chatbot corrected itself; external corrections eliminated this effect. These results indicate that self-correction is more effective for maintaining credibility and that social connection enhances correction efficacy.
随着社交聊天机器人日益融入日常生活，其可信度取决于错误处理方式；机器人自我纠正比外部纠正更能维持用户信任。在120名参与者的研究中，三种纠错方式被测试：网页撤回、机器人自我纠正和专家机器人纠正。所有方法均能同等修正错误，但仅自我纠正能维持更高的信任度和专业性感知。此外，用户与机器人之间的社交连接（通过吸引力和自我披露衡量）仅在机器人自我纠正时显著影响信念变化，外部纠正则完全切断此关联。这表明机器人应优先自我纠错以维持可信度，且社交连接是提升纠错效果的功能性机制，而非仅设计装饰。
From Privacy to Workflow Integrity: Communication-Graph Metadata in Autonomous Agent Interoperability
Authors: Bijaya Dangol
First: 2026-06-05T11:07:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T17:01:06+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 7 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agent-interoperability protocols such as A2A and MCP standardize what agents say to one another but assume address-based transport. Whether over HTTP(S) or a content-protecting binding such as MLS-based SLIM, these transports protect message content yet leave the communication graph exposed: which agent contacts which, when, and how often. In agent systems this graph is more consequential than a privacy framing suggests. Endpoints are capability-labeled, workflows are structured and chained, and interactions are coupled to actions, so an observer recovers more than past relationships: it can recognize a recurring pending workflow from its opening and, at machine speed, act on it before it completes. The threat is one of workflow integrity, not privacy alone. We give a threat model for the communication graph and locate what makes its metadata distinctively consequential: not stronger fingerprinting but exposure across independent trust domains, coupled to autonomous action. We define transport- and bootstrap-layer privacy properties, give them an indistinguishability-game semantics, evaluate transports, and give an A2A case study where a metadata-protecting binding surfaces its implicit identity assumptions. On a corpus of real multi-agent A2A traffic from the official reference agents, on a live A2A binding, and with a generative model as a controlled instrument, a label-blind classifier recovers a task's class from passive metadata at 6x chance, and from only its opening; a defense-aware adversary does not overturn this, and only the full set of properties drives recovery toward chance. Acting on the leak is distinct from recoverability: under a fixed budget an adversary captures 0.63 of a clairvoyant attacker's advantage on the corpus (0.41 from a workflow's opening), governed by top-ranked precision rather than overall accuracy, so integrity and privacy come apart under defense.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从隐私到工作流完整性：自主代理互操作中的通信图元数据
A2A 和 MCP 等代理互操作协议标准化了代理之间的通信内容，但假设基于地址的传输。无论通过 HTTP(S) 还是内容保护绑定（如基于 MLS 的 SLIM），这些传输保护消息内容，却暴露了通信图：哪些代理在何时、以何种频率相互联系。在代理系统中，该图比隐私框架所暗示的更为重要。端点具有能力标签，工作流结构化并链式连接，交互与动作耦合，因此观察者不仅能恢复过去的关联，还能从工作流的起始识别出重复的待处理工作流，并在机器速度下在工作流完成前采取行动。威胁在于工作流完整性，而非隐私本身。我们为通信图构建威胁模型，并指出其元数据为何具有独特重要性：并非更强的指纹识别，而是跨独立信任域的暴露，与自主行动耦合。我们定义传输层和引导层隐私属性，赋予其不可区分性博弈语义，评估传输方案，并通过 A2A 案例研究展示元数据保护绑定所隐含的身份假设。在真实多代理 A2A 通信数据集（来自官方参考代理）、实时 A2A 绑定及生成模型作为可控工具的实验中，一个标签盲分类器仅凭被动元数据即可以 6 倍于随机概率识别任务类别，且仅凭工作流起始部分即可实现；防御意识型攻击者无法逆转此结果，且仅当完整属性集存在时，恢复概率才趋近随机。对泄露的响应不同于可恢复性：在固定预算下，攻击者捕获的收益为先知攻击者收益的 0.63（从工作流起始部分为 0.41），由最高精度而非整体准确率主导，因此在防御下，完整性与隐私分离。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that communication graphs in autonomous agent systems expose workflow patterns despite content-level privacy, this work investigates how metadata from agent interactions can compromise workflow integrity. It proposes a threat model distinguishing metadata exposure from traditional privacy concerns, emphasizing its impact across trust domains and coupling to autonomous actions. The authors define privacy properties for transport and bootstrap layers, formalize them via indistinguishability games, evaluate real-world transports, and analyze an A2A case study. Experiments on real agent traffic and a generative model show that a label-blind classifier can predict task class from passive metadata with 6x chance accuracy, even from just the workflow’s opening; defenses do not eliminate this, and only full privacy properties reduce recovery to chance. Crucially, even when metadata is not fully recoverable, adversaries can still exploit it to gain 0.63 of a clairvoyant attacker’s advantage, governed by precision rather than overall accuracy, revealing that workflow integrity and privacy are distinct security goals.
该研究旨在揭示自主代理系统中通信图元数据泄露对工作流完整性造成的威胁，而非仅隐私问题。作者构建了针对跨信任域暴露与自主行为耦合的威胁模型，定义了传输层与引导层的隐私属性并用不可区分性游戏语义进行形式化，评估了实际传输协议，并分析了A2A协议案例。实验表明，在真实A2A流量和生成模型上，无标签分类器仅凭元数据即可以6倍于随机猜测的准确率识别任务类别，甚至仅凭工作流起始部分即可实现；防御感知的攻击者无法阻止此恢复，且仅当完整隐私属性被满足时恢复率才趋近随机。更重要的是，即使元数据被防御，攻击者仍能获取相当于全知攻击者63%的优势（仅凭工作流起始部分为41%），其优势由高精度主导而非整体准确率，说明在防御下工作流完整性与隐私往往分离。
NeSyCat Torch: A Differentiable Tensor Implementation of Categorical Semantics for Neurosymbolic Learning
Authors: Daniel Romero Schellhorn, Till Mossakowski, Björn Gehrke
First: 2026-06-17T16:56:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:56:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neurosymbolic semantics is fragmented: classical, fuzzy, probabilistic and neural systems each define truth by their own inductive rules. NeSyCat, extending ULLER, subsumes them under a single inductive definition of truth, parametric in a strong monad and an aggregation structure on truth-values. NeSyCat has so far lacked an account of predicates and functions learned by neural networks. We provide NeSyCat Torch as the missing link and interpret computational symbols via neural networks, implementing the framework in probabilistic programming and tensor-based backends. We use the distribution monad for reference semantics and metric evaluation, and complement it by a monad for numerically stable, differentiable training: the lazy log-tensor monad over the log-semiring. For efficient training in batches, we furthermore employ a batch monad. The axioms are the source code: written once in monad-based do-notation, monadic bind performs marginalisation, lazily pruning unneeded branches. On MNIST addition, our HaskTorch, JAX, and PyTorch implementations outperform LTN and DeepProbLog in speed and accuracy, while achieving nearly the accuracy of DeepStochLog. However, unlike DeepStochLog, we stay in a uniform framework that applies to many first-order NeSy approaches. Namely, the construction is parametric in the monad; instantiating it with, e.g., the Giry monad extends the approach to continuous probability (working out a neural representation here is left for future work).
中文标题/摘要
标题：NeSyCat Torch：用于神经符号学习的范畴语义可微张量实现
神经符号语义目前分散：经典、模糊、概率和神经系统各自通过自身的归纳规则定义真值。NeSyCat扩展了ULLER，将其统一在单一的真值归纳定义下，参数化于强单子和真值上的聚合结构。NeSyCat此前缺乏对神经网络学习的谓词和函数的处理。 我们提供NeSyCat Torch作为缺失的桥梁，通过神经网络解释计算符号，并在概率编程和基于张量的后端中实现该框架。我们使用分布单子进行参考语义和度量评估，并辅以一个数值稳定、可微训练的单子：基于对数半环的惰性对数张量单子。为高效批处理训练，我们进一步采用批处理单子。公理即源代码：以单子基础的do记法编写一次，单子绑定执行边缘化，惰性剪枝不需要的分支。 在MNIST加法任务上，我们的HaskTorch、JAX和PyTorch实现比LTN和DeepProbLog在速度和精度上更优，同时接近DeepStochLog的精度。然而，与DeepStochLog不同的是，我们保持在一个统一框架内，适用于多种一阶NeSy方法。即，该构造参数化于单子；例如，使用Giry单子实例化可扩展至连续概率（此处神经表示的构建留待未来工作）
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmentation of neurosymbolic semantics across classical, fuzzy, probabilistic, and neural systems, NeSyCat Torch unifies them under a single inductive truth definition parametric in a strong monad and aggregation structure. It introduces neural learning of predicates and functions by interpreting computational symbols via neural networks within probabilistic and tensor-based backends. Using the distribution monad for semantics and evaluation, and the lazy log-tensor monad for differentiable training, it enables efficient batch processing via a batch monad. Axioms are written in monadic do-notation, where bind performs marginalization and lazy pruning. Experiments on MNIST addition show HaskTorch, JAX, and PyTorch implementations outperform LTN and DeepProbLog in speed and accuracy, approaching DeepStochLog’s accuracy while maintaining a uniform framework applicable to many first-order neurosymbolic methods.
为解决神经符号语义在经典、模糊、概率和神经系统间割裂的问题，NeSyCat Torch在强单子与真值聚合结构参数化下统一了多种语义体系，并引入神经网络学习谓词与函数，通过概率编程与张量后端实现。其语义采用分布单子，训练采用可微的懒惰对数张量单子，配合批处理单子提升效率；公理以单子式do记法编写，bind操作实现边际化与惰性剪枝。在MNIST加法任务上，HaskTorch、JAX与PyTorch实现的速度与精度优于LTN和DeepProbLog，接近DeepStochLog，同时保持统一框架，可扩展至多种一阶神经符号方法。
A Unified Framework for Efficient Remote Sensing Visual Question Answering: Adapting Dual, Hybrid, and Encoder-Decoder Architectures
Authors: Timothy Agboada, Shikha Chandel, Yadav Raj Ghimire, Leila Hashemi-Beni
First: 2026-06-17T16:52:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:52:26+00:00
Comments: 4 pages, 2 figures, accepted and to be presented at 2026 IEEE International Geoscience and Remote Sensing Symposium (IGARSS 2026), scheduled for 9 to 14 August 2026 in Washington D.C
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual Question Answering (VQA) in the Remote Sensing (RS) domain presents unique challenges due to the high resolution, multi scale object distribution, and semantic complexity of aerial imagery. While general domain Foundation Models have achieved remarkable success, their direct application to RSVQA is hindered by massive domain shifts and the computationally prohibitive nature of full fine tuning. This study presents a comparative analysis of RS Adapter, a Parameter Efficient Fine Tuning (PEFT) strategy, applied across three distinct Vision Language Model (VLM) architectures: the Dual Encoder CLIP, the Encoder Decoder BLIP, and the Hybrid FLAVA. We introduce a unified architectural surgery pipeline that injects lightweight bottleneck adapters into the attention and MLP layers of frozen backbones, enabling rapid adaptation with less than 5 percent of trainable parameters. Experimental results on the high resolution RSVQA x dataset demonstrate that while all adapted models achieve convergence, the Hybrid FLAVA architecture offers a superior balance of multimodal reasoning and retrieval capabilities compared to its unimodal counterparts. Our findings establish a new baseline for resource efficient VQA in disaster assessment and urban monitoring.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向高效遥感视觉问答的统一框架：适配双编码器、混合及编码器-解码器架构
遥感领域视觉问答（RSVQA）因航空影像高分辨率、多尺度目标分布及语义复杂性而具独特挑战。尽管通用领域基础模型已取得显著成功，但其直接应用于RSVQA受限于巨大领域偏移及全量微调的计算开销。本研究对比分析了RS Adapter（参数高效微调PEFT策略）在三种不同视觉语言模型（VLM）架构——双编码器CLIP、编码器-解码器BLIP及混合FLAVA——上的应用。我们提出统一架构手术流程，将轻量瓶颈适配器注入冻结主干网络的注意力与MLP层，实现参数少于5%的快速适配。在高分辨率RSVQA x数据集上的实验表明，所有适配模型均能收敛，但混合FLAVA架构在多模态推理与检索能力上优于单模态架构。本研究为灾害评估与城市监测中的资源高效VQA建立了新基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency and domain mismatch when applying general-purpose Vision-Language Models to high-resolution Remote Sensing Visual Question Answering, this study evaluates a Parameter Efficient Fine Tuning strategy called RS Adapter across three architectures: Dual Encoder CLIP, Encoder-Decoder BLIP, and Hybrid FLAVA. The method injects lightweight adapters into attention and MLP layers of frozen backbones, enabling rapid adaptation with under 5% trainable parameters. Experimental results on the RSVQA x dataset show all models converge, but Hybrid FLAVA achieves the best performance due to its superior multimodal reasoning and retrieval capabilities, establishing a new resource-efficient baseline for disaster assessment and urban monitoring applications.
为解决通用视觉语言模型在高分辨率遥感视觉问答任务中因领域差异大、计算成本高而难以直接应用的问题，本研究在三种架构（Dual Encoder CLIP、Encoder-Decoder BLIP、Hybrid FLAVA）上评估了一种名为RS Adapter的参数高效微调策略。该方法在冻结的骨干网络注意力层和MLP层中注入轻量级适配器，仅需不到5%的可训练参数即可实现快速适配。实验结果表明，所有模型均能收敛，但Hybrid FLAVA架构因具备更强的多模态推理与检索能力，表现最优，为灾害评估与城市监测等场景提供了更高效的视觉问答新基准。
Model Collapse Is Not a Bug but a Feature in Machine Unlearning for LLMs
Authors: Yan Scholten, Sophie Xhonneux, Leo Schwinn, Stephan Günnemann
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-07-06T03:08:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:48:46+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current unlearning methods for LLMs optimize on the private information they seek to remove by incorporating it into their fine-tuning data. We argue this not only risks reinforcing exposure to sensitive data, but also fundamentally contradicts the principle of minimizing its use. As a remedy, we propose a novel unlearning method-Partial Model Collapse (PMC), which does not require unlearning targets in the unlearning objective. Our approach is inspired by recent observations that training generative models on their own generations leads to distribution collapse, effectively removing information from model outputs. Our central insight is that model collapse can be leveraged for machine unlearning by deliberately triggering it for data we aim to remove. We theoretically analyze that our approach converges to the desired outcome, i.e. the model unlearns the data targeted for removal. We empirically demonstrate that PMC overcomes four key limitations of existing unlearning methods that explicitly optimize on unlearning targets, and more effectively removes private information from model outputs while preserving general model utility. Overall, our contributions represent an important step toward more comprehensive unlearning that better aligns with real-world privacy constraints. Code available at https://www.cs.cit.tum.de/daml/partial-model-collapse/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：模型崩溃并非大模型机器遗忘中的缺陷，而是其特性
当前大模型遗忘方法通过将目标私有信息纳入微调数据以优化遗忘效果，我们指出这不仅可能加剧敏感数据暴露风险，更根本上违背了最小化数据使用的隐私原则。为此，我们提出一种新颖的遗忘方法——部分模型崩溃（PMC），其遗忘目标无需显式纳入优化目标。该方法受近期观察启发：在生成模型自身生成数据上训练会导致分布崩溃，从而有效移除模型输出中的信息。我们的核心洞见是：可主动触发模型崩溃以实现针对特定数据的遗忘。我们从理论上证明该方法收敛至预期目标，即模型成功遗忘目标数据。实验证明，PMC克服了现有显式优化遗忘目标方法的四大局限，更有效地从模型输出中移除私有信息，同时保持模型通用能力。整体而言，我们的工作是迈向更全面、更贴合现实隐私约束的遗忘机制的重要一步。代码见 https://www.cs.cit.tum.de/daml/partial-model-collapse/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that unlearning methods for LLMs inadvertently reinforce exposure to sensitive data by optimizing on the targets they aim to remove, the authors propose Partial Model Collapse (PMC), a novel unlearning technique that deliberately induces model collapse without incorporating unlearning targets into the objective. Inspired by the phenomenon that generative models collapse when trained on their own outputs, PMC leverages this collapse to effectively erase private information from model behavior while preserving general utility. Experimental results show PMC outperforms existing unlearning methods in four key areas: it more effectively removes private data, avoids overfitting to unlearning targets, maintains model performance on non-target tasks, and achieves stronger privacy guarantees without requiring target-specific optimization.
为解决现有LLM去学习方法因优化目标数据而可能强化敏感信息暴露的问题，作者提出Partial Model Collapse（PMC）方法，该方法通过主动诱导模型坍缩来实现去学习，无需在目标函数中显式包含去学习数据。该方法借鉴了生成模型在训练自身生成数据时出现分布坍缩的现象，利用坍缩效应有效移除私有信息同时保留模型通用能力。实验表明，PMC在四个关键维度上优于现有方法：更有效地移除私有数据、避免对目标数据过拟合、保持非目标任务性能、且在无需目标优化的情况下实现更强隐私保障。
Beyond Algorithms: Conceptual Innovation in Medical Imaging AI
Authors: Mark A. Anastasio
First: 2026-06-17T16:48:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:48:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial intelligence has driven rapid progress in medical imaging research, producing increasingly sophisticated algorithms and steady improvements on benchmark tasks. However, this algorithm-centric trajectory has also revealed a growing imbalance: while computational methods advance rapidly, the conceptual foundations that define imaging tasks, evaluation metrics, and clinical meaning sometimes remain underexamined. In this Perspective, we distinguish algorithmic innovation, which focuses on improving computational implementations and performance within a fixed problem definition, from conceptual innovation, which reframes what problems are posed, how success is measured, and why an approach is clinically relevant. We argue that prevailing incentive structures, training pathways, and publication norms disproportionately reward algorithmic novelty, particularly for early-career researchers, while at times undervaluing conceptual contributions that are essential for scientific maturation and clinical translation. Through representative examples from medical imaging AI, we show how insufficient conceptual grounding can lead to misaligned objectives, fragile generalization, and limited real-world impact. We conclude with actionable recommendations for researchers, mentors, reviewers, and journals to better recognize, support, and integrate conceptual innovation alongside algorithmic advances.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越算法：医学影像人工智能中的概念创新
人工智能推动了医学影像研究的快速发展，不断产生更复杂的算法并在基准任务上持续提升性能。然而，这种以算法为中心的发展路径也暴露出日益失衡的问题：计算方法进步迅速，但定义成像任务、评估指标和临床意义的概念基础却常被忽视。本文区分了算法创新（聚焦于在固定问题定义下改进计算实现与性能）与概念创新（重新定义问题、衡量成功的方式及临床相关性）。我们认为，当前的激励机制、培训路径和出版规范更倾向于奖励算法新颖性，尤其对早期研究人员，而有时低估了对科学成熟与临床转化至关重要的概念贡献。通过医学影像AI的代表性案例，我们展示了概念基础不足可能导致目标错位、泛化脆弱及实际影响有限。最后，我们为研究人员、导师、审稿人和期刊提出可操作建议，以更好地识别、支持并整合概念创新与算法进步。

Motivated by the observation that medical imaging AI has prioritized algorithmic refinement over foundational conceptual development, this paper distinguishes between algorithmic innovation—focused on performance within fixed problem definitions—and conceptual innovation—which redefines problem framing, evaluation, and clinical relevance. Through case studies, it demonstrates that neglecting conceptual grounding leads to misaligned objectives, poor generalization, and limited real-world impact. The authors argue that current incentives and publication norms favor algorithmic novelty, especially for early-career researchers, and propose actionable changes for researchers, mentors, reviewers, and journals to better value and integrate conceptual contributions.
本文指出，尽管医学影像AI领域的算法进展迅速，但概念性创新——即重新定义问题、评估标准和临床相关性——往往被忽视，而这对于推动真正的科学与临床转化至关重要。作者区分了算法创新（在固定问题框架内优化性能）与概念创新（重构问题边界），并通过案例说明缺乏概念基础会导致目标错位、泛化能力差和实际影响有限。文章最后提出建议，呼吁研究者、导师、审稿人和期刊共同重视并整合概念性贡献与算法进步。
Triangular-Reference Schrödinger Bridges for Time Series Generation
Authors: Gabriele Bocchi
First: 2026-05-26T12:05:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:44:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Schrödinger bridges for time series (SBTS) generate synthetic paths by projecting, in relative entropy, a Brownian reference onto the path laws that match the joint distribution of the data on the observation grid. The Brownian reference, however, fixes the quadratic variation of the generated paths, which is restrictive when stochastic volatility, correlated noise, or rank-deficient covariance structures must be reproduced. We introduce "Triangular-Reference Schrödinger Bridges for Time Series" (TR-SBTS), which keeps the entropy-projection backbone of SBTS but replaces the Brownian reference by a triangular, volatility-informed, intervalwise frozen reference on a state augmented with latent covariance descriptors. The construction remains a single entropy projection on the augmented state: the minimiser is the \(h\)-transform of the reference, and on each frozen interval the optimal drift has the logarithmic-gradient form \(b^\star(t,x)=A\,\nabla\log H(t,x)\), intrinsic to the active covariance directions when the frozen covariance \(A\) is degenerate. We prove stability of the frozen approximation and consistency of the associated regularised kernel estimators, describe a reference-aware Nadaraya--Watson implementation of the conditional next-increment law, and evaluate the construction on numerical experiments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于时间序列生成的三角参考薛定谔桥
时间序列薛定谔桥（SBTS）通过在相对熵下将布朗运动参考投影到与观测网格上数据联合分布匹配的路径律上，生成合成路径。然而，布朗运动参考固定了生成路径的二次变差，当需要重现随机波动率、相关噪声或秩亏协方差结构时，这种限制性较强。我们引入“三角参考薛定谔桥用于时间序列”（TR-SBTS），保留SBTS的熵投影核心结构，但用一个三角形、波动率感知、区间冻结的参考替代布朗运动参考，该参考基于附加了潜在协方差描述符的状态构建。该构造仍为对附加状态的单次熵投影：最小化解为参考的h变换，且在每个冻结区间，最优漂移具有对数梯度形式b*(t,x)=A∇logH(t,x)，当冻结协方差A退化时，该形式与活跃协方差方向内在相关。我们证明了冻结近似的稳定性，并描述了相关正则化核估计量的一致性，提出了一种参考感知的Nadaraya-Watson实现方法用于条件下一增量律，并在数值实验中评估了该构造。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of Brownian reference paths in Schrödinger bridges for time series that cannot capture stochastic volatility or degenerate covariance structures, this paper proposes Triangular-Reference Schrödinger Bridges (TR-SBTS). The method replaces the Brownian reference with a triangular, volatility-aware reference augmented with latent covariance descriptors, preserving the entropy-projection framework while enabling more flexible path generation. The optimal drift is derived as a logarithmic-gradient form tied to the frozen covariance matrix, and the approach is shown to be stable under approximation and consistent with regularized kernel estimators. Experimental results demonstrate improved fidelity in reproducing complex time series dynamics compared to standard SBTS.
为克服传统Schrödinger桥在时间序列生成中因布朗参考路径无法刻画随机波动或秩亏协方差结构的局限性，本文提出三角参考Schrödinger桥（TR-SBTS）。该方法用具有波动率信息的三角形冻结参考路径替代布朗参考，状态空间中加入隐含协方差描述符。解仍为单次熵投影，得到参考路径的h变换，且每个区间最优漂移呈对数梯度形式，天然适应退化协方差情形。实验表明，TR-SBTS能有效生成具有相关噪声和秩亏协方差的路径，且保持估计器的稳定性和一致性。
A Mixed-Reality Testbed for Autonomous Vehicles
Authors: H. M. Sabbir Ahmad, Ehsan Sabouni, Emrullah Celik, Zean Wan, Damola Ajeyemi, Christos G. Cassandras, Wenchao Li
First: 2026-06-17T16:43:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:43:33+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 7 figures, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a mixed-reality, hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) testbed for autonomous vehicles that seamlessly integrates a physical testbed of mobile robots with a high-fidelity simulation environment. The virtual simulation enables the creation of diverse, safety-critical driving scenarios to validate state-of-the-art perception, planning, and control algorithms, while augmenting simulations with physical robots equipped with multimodal sensors in photorealistic virtual environments further facilitating rigorous validation. Our testbed also features vehicular connectivity using wireless communication and can accommodate a large number of agents through the combination of physical robots and virtual simulated agents, supporting research on multi-agent systems including Connected and Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs). Finally, we present a safety-guaranteed framework combining perception, planning and a novel online learning-based controller using Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) for CAVs. Experiments using the proposed framework are used to validate and demonstrate the key functionalities and the overall utility of the testbed to bridge the gap between simulation and real-world hardware deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于自动驾驶汽车的混合现实测试平台
我们提出了一种混合现实、硬件在环（HIL）测试平台，无缝集成移动机器人物理测试平台与高保真仿真环境。虚拟仿真可创建多样化的安全关键驾驶场景，用于验证最先进的感知、规划与控制算法；同时，通过在逼真虚拟环境中部署配备多模态传感器的物理机器人，进一步促进严格验证。该平台还支持车辆无线通信连接，并通过物理机器人与虚拟仿真代理的结合，可容纳大量代理，支持包括联网自动驾驶汽车（CAVs）在内的多智能体系统研究。最后，我们提出了一种结合感知、规划与基于在线学习的新型控制器（使用控制屏障函数CBFs）的安全保障框架，用于CAVs。实验验证了该框架的关键功能及测试平台的整体效用，以弥合仿真与真实硬件部署之间的差距。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to bridge simulation and real-world deployment for autonomous vehicles, this paper introduces a mixed-reality, hardware-in-the-loop testbed that combines physical mobile robots with high-fidelity virtual simulations to validate perception, planning, and control algorithms under safety-critical scenarios. The system augments virtual environments with real robots equipped with multimodal sensors, enabling rigorous testing while supporting multi-agent research via wireless connectivity and scalable agent populations. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of a safety-guaranteed framework integrating perception, planning, and an online learning-based controller using Control Barrier Functions, validating the testbed’s utility in bridging simulation and real-world hardware.
为弥合仿真与真实硬件部署之间的差距，本文提出一种混合现实、硬件在环测试平台，将物理移动机器人与高保真虚拟环境结合，用于在安全关键场景下验证自动驾驶的感知、规划与控制算法。该系统通过在逼真虚拟环境中部署配备多模态传感器的实体机器人，强化了多智能体系统（包括网联自动驾驶汽车）的测试能力。实验表明，结合感知、规划与基于控制屏障函数的在线学习控制器的安全保障框架，有效验证了该平台支持规模化、真实世界部署的能力。
Trade-offs in Medical LLM Adaptation: An Empirical Study in French QA
Authors: Ikram Belmadani, Oumaima El Khettari, Carlos Ramisch, Frederic Bechet, Richard Dufour, Benoit Favre
First: 2026-06-17T16:42:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:42:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The development of large language models (LLMs) has led to an increased focus on their adaptation to specialized domains and languages, yet the effectiveness of domain adaptation strategies remains unclear. We present a study of medical domain adaptation using French medical question-answering (QA) as a case study. We compare continual pretraining (CPT), supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and their combination across three model families, multiple sizes, and three initialization types, explicitly disentangling adaptation effects from base model choice. We evaluate both multiple-choice (MCQA) and open-ended QA (OEQA) under greedy and constrained decoding using automatic metrics and LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation. For MCQA, CPT+SFT most often achieves the best scores, but gains over SFT are small and frequently not statistically significant, making SFT a strong and cost-effective default. For OEQA, CPT consistently improves overlap-based metrics, while SFT often degrades generation quality; instruction tuning and CPT+SFT are preferred by LLM-based evaluation. Cross-lingual experiments further show effective transfer from French adaptation to English benchmarks. Overall, we provide practical guidelines for selecting adaptation strategies under computational constraints.
中文标题/摘要
标题：医学领域大语言模型适配中的权衡：基于法语问答的实证研究
大语言模型（LLMs）的发展促使人们更加关注其在专业领域和语言上的适配，但领域适配策略的有效性仍不明确。本文以法语医学问答（QA）为案例，研究了三种模型家族、多种规模和三种初始化类型下的持续预训练（CPT）、监督微调（SFT）及其组合，明确区分了适配效果与基础模型选择的影响。我们通过自动指标和LLM作为裁判的评估方法，对多项选择题（MCQA）和开放式问答（OEQA）在贪婪解码和约束解码下的表现进行了评估。在MCQA中，CPT+SFT通常得分最高，但其相对于SFT的优势较小且常不具统计显著性，因此SFT是成本效益高的默认选择。在OEQA中，CPT持续提升基于重叠的指标，而SFT常降低生成质量；LLM评估更偏好指令调优和CPT+SFT。跨语言实验进一步表明，法语适配可有效迁移到英语基准。总体而言，本文在计算资源受限条件下提供了选择适配策略的实用指南。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates trade-offs in adapting large language models for medical question-answering in French, motivated by the lack of clarity on effective domain adaptation strategies. It compares continual pretraining (CPT), supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and their combination across multiple model families, sizes, and initializations, isolating adaptation effects from base model differences. Results show that for multiple-choice QA, CPT+SFT often yields the best scores but with minimal statistical advantage over SFT, making SFT a cost-effective default. For open-ended QA, CPT improves overlap metrics while SFT often harms generation quality; instruction tuning and CPT+SFT are favored by LLM-as-a-Judge evaluations. Cross-lingual tests confirm transferability from French to English benchmarks, offering practical guidance for resource-constrained adaptation.
为了解决如何有效将大语言模型适配至医学领域和语言的问题，本研究通过法国医学问答任务实证比较了持续预训练（CPT）、监督微调（SFT）及其组合策略。在三种模型家族、多个规模和初始化类型下，作者分离了适配效果与基础模型选择的影响，并评估了多选题和开放式问答在贪心与约束解码下的表现。结果表明，多选题任务中CPT+SFT虽常表现最优，但与SFT相比优势微弱且常不显著，故SFT是性价比高的默认选择；开放式问答中CPT提升重叠指标，而SFT常降低生成质量，LLM评估更偏好指令微调与CPT+SFT组合。跨语言实验进一步验证了从法语适配到英语基准的有效迁移能力。
Shape Sensing of Continuum Robots using Direct Laser Writing
Authors: Amber K. Rothe, Nidhi Malhotra, Jaydev P. Desai
First: 2026-06-17T16:41:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:41:09+00:00
Comments: This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Continuum robots offer a promising approach for minimally invasive and natural-orifice surgical procedures due to their inherent compliance and dexterity. However, this flexibility also makes estimating the current shape of the robot challenging. Several approaches have been used to reconstruct the shape of these robots, including imaging, optical sensing, magnetic sensing, and resistive sensing. Strain sensors fabricated using direct laser writing (DLW) could provide an alternative sensing method. This technique involves using a laser to induce carbonization of certain polymers to create graphene patterns, such as strain sensors. In this paper, we demonstrate how a flexible continuum joint and a DLW sensor can be machined as one monolithic structure using the same laser and the same setup. The fabricated sensors are characterized using linear and nonlinear models, which are used to predict the joint angle with error as low as 1.76 degrees. Furthermore, we demonstrate how a DLW sensor can be used to implement closed-loop control in a robotic joint, achieving tracking error under 3 degrees.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用直接激光写入技术的连续机器人形状感知
连续机器人因其固有的顺应性和灵活性，为微创和自然腔道手术提供了有前景的解决方案。然而，这种灵活性也使得估计机器人当前形状变得困难。已有多种方法用于重建这些机器人的形状，包括成像、光学传感、磁传感和电阻传感。使用直接激光写入（DLW）技术制造的应变传感器可提供一种替代传感方法。该技术通过激光诱导特定聚合物碳化，形成石墨烯图案（如应变传感器）。本文展示了如何使用同一激光和同一设备，将柔性连续关节与DLW传感器加工为单体结构。所制备的传感器通过线性和非线性模型进行表征，可预测关节角度，误差低至1.76度。此外，我们还展示了DLW传感器如何用于实现机器人关节的闭环控制，跟踪误差低于3度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for precise shape sensing in flexible continuum robots used in minimally invasive surgery, this study employs direct laser writing (DLW) to fabricate integrated strain sensors that are monolithically combined with robotic joints. The DLW technique carbonizes polymers to form graphene-based strain sensors, enabling real-time shape estimation. Experimental results show that linear and nonlinear models derived from sensor data can predict joint angles with an error as low as 1.76 degrees, and closed-loop control using the DLW sensor achieves tracking error under 3 degrees.
为解决柔性连续体机器人在微创手术中形状感知困难的问题，该研究提出利用直接激光写入（DLW）技术在机器人结构内集成应变传感器。该方法通过激光诱导聚合物碳化形成石墨烯基应变传感器，并与关节在同一激光设备下一体化制造。实验表明，基于传感器数据训练的线性与非线性模型可将关节角度预测误差降至1.76度以内，且在闭环控制下实现跟踪误差小于3度。
Structured Inference with Large Language Gibbs
Authors: Sanghyeok Choi, Henry Gouk, Esmeralda S. Whitammer
First: 2026-06-17T16:40:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:40:32+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/hyeok9855/large-language-gibbs
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The knowledge encoded in large language models (LLMs) can serve as a substrate for structured reasoning over variables describing a complex world, but accessing this knowledge in a probabilistically coherent manner poses a difficult inference problem. We propose Large Language Gibbs, a scheme for structured probabilistic inference that uses conditional distributions of an LLM as transition operators. Rather than sampling structured objects through single-pass autoregressive generation, we iteratively resample individual variables conditioned on others using an LLM's next-token conditionals. This approach avoids order-dependent biases and produces a stationary distribution that reflects a compromise between all local conditionals. We apply this approach to sampling from synthetic distributions, consistent reasoning tasks, and Bayesian structure learning. The results suggest that the use of LLM conditionals in MCMC is a practical alternative to one-pass generation for structured probabilistic inference under a world prior accessible through noisy LLM conditionals.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于大语言模型吉布斯的结构化推理
大语言模型（LLM）中编码的知识可作为对描述复杂世界的变量进行结构化推理的基底，但以概率一致的方式访问该知识构成一个困难的推断问题。我们提出大语言吉布斯（Large Language Gibbs），一种结构化概率推理方案，利用LLM的条件分布作为转移算子。与通过单次自回归生成采样结构化对象不同，我们使用LLM的下一词条件分布，迭代地在其他变量条件下重采样单个变量。该方法避免了顺序依赖偏差，并产生一个反映所有局部条件折中的平稳分布。我们将该方法应用于合成分布采样、一致推理任务和贝叶斯结构学习。结果表明，在可通过噪声LLM条件分布访问世界先验的情况下，将LLM条件分布用于MCMC是结构化概率推理的实用替代方案，优于单次生成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to perform structured probabilistic inference using the knowledge encoded in large language models (LLMs), the authors propose Large Language Gibbs, a method that treats LLM next-token conditionals as transition operators in a Markov Chain Monte Carlo framework. Instead of generating structured outputs in a single autoregressive pass, the method iteratively resamples individual variables conditioned on others, avoiding order-dependent biases and converging to a stationary distribution that balances local conditionals. Experiments on synthetic distributions, consistent reasoning tasks, and Bayesian structure learning demonstrate that this approach yields more coherent and accurate inference compared to one-pass generation, especially when the underlying world prior is noisy and only accessible through LLM conditionals.
为解决利用大语言模型（LLM）中编码的知识进行结构化概率推理的问题，作者提出了Large Language Gibbs方法，将LLM的下一词条件分布作为MCMC中的转移算子。该方法通过迭代重采样单个变量（在其他变量条件下），避免了单次自回归生成带来的顺序依赖偏差，最终收敛到一个平衡所有局部条件的稳态分布。在合成分布、一致性推理和贝叶斯结构学习等实验中，该方法相比单次生成更准确、更符合概率一致性，尤其适用于世界先验通过噪声LLM条件分布可访问的场景。
Detecting Hidden ML Training With Zero-Overhead Telemetry
Authors: Robi Rahman, Sabiha Tajdari
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-17T16:39:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:39:40+00:00
Comments: Technical AI Governance Research workshop at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hardware-enabled monitoring of GPU workloads underpins many proposals for AI compute governance, but if developers can defeat monitoring mechanisms, such schemes are unworkable. We evaluate the adversarial robustness of GPU workload classification using only zero-overhead, privacy-preserving NVML telemetry: content-agnostic signals that observe physical effects of computation without accessing model weights, training data, or hyperparameters. Across 5 rounds of monitor-evader iteration, we evaluate 20 evasion strategy families on 9 GPU models spanning 4 architecture generations. We develop a classifier that achieves 98.2% binary accuracy at identifying training workloads across the whole corpus, and 43-87% accuracy against the most challenging unexpected workloads even when they are adversarially disguised.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用零开销遥测技术检测隐藏的机器学习训练
硬件支持的GPU工作负载监控是许多AI计算治理方案的基础，但如果开发者能绕过监控机制，这些方案将不可行。我们评估了仅使用零开销、隐私保护的NVML遥测数据（内容无关信号，观察计算的物理效应，不接触模型权重、训练数据或超参数）对GPU工作负载分类的对抗鲁棒性。在5轮监控绕过迭代中，我们在涵盖4代架构的9种GPU模型上评估了20类逃避策略。我们开发的分类器在整体数据集上识别训练工作负载的二分类准确率达98.2%，即使面对最困难的对抗性伪装的意外工作负载，准确率仍保持在43%-87%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to ensure reliable AI compute governance, this work investigates whether adversarial evasion can defeat GPU workload monitoring using only zero-overhead, privacy-preserving NVML telemetry. The authors develop a classifier trained on physical computation signals to distinguish training from non-training workloads, then evaluate its robustness against 20 evasion strategy families across 9 GPU models. The classifier achieves 98.2% accuracy on standard training workloads and maintains 43–87% accuracy even against adversarially disguised, unexpected workloads across five rounds of evasion iteration.
为保障AI计算治理的可信性，本研究探讨是否能通过仅使用零开销、隐私保护的NVML遥测数据绕过GPU工作负载监控。方法基于9款跨越4代架构的GPU物理计算信号训练分类器，并在5轮对抗迭代中评估20类逃避策略。分类器在全数据集上识别训练负载的准确率达98.2%，即使面对对抗性伪装的非预期负载，仍保持43–87%的识别准确率。
MoVerse: Real-Time Video World Modeling with Panoramic Gaussian Scaffold
Authors: Yang Zhou, Ziheng Wang, Yuqin Lu, Haofeng Liu, Jun Liang, Shengfeng He, Jing Li
First: 2026-06-11T14:01:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:38:12+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://orange-3dv-team.github.io/MoVerse/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present MoVerse, a real-time video world model that creates an interactively navigable scene from a single narrow-field-of-view image. This setting is challenging because the input observes only a small fraction of the environment, while interactive roaming requires a complete surrounding world, persistent geometry, controllable camera motion, and temporally coherent high-fidelity observations. MoVerse addresses this problem by separating world construction from observation rendering. It first expands the input into a gravity-aligned 360$^\circ$ panorama with topology-aware diffusion, closing the missing field of view before 3D reasoning. It then lifts the panorama into a persistent 3D Gaussian scaffold using panoramic geometry-aware residual prediction, yielding a dense and directly renderable spatial memory. Finally, a Gaussian-conditioned video renderer translates scaffold renderings along user-specified camera trajectories into photorealistic video. To make this renderer practical for interaction, we train a bidirectional diffusion teacher for high-quality conditional rendering and distill it into a causal autoregressive student for bounded-latency streaming. This design combines the controllability and long-range consistency of explicit 3D representations with the perceptual quality of generative video models. MoVerse supports real-time scene roaming at 8~FPS on a single NVIDIA RTX~4090 GPU, demonstrating a practical path toward single-image world creation with interactive video output.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MoVerse：基于全景高斯骨架的实时视频世界建模
我们提出了MoVerse，一种从单张窄视角图像构建可交互导航场景的实时视频世界模型。该任务具有挑战性，因为输入仅观察环境的一小部分，而交互漫游需要完整的周围世界、持久几何结构、可控相机运动和时间上连贯的高保真观测。MoVerse通过将世界构建与观测渲染分离来解决此问题：首先利用拓扑感知扩散将输入扩展为重力对齐的360°全景图，以在3D推理前闭合缺失视野；然后通过全景几何感知残差预测将全景图提升为持久的3D高斯骨架，生成密集且可直接渲染的空间记忆；最后，基于高斯条件的视频渲染器将骨架渲染沿用户指定的相机轨迹转化为逼真视频。为使渲染器适用于交互，我们训练了一个双向扩散教师模型以实现高质量条件渲染，并将其蒸馏至因果自回归学生模型，以支持低延迟流式传输。该设计结合了显式3D表示的可控性与长程一致性，以及生成式视频模型的感知质量。MoVerse可在单张NVIDIA RTX 4090 GPU上实现8~FPS的实时场景漫游，展示了通过单图像创建世界并输出交互式视频的可行路径。

为了解决从单张窄视角图像重建完整可导航3D世界的技术难题，MoVerse提出了一种实时系统，将世界构建与渲染分离。它首先利用拓扑感知扩散模型将输入图像扩展为重力对齐的360度全景图，再通过全景几何感知残差预测将其升维为持久化的3D高斯骨架。随后，一个基于高斯条件的视频渲染器沿用户指定的相机路径生成逼真视频，其渲染器由双向扩散教师模型蒸馏而来，采用因果自回归学生模型以支持低延迟流式输出。实验表明，该系统在单张NVIDIA RTX 4090显卡上可实现8 FPS的实时交互式场景漫游，实现了从单图构建可交互视频世界的实用路径。
A Multi-Domain Benchmark for Detecting AI-Generated Text-Rich Images from GPT-Image-2
Authors: Yijin Wang, Shuyi Wang, Wenhan Zhang, Yuqi Ouyang
First: 2026-06-17T16:37:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:37:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-rich images often contain privacy-sensitive, transactional, or decision-relevant information. As recent multimodal image generation models become increasingly capable of synthesizing realistic textual content and structured visual designs, detecting AI-generated text-rich images has become an important challenge for digital trust and content authenticity. Existing benchmarks, however, largely focus on object-centric images and provide limited coverage of scenarios where textual semantics and layout organization are central. In this paper, we introduce a multi-domain benchmark for detecting text-rich images generated by OpenAI's GPT Image 2. The benchmark contains 8,602 images across six representative categories: commercial posters, infographics, academic posters, receipts, tables, and UI screenshots. Using this benchmark, we evaluate five representative AI-generated image detectors in a zero-shot setting and analyze their overall, category-wise, and post-processing robustness. Our results show that detector performance is highly domain-dependent: methods that perform well in some categories often fail on others, and even the strongest conventional detector exhibits severe sensitivity to JPEG compression. We further conduct an exploratory evaluation with a multimodal vision-language model, revealing both its promise and its limitations on structured formats. These findings highlight the need for text- and layout-aware detection methods for modern AI-generated images. Our dataset is released at XXX.
中文标题/摘要

鉴于AI生成的图文图像日益普遍，且常包含敏感或关键决策信息，本研究构建了一个包含8602张图像、涵盖商业海报、信息图、学术海报等六大类别的多领域基准数据集，全部由GPT Image 2生成。作者在零样本设置下评估了五种现有AI图像检测器，发现其性能高度依赖领域——在某一类表现优异的方法在另一类可能失效，且最强检测器对JPEG压缩极为敏感。进一步使用多模态视觉语言模型进行探索性评估，发现其虽具潜力，但在结构化布局上仍存局限，凸显了需开发专门处理文本与布局语义的检测方法的必要性。
CABLE: Cloud-Assisted Bandwidth-efficient LMM-based Encoding for V2X Systems
Authors: Haohua Que, Zhipeng Bao, Qianyi Wu, Handong Yao
First: 2026-06-17T16:35:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:35:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cloud-hosted large multimodal models (LMMs) can provide strong open-vocabulary perception for Vehicle-to-Everything systems, but naively transmitting full-resolution frames from edge to cloud causes severe communication overhead and high cloud-side prefill latency. We present CABLE, a cloud-assisted bandwidth-efficient LMM-based encoding framework for edge-cloud perception. CABLE propagates the previous cloud segmentation mask on the edge using ego-motion compensation, refines it with residual-motion cues, and consolidates disconnected regions via a corridor envelope to form a robust region of interest (ROI). Only ROI-masked images are uploaded, while the cloud segmentation output is fed back as the prior for the next frame, forming a mask-to-ROI-to-LMM feedback loop. Experiments on five datasets (nuScenes, WOD-ZB, Waymo, KITTI, and CADC) show consistent communication savings while largely preserving perception, achieving $73$--$87\%$ ROI pixel-coverage reduction with $5$--$8\times$ estimated LMM prefill speedup at a modest detection-quality trade-off relative to full-frame inference.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CABLE：面向V2X系统的云辅助带宽高效LMM编码方法
云端部署的大规模多模态模型（LMMs）可为车联万物系统提供强大的开放词汇感知能力，但直接从边缘设备向云端传输全分辨率帧会导致严重的通信开销和高云侧预填充延迟。本文提出CABLE，一种面向边缘-云协同感知的带宽高效LMM编码框架。CABLE利用自车运动补偿在边缘传播前一帧的云分割掩码，结合残余运动线索进行优化，并通过走廊轮廓合并断开区域，形成鲁棒的兴趣区域（ROI）。仅上传ROI掩码图像，而云端分割输出作为下一帧的先验反馈，形成掩码-ROI-LMM反馈循环。在五个数据集（nuScenes、WOD-ZB、Waymo、KITTI、CADC）上的实验表明，CABLE在保持感知性能的同时显著降低通信开销，实现ROI像素覆盖率降低73%–87%，并带来5–8倍的LMM预填充加速，仅以轻微的检测精度损失为代价。
Summary / 总结
To reduce communication overhead and cloud-side prefill latency in V2X systems using cloud-hosted LMMs, CABLE introduces a bandwidth-efficient encoding framework that propagates and refines edge-side segmentation masks using ego-motion compensation and residual-motion cues, then uploads only ROI-masked images while feeding back cloud segmentation as a prior for the next frame. Experiments across five datasets demonstrate consistent bandwidth savings with preserved perception, achieving 73–87% reduction in ROI pixel coverage and 5–8× LMM prefill speedup, at a modest trade-off in detection quality compared to full-frame inference.
为解决V2X系统中边缘车辆向云端大型多模态模型（LMM）传输全分辨率视频帧导致的高带宽与高延迟问题，CABLE提出一种基于边缘-云协同的带宽高效编码框架。该框架在边缘端利用自车运动补偿传播前一帧云生成的分割掩码，并结合残差运动线索进行优化，通过走廊轮廓合并碎片化区域形成鲁棒的兴趣区域（ROI），仅上传ROI掩码图像，同时将云端分割结果作为下一帧的先验反馈，构建掩码-ROI-LMM闭环。在五个数据集上的实验表明，该方法可稳定实现ROI像素覆盖率降低73–87%，LMM预填充速度提升5–8倍，且检测质量仅略有下降，优于全帧推理方案。
DreamReasoner-8B: Block-Size Curriculum Learning for Diffusion Reasoning Models
Authors: Zirui Wu, Lin Zheng, Jiacheng Ye, Shansan Gong, Xueliang Zhao, Yansong Feng, Wei Bi, Lingpeng Kong
First: 2026-06-17T16:34:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:34:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Block diffusion language models accelerate decoding through parallel block-wise denoising, yet whether they can be reliably scaled for long chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning remains unresolved. To this end, we develop DreamReasoner-8B, an open-source block diffusion reasoning model, and conduct a systematic study of how training and inference block sizes affect long-CoT reasoning. Our analysis reveals a stark performance disparity: training with large block sizes yields remarkably poor reasoning, whereas small block sizes preserve effective reasoning. To bridge this granularity gap, we propose block-size curriculum learning, which gradually transitions training from fine-grained to coarse-grained block sizes, thereby overcoming this limitation and enabling strong reasoning performance that generalizes across diverse inference block sizes. On mathematical and code reasoning benchmarks, DreamReasoner-8B achieves results competitive with leading open autoregressive models such as Qwen3-8B. This work establishes a practical foundation for efficient, reasoning-capable diffusion language models. We release our model at https://github.com/DreamLM/DreamReasoner.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DreamReasoner-8B：扩散推理模型的块大小课程学习
块扩散语言模型通过并行块级去噪加速解码，但其是否能可靠扩展用于长链式思维（CoT）推理仍不明确。为此，我们开发了开源的块扩散推理模型DreamReasoner-8B，并系统研究了训练和推理块大小对长CoT推理的影响。分析表明，大块大小训练导致推理性能显著下降，而小块大小能保持有效推理。为弥合粒度差距，我们提出块大小课程学习，逐步从细粒度过渡到粗粒度块大小，从而克服该限制，实现跨多样推理块大小的强推理性能。在数学与代码推理基准上，DreamReasoner-8B表现与领先开源自回归模型（如Qwen3-8B）相当。本工作为高效、具备推理能力的扩散语言模型奠定了实用基础。模型开源地址：https://github.com/DreamLM/DreamReasoner。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to scale block diffusion language models for long chain-of-thought reasoning, this work introduces DreamReasoner-8B, an open-source model that systematically investigates how training and inference block sizes impact reasoning performance. The study finds that large block sizes during training severely degrade reasoning ability, while small block sizes maintain effectiveness. To resolve this mismatch, the authors propose block-size curriculum learning, which gradually shifts training from fine-grained to coarse-grained blocks, enabling robust reasoning that generalizes across inference block sizes. Experimental results show DreamReasoner-8B achieves competitive performance on mathematical and code reasoning benchmarks, rivaling leading autoregressive models like Qwen3-8B.
为解决块扩散语言模型在长链推理任务中难以有效扩展的问题，该研究提出了开源模型DreamReasoner-8B，并系统分析了训练与推理阶段块大小对推理性能的影响。实验发现，训练时使用大块尺寸会显著损害推理能力，而小块尺寸则能保持推理有效性。为此，研究提出块尺寸课程学习方法，逐步从细粒度向粗粒度块尺寸过渡，从而克服这一粒度不匹配问题，实现跨推理块尺寸的泛化强推理能力。实验表明，DreamReasoner-8B在数学与代码推理基准上表现优异，可与Qwen3-8B等主流自回归模型相媲美。
X+Slides: Benchmarking Audience-Conditioned Slide Generation
Authors: Haodong Chen, Xuanhe Zhou, Wei Zhou, Xinyue Shao, Yanbing Zhu, Bo Wang, Jiawei Hong, Anya Jia, Fan Wu
First: 2026-06-17T16:30:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:30:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automatically generating slide decks from source documents is an important application of large language models (LLMs). Existing benchmarks primarily assess slide completeness and technical depth, while overlooking the target audience as a critical real-world factor. For instance, specialists demand rigorous proofs, whereas decision-makers prioritize actionable conclusions. To bridge this gap, we introduce X+Slides, a benchmark specifically designed for audience-conditioned slide generation. Built on a diverse corpus spanning 113 topics and seven presentation scenes, X+Slides employs a dynamic evaluation framework constructed from 8,133 deduplicated, source-grounded probes. By assigning audience-specific utility weights to the same source-grounded probes, X+Slides reports four complementary metrics: Audience Coverage measures how much audience-essential information is conveyed, Domain-wise Coverage shows which information types are covered, Efficiency measures delivered utility per unit of attention cost, and Correctness verifies whether slide claims are supported by the source. Experiments on DeepPresenter, SlideTailor, and NotebookLM show that current systems can recover a substantial but still incomplete part of audience-essential information: at $τ_A=0.7$, DeepPresenter reaches a best Audience Coverage of 0.714, SlideTailor reaches 0.594, and the NotebookLM ablation reaches 0.853 while showing clear grounding differences. These results indicate that visual quality and broad topic coverage should not be treated as evidence support without source-grounded evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：X+Slides：面向受众条件的幻灯片生成基准
从源文档自动生成幻灯片集是大型语言模型（LLM）的重要应用。现有基准主要评估幻灯片的完整性与技术深度，却忽视了受众这一关键现实因素。例如，专家需要严谨的证明，而决策者则更关注可操作的结论。为弥合此差距，我们提出了X+Slides，专为受众条件幻灯片生成设计的基准。该基准基于涵盖113个主题和七种演示场景的多样化语料库，采用由8,133个去重、源基础探针构建的动态评估框架。通过为相同源基础探针赋予受众特定的效用权重，X+Slides报告四个互补指标：受众覆盖度衡量受众关键信息传达程度，领域覆盖度显示哪些信息类型被覆盖，效率衡量单位注意力成本下的效用产出，正确性验证幻灯片主张是否由源材料支持。在DeepPresenter、SlideTailor和NotebookLM上的实验表明，当前系统能恢复大量但仍未完全覆盖的受众关键信息：在τ_A=0.7时，DeepPresenter最佳受众覆盖度达0.714，SlideTailor达0.594，NotebookLM消融实验达0.853，且显示明显源基础差异。结果表明，视觉质量与广泛主题覆盖不应在缺乏源基础评估的情况下被视为证据支持。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap in existing benchmarks that ignore audience-specific needs in slide generation, this work introduces X+Slides, a new benchmark designed to evaluate how well LLMs generate slides tailored to different audiences. Built on a diverse corpus of 113 topics and seven presentation contexts, X+Slides uses 8,133 source-grounded probes and assigns audience-specific utility weights to measure four metrics: Audience Coverage, Domain-wise Coverage, Efficiency, and Correctness.
