为弥补现有评测体系忽视受众需求的缺陷，本文提出X+Slides基准，专门用于评估大语言模型根据不同受众生成幻灯片的能力。该基准基于113个主题和七种演示场景的8133个去重、源文档对齐的探针，按受众特定权重评估四个指标：受众覆盖度、领域覆盖度、效率和正确性。实验表明，DeepPresenter、SlideTailor和NotebookLM在受众关键信息覆盖度上最高可达0.853（NotebookLM消融实验），但仍存在明显信息缺失，说明仅凭视觉质量或主题广度无法证明模型支持能力，必须依赖源文档对齐的评估。
SCAN: Enhance Time Series Anomaly Detection via Multi-Scale Neighborhood-Centered Clustering
Authors: Xingze Zheng, Hanyin Cheng, Siyuan Wang, Yiting Hao, Peng Chen, Yuan Jun, Yang Shu
First: 2026-06-17T16:30:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:30:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time series anomaly detection plays a crucial role in a wide range of real-world applications. Reconstruction-based methods have become the mainstream paradigm, but they suffer from over-generalization and under-generalization problems, which are challenging to balance. To address this, we introduce multi-scale clustering to enhance reconstruction-based methods. At the representation level, we integrate the cluster center representations of normal patterns to constrain the model to target representative normal patterns for reconstruction, preventing dominance of powerful capacity and representation capability. At the anomaly criterion level, we derive anomaly confidence score based on cluster membership probability and combine it with reconstruction error, providing dual criteria for detection. Furthermore, the effectiveness of the cluster center representations and anomaly confidence score depends on the clustering performance. Accordingly, we extract neighborhood-centered representations for multi-view clustering to improve clustering performance. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets from diverse application domains demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of SCAN.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SCAN：通过多尺度邻域中心聚类增强时间序列异常检测
时间序列异常检测在众多现实应用中发挥着关键作用。基于重构的方法已成为主流范式，但其存在过泛化与欠泛化问题，难以平衡。为此，我们引入多尺度聚类以增强基于重构的方法。在表示层面，我们整合正常模式的聚类中心表示，约束模型聚焦于代表性正常模式进行重构，避免强大容量与表示能力的主导。在异常判定层面，我们基于聚类成员概率推导异常置信分数，并与重构误差结合，提供双重检测准则。此外，聚类中心表示与异常置信分数的有效性依赖于聚类性能。因此，我们提取邻域中心表示用于多视角聚类，以提升聚类效果。在多个来自不同应用领域的现实数据集上的广泛实验表明，SCAN达到当前最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of reconstruction-based anomaly detection methods that struggle with balancing over- and under-generalization, this paper proposes SCAN, which enhances detection via multi-scale neighborhood-centered clustering. The method integrates cluster center representations of normal patterns to guide reconstruction and derives an anomaly score combining cluster membership probability with reconstruction error, offering dual detection criteria. Experimental results across multiple real-world datasets show SCAN achieves state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of its clustering-driven approach in improving anomaly detection accuracy and robustness.
为解决重建式时间序列异常检测中的过拟合与欠拟合问题，SCAN引入多尺度邻域中心聚类以增强模型泛化能力。它通过整合正常模式的聚类中心表征引导模型重建代表性正常样本，同时结合聚类成员概率与重建误差构建双准则异常评分。通过提取邻域中心表示进行多视角聚类，SCAN提升了聚类质量，最终在多个真实世界数据集上达到最先进性能。
OneCanvas: 3D Scene Understanding via Panoramic Reprojection
Authors: Bartłomiej Baranowski, Dave Zhenyu Chen, Matthias Nießner
First: 2026-06-17T16:29:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:29:19+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://baranowskibrt.github.io/onecanvas/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Existing approaches to 3D scene understanding in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) either rely on complex, model-specific geometry encoders or large training budgets in pursuit of spatial reasoning. Instead, OneCanvas aggregates patch features from all views onto a single equirectangular panoramic canvas. Namely, each patch is unprojected to a 3D world coordinate using its depth and camera pose, then placed on the canvas at the continuous longitude and latitude of that point as seen from the canvas origin, with no rasterization or aggregation across overlapping views. A 3D position embedding of the patch's metric coordinates is added to its feature, restoring the depth lost when collapsing the world position to an angular canvas coordinate. Patches from all frames thus share one spatial coordinate system with no fusion or major architectural modifications of the backbone. The pretrained VLM consumes this representation as if it were an ordinary image. Because the canvas can be centered on any pose of interest, the same representation directly supports situated reasoning from a specific viewpoint, a common requirement in robotics and embodied AI. Thanks to this representation, we can also introduce a spatial pretraining curriculum: by procedurally placing patch features of objects, drawn from real images, at chosen 3D world positions on an otherwise empty canvas, we generate on-the-fly supervision spanning a broad range of spatial reasoning tasks, with answer distributions controlled to reduce spatial reasoning shortcuts. OneCanvas achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on SQA3D and VSI-Bench, and generalizes to out-of-distribution data on SPBench, using an order of magnitude less training compute than the strongest competing methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OneCanvas：通过全景重投影实现3D场景理解
现有视觉-语言模型（VLMs）中用于3D场景理解的方法，要么依赖复杂的、特定模型的几何编码器，要么需要大量训练资源以实现空间推理。相反，OneCanvas将所有视角的图像块特征聚合到一个单一的等距全景画布上。具体而言，每个图像块根据其深度和相机姿态反投影到3D世界坐标，然后以从画布原点观察该点的连续经度和纬度位置放置在画布上，无需光栅化或跨重叠视角的聚合。为恢复因将世界位置压缩为角坐标而丢失的深度信息，向图像块特征中添加其度量坐标的3D位置嵌入。因此，所有帧的图像块共享同一空间坐标系，无需融合或修改骨干网络架构。预训练的VLM将此表示视为普通图像进行处理。由于画布可任意中心化于感兴趣姿态，该表示直接支持从特定视角进行情境推理，这是机器人和具身AI的常见需求。借助此表示，我们还可引入空间预训练课程：通过程序化地将来自真实图像的物体图像块特征放置在空画布上选定的3D世界位置，我们可即时生成覆盖广泛空间推理任务的监督信号，并通过控制答案分布以减少空间推理捷径。OneCanvas在SQA3D和VSI-Bench上达到最先进精度，并在SPBench的分布外数据上实现泛化，且训练计算量仅为最强竞争方法的十分之一。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost and architectural complexity of existing 3D scene understanding methods in Vision-Language Models, OneCanvas proposes a novel approach that projects all camera views onto a single equirectangular panoramic canvas without rasterization or view fusion. Each patch is mapped to 3D world coordinates using depth and camera pose, then placed on the canvas at its angular position relative to the canvas origin, with a 3D position embedding added to preserve metric depth. This unified spatial representation allows pretrained VLMs to process 3D scenes as if they were images, enabling viewpoint-specific reasoning. The method further introduces a spatial pretraining curriculum by procedurally placing real object patches at arbitrary 3D positions on an empty canvas, generating diverse spatial reasoning supervision. Experiments show OneCanvas achieves state-of-the-art performance on SQA3D and VSI-Bench, and generalizes well to out-of-distribution data on SPBench, while using an order of magnitude less training compute than top competitors.
为降低现有视觉-语言模型在3D场景理解中的计算开销与架构复杂性，OneCanvas提出一种新方法，将所有视角的图像块投影到单个等距全景画布上，无需光栅化或视图融合。每个图像块根据深度和相机位姿映射到3D世界坐标，再按相对于画布原点的经纬度位置放置，并附加3D位置嵌入以恢复丢失的深度信息。该统一空间表示使预训练的视觉-语言模型可像处理普通图像一样处理3D场景，支持特定视角下的推理。方法还通过在空画布上程序化放置来自真实图像的物体块，生成多样化的空间监督信号，避免空间推理捷径。实验表明，OneCanvas在SQA3D和VSI-Bench上达到当前最优性能，在SPBench的分布外数据上泛化能力良好，且训练计算量仅为最先进方法的十分之一。
Acceleration of an algebraic multigrid pressure solver using graph neural networks
Authors: Eric Chillón, Artur K. Lidtke, Nguyen Anh Khoa Doan, Bernat Font
First: 2026-06-17T16:27:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:27:28+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 11 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Solving the pressure-Poisson equation remains the primary computational bottleneck in incompressible unstructured flow solvers primarily due to the inherent sensitivity of traditional linear solvers to mesh irregularities. This work introduces a data-driven algebraic multigrid (AMG) smoother that uses a modified graph convolutional isomorphism network (GCIN). The graph neural network predicts optimal polynomial coefficients to construct a sparse pseudo-inverse operator across diverse grid topologies. The coefficients are optimized to reduce the residual after each V-cycle iteration. By directly capturing the algebraic structure of the system from the sparse coefficient matrix, the proposed method maintains the solver's linearity while adapting to local anisotropies in unstructured grids. Our framework demonstrates significant performance gains by reducing the number of V-cycles required for a given tolerance and delivering wall-clock speedups from 4% to 37% across diverse benchmarks. Notably, the model exhibits robust generalization by maintaining efficiency on meshes up to 128 times larger than those seen in training, and by accelerating the solver's convergence on unseen industry-relevant problems such as the AirfRANS dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用图神经网络加速代数多重网格压力求解器
求解压力泊松方程仍是无结构不可压缩流求解器中的主要计算瓶颈，主要由于传统线性求解器对网格不规则性高度敏感。本文提出一种基于数据驱动的代数多重网格（AMG）平滑器，采用改进的图卷积同构网络（GCIN）。该图神经网络预测最优多项式系数，以构建适用于多种网格拓扑的稀疏伪逆算子。系数经优化以降低每次V循环迭代后的残差。该方法通过直接从稀疏系数矩阵中捕捉系统代数结构，在保持求解器线性的同时，适应无结构网格中的局部各向异性。我们的框架在降低达到给定容差所需V循环次数方面显著提升性能，并在多种基准测试中实现4%至37%的壁钟加速。值得注意的是，该模型展现出强大的泛化能力：在训练网格规模达128倍的更大网格上仍保持高效，并加速了在未见过的工业相关问题（如AirfRANS数据集）上的求解器收敛。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational bottleneck caused by mesh irregularities in incompressible unstructured flow solvers, this work proposes a data-driven algebraic multigrid smoother using a modified graph convolutional isomorphism network. The graph neural network predicts optimal polynomial coefficients to construct a sparse pseudo-inverse operator tailored to diverse grid topologies, reducing residual after each V-cycle iteration. Experimental results show the method reduces required V-cycles and delivers 4% to 37% wall-clock speedups across benchmarks, with robust generalization to meshes 128 times larger than training data and acceleration on unseen industry problems like the AirfRANS dataset.
为解决无结构流场求解器中因网格不规则导致的压力泊松方程计算瓶颈，本文提出一种基于图卷积同构网络的自适应代数多重网格平滑器。该图神经网络预测最优多项式系数，构建适用于不同网格拓扑的稀疏伪逆算子，以降低每轮V循环的残差。实验表明，该方法显著减少所需V循环次数，实现4%至37%的壁钟加速，且在训练网格规模128倍以上的未见网格上仍保持高效，成功加速如AirfRANS等工业问题的收敛。
Latent-Conditioned Parameterized Quantum Circuits as Universal Approximators for Distributions over Quantum States
Authors: Quoc Hoan Tran, Koki Chinzei, Yasuhiro Endo, Hirotaka Oshima
First: 2026-05-27T16:19:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:27:03+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 11 figures (fix the proof and update appendix for barren plateaus analysis)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many applications in quantum simulation, quantum chemistry, and quantum machine learning require not a single quantum state but an ensemble of states characterizing the heterogeneity of a target system. Preparing such ensembles state-by-state is prohibitive in both variational and fault-tolerant settings, thereby motivating a generative modeling approach. We introduce latent-conditioned parameterized quantum circuits (LPQCs), a hybrid quantum-classical framework in which classical neural networks map a latent variable sampled from a prior distribution to the parameters of a parameterized quantum circuit. We prove that LPQCs are universal approximators for probability measures over density operators in the 1-Wasserstein distance, extending classical universal approximation theorems to the quantum-distribution setting. We additionally introduce a multimodal latent prior and a mixture-of-experts circuit architecture, and show empirically that the latent-conditioned parameterization alleviates the barren plateau problem during optimization, a behavior for which we provide rigorous partial guarantees. Numerical experiments validate the framework on a synthetic multi-cluster ensemble of mixed quantum states and on a QM9-derived ensemble of 3-D molecular structures. In these tasks, LPQC outperforms recent quantum generative baselines and matches the generation quality of a classical neural-network baseline, while requiring an output dimension that grows only linearly with the number of qubits rather than exponentially. By leveraging classical expressivity in the latent space, LPQCs offer a tractable route to quantum generative modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于潜在条件的参数化量子电路作为量子态分布的通用逼近器
在量子模拟、量子化学和量子机器学习中，许多应用需要的不是单个量子态，而是表征目标系统异质性的量子态集合。逐个制备此类集合在变分和容错设置中均不可行，因此推动了生成建模方法的发展。我们引入了潜在条件参数化量子电路（LPQCs），这是一种混合量子-经典框架，其中经典神经网络将从先验分布中采样的潜在变量映射为参数化量子电路的参数。我们证明了LPQCs在1- Wasserstein距离下是密度算子概率测度的通用逼近器，将经典通用逼近定理扩展至量子分布场景。此外，我们引入了多模态潜在先验和专家混合电路架构，并通过实验证明潜在条件参数化在优化过程中缓解了 barren plateau 问题，对此我们提供了部分严格的理论保证。数值实验在合成的多簇混合量子态集合和基于QM9的3D分子结构集合上验证了该框架。在这些任务中，LPQCs优于近期的量子生成基线方法，与经典神经网络基线生成质量相当，同时其输出维度仅随量子比特数线性增长，而非指数增长。通过在潜在空间中利用经典表达能力，LPQCs为量子生成建模提供了一条可行路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate ensembles of quantum states for applications in quantum simulation and machine learning, this work introduces latent-conditioned parameterized quantum circuits (LPQCs), a hybrid quantum-classical framework where classical neural networks map latent variables to quantum circuit parameters. The authors prove LPQCs are universal approximators for quantum state distributions under the 1-Wasserstein distance, extending classical approximation theorems to the quantum domain. Empirical results show LPQCs effectively generate multimodal quantum ensembles, outperforming recent quantum generative models and matching classical baselines, while scaling linearly with qubit count—unlike exponential scaling in prior methods. The latent conditioning also mitigates the barren plateau problem, with partial theoretical guarantees provided.
为应对量子模拟与机器学习中生成量子态集合的需求，本文提出一种名为LPQCs的混合量子-经典框架，其中经典神经网络将采样自先验分布的潜在变量映射为量子电路参数。作者证明LPQCs在1- Wasserstein距离下可作为量子态分布的通用逼近器，将经典逼近理论扩展至量子域。实验表明，LPQCs能有效生成多模态量子态集合，优于近期量子生成模型，且在生成质量上可匹敌经典神经网络基线，同时其输出维度随量子比特数线性增长，而非指数增长。潜在变量的引入还缓解了优化中的 barren plateau 问题，并提供了部分理论保障。
Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-I: Spectral Geometry Observatory
Authors: Kaustubh Kapil, Kishor P. Upla
First: 2026-06-17T16:26:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:26:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite the widespread adoption of Vision Transformers (ViTs) and their success across numerous computer vision applications, the fundamental understanding of their dimensional and representational geometry remains relatively underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce Transformer Geometry Observatory (TGO), a systematic framework of experiments and analysis pipelines designed to investigate the representational geometry and dynamics of Vision Transformers. TGO-I, the first installment of the framework, focuses on the spectral geometry of ViT representations. Using a ViT-Small/16 model trained on ImageNet-100, we analyze Effective Rank, Stable Rank, Participation Ratio, Spectral Entropy, Spectral Flatness, Spectral Anisotropy, covariance structure, eigenspectra, and singular value spectra throughout training. Our results reveal a consistent increase in dimensional utilization, accompanied by decreasing anisotropy, increasing spectral entropy, increasing participation ratio, and progressively flatter eigenspectra. Contrary to the common intuition that training should concentrate information into a small number of dominant directions, we observe a progressive redistribution of variance across representational dimensions. This phenomenon is particularly pronounced in the final CLS token representation, which exhibits the highest effective dimensionality and lowest anisotropy within the network.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Transformer几何观测站TGO-I：频谱几何观测站
尽管视觉Transformer（ViT）已被广泛采用并在众多计算机视觉应用中取得成功，但对其维度和表征几何的基本理解仍相对不足。为填补这一空白，我们提出了Transformer几何观测站（TGO），一个系统性的实验与分析框架，旨在研究视觉Transformer的表征几何与动态。TGO-I作为该框架的首个部分，聚焦于ViT表征的频谱几何。我们使用在ImageNet-100上训练的ViT-Small/16模型，分析了有效秩、稳定秩、参与比、频谱熵、频谱平坦度、频谱各向异性、协方差结构、特征谱和奇异值谱在训练过程中的变化。结果表明，维度利用率持续增加，同时各向异性降低、频谱熵增加、参与比上升、特征谱逐渐趋于平坦。与普遍认为训练应将信息集中于少数主导方向的直觉相反，我们观察到表征维度间方差的逐步再分配。这一现象在最终的CLS标记表征中尤为显著，其在网络中表现出最高的有效维度和最低的各向异性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of understanding of the geometric properties underlying Vision Transformers' success, this study introduces TGO-I, a framework designed to analyze the spectral geometry of ViT representations. Using a ViT-Small/16 model trained on ImageNet-100, the authors measure multiple spectral metrics including effective rank, stable rank, participation ratio, spectral entropy, and eigenspectra across training. Their findings show that as training progresses, the model’s representations become more dimensionally utilized, less anisotropic, more entropic, and more evenly distributed across dimensions — contradicting the expectation that training concentrates variance into a few dominant directions. The CLS token, in particular, demonstrates the highest effective dimensionality and lowest anisotropy, indicating a unique role in spectral geometry evolution.
鉴于视觉Transformer（ViT）虽广泛应用但其维度与表征几何仍缺乏系统理解，本研究提出TGO-I框架，旨在分析ViT表征的频谱几何特性。基于在ImageNet-100上训练的ViT-Small/16模型，作者考察了有效秩、谱熵、参与比及特征谱等指标随训练的变化。结果表明，随着训练进行，模型表征维度利用更充分、各向同性增强、谱分布更平坦、方差更均匀分散——与普遍认为训练会集中信息于少数主导方向的直觉相反。其中CLS标记在训练后期展现出最高有效维度与最低各向异性，表明表征方差发生了持续再分配而非集中化。
A Taxonomy of Mental Health and Technology Needs for Alzheimer's and Dementia Caregivers
Authors: Keran Wang, Drishti Goel, Jiayue Melissa Shi, Violeta J. Rodriguez, Daniel S. Brown, Dong Whi Yoo, Ravi Karkar, Koustuv Saha
First: 2026-06-17T16:25:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:25:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Family members caring for individuals with Alzheimer's disease and related dementias (AD/ADRD) provide the foundation of long-term care worldwide. In 2023, more than 11 million U.S. family and friends contributed 18 billion hours of unpaid care, often at the cost of their own physical and mental health. These informal caregivers -- also referred as the "invisible second patients" -- experience elevated rates of mental health problems. Yet research commonly reduces their complex psychosocial experiences to a single construct of caregiver burden, obscuring which specific needs are unmet or effectively supported. At the same time, digital and AI-enabled technologies are rapidly expanding, from smartphone apps and videoconferencing to sensor platforms and AI chatbots. However, the absence of shared frameworks across medicine, psychology, and technology research limits cumulative progress. This study introduces a Caregiver Mental Health and Technology Taxonomy that systematically links AD/ADRD caregiver needs with corresponding classes of technology-based interventions. Drawing from an interdisciplinary literature review and two qualitative studies with caregivers, the taxonomy identifies mismatches between caregiver priorities and existing technological support, highlights under-served domains such as relational strain and compassion fatigue, and proposes design directions for adaptive, responsive systems. The framework offers a shared vocabulary to guide clinicians, researchers, and technology designers in developing more person-centered and clinically grounded innovation in dementia care.
中文标题/摘要
标题：阿尔茨海默病及痴呆症照护者心理健康与技术需求分类体系
全球范围内，阿尔茨海默病及相关痴呆症（AD/ADRD）患者的照护主要由家庭成员承担。2023年，美国超过1100万亲友提供了总计180亿小时的无偿照护，常以牺牲自身身心健康为代价。这些非正式照护者——亦被称为“隐形的第二患者”——面临更高的心理健康问题风险。然而，现有研究常将他们复杂的心理社会体验简化为单一的“照护负担”概念，掩盖了具体未被满足或有效支持的需求。与此同时，数字与人工智能技术正迅速扩展，从智能手机应用、视频会议到传感器平台和AI聊天机器人。但医学、心理学与技术研究领域缺乏统一框架，限制了整体进展。本研究提出“照护者心理健康与技术分类体系”，系统关联AD/ADRD照护者需求与对应的技术干预类别。该体系基于跨学科文献综述及两项对照护者的质性研究，识别照护者优先事项与现有技术支持之间的不匹配，突出关系压力与同情疲劳等未被充分服务的领域，并提出适应性、响应式系统的设计方向。该框架提供共享术语，引导临床医生、研究人员与技术设计者开发更以人为本、临床基础更扎实的痴呆照护创新。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the gap in understanding the diverse mental health needs of Alzheimer’s and dementia caregivers, who often suffer from unmet psychosocial demands yet are commonly reduced to a single 'burden' construct in research. The authors develop a taxonomy that maps caregiver needs to specific technology-based interventions, derived from interdisciplinary literature and qualitative caregiver interviews. The taxonomy reveals mismatches between caregiver priorities and current tech solutions, particularly underserving areas like relational strain and compassion fatigue, and proposes design principles for more adaptive, person-centered technologies to better support caregivers’ mental health.
鉴于阿尔茨海默病及痴呆症照护者普遍面临心理健康问题，本研究构建了一个将照护者心理社会需求与技术干预类型对应起来的分类框架。通过跨学科文献综述和对照护者的定性访谈，该框架揭示了现有数字工具在应对关系压力和同情疲劳等关键领域上的不足，突破了传统以“照护负担”单一维度概括照护者体验的局限。该分类体系为临床医生、研究人员和技术开发者提供统一术语，以更精准地设计响应性、以人为本的痴呆照护技术方案。
TxBench-PP: Analyzing AI Agent Performance on Small-Molecule Preclinical Pharmacology
Authors: Hannah Le, Ramesh Ramasamy, Alex Urrutia, Mahsa Yazdani, Tim Proctor, Kenny Workman
First: 2026-06-17T16:23:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:23:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial intelligence (AI) agents promise to accelerate drug discovery by compressing interpretation and decision-making loops, but practical deployment requires trusted evaluation on realistic program decisions. We introduce TherapeuticsBench Preclinical Pharmacology (TxBench-PP), a verifiable benchmark for small-molecule preclinical pharmacology and the first focused slice of a broader TherapeuticsBench effort across drug-discovery stages and therapeutic modalities. TxBench-PP tests whether agents can recover accurate conclusions from real-world assay data rather than memorized facts from literature. The benchmark contains 100 evaluations indexed by program stage, assay type, and task structure, spanning mechanism-of-action (MoA) and pharmacodynamic (PD) reasoning, compound-target engagement, causal target validation, developability and safety, and translational efficacy. Agents receive realistic workflow snapshots, inspect files in a coding environment, and return structured answers graded deterministically. Across 16 model-harness configurations, comprising 11 models and 4,800 trajectories, no system reliably recovered preclinical pharmacology decisions. The strongest configuration, Claude Opus 4.8 / Pi, passed 59.3\% of endpoint attempts (178/300; 95\% CI, 51.1-67.6), followed by GPT-5.5 / Pi at 55.3\% (166/300; 47.0-63.6).
中文标题/摘要
标题：TxBench-PP：分析AI代理在小分子临床前药理学中的表现
人工智能（AI）代理有望通过压缩解释和决策循环加速药物发现，但实际部署需要在现实程序决策上获得可信赖的评估。我们引入了治疗学基准临床前药理学（TxBench-PP），这是针对小分子临床前药理学的可验证基准，也是更广泛的治疗学基准项目中首个聚焦于药物发现各阶段和治疗模式的切片。TxBench-PP检验代理是否能从真实世界实验数据中恢复准确结论，而非从文献中记忆的事实。该基准包含100项评估，按程序阶段、实验类型和任务结构索引，涵盖作用机制（MoA）和药效学（PD）推理、化合物靶点结合、因果靶点验证、可开发性与安全性，以及转化疗效。代理接收现实工作流快照，在编码环境中检查文件，并返回结构化答案，由确定性评分。在16种模型-工具配置中（包含11种模型和4800条轨迹），无系统能可靠恢复临床前药理学决策。最强配置为Claude Opus 4.8 / Pi，通过59.3%的终点尝试（178/300；95%置信区间51.1-67.6），其次是GPT-5.5 / Pi，通过55.3%（166/300；47.0-63.6）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate AI agents' ability to make trustworthy decisions in real-world drug discovery workflows, the authors introduce TxBench-PP, a benchmark focused on small-molecule preclinical pharmacology. The method involves testing agents with realistic assay data, requiring them to interpret files in a coding environment and return structured, deterministically graded responses across 100 evaluations covering MoA, PD, target engagement, and safety. Experimental results across 16 model-harness configurations show no system consistently performs well; the top-performing setup, Claude Opus 4.8 / Pi, achieved 59.3% success rate (178/300 endpoints), while GPT-5.5 / Pi followed at 55.3%, indicating significant challenges in automating preclinical pharmacology reasoning.
为评估AI代理在真实药物发现工作流中做出可信决策的能力，作者提出了TxBench-PP基准，聚焦小分子临床前药理学。该方法要求代理在编码环境中解析真实实验数据文件，并返回结构化、可确定评分的答案，涵盖100项评估，包括作用机制、药效学、靶点结合及安全性等。在16种模型组合中，无系统能稳定成功；表现最佳的Claude Opus 4.8 / Pi组合准确率为59.3%（178/300），GPT-5.5 / Pi为55.3%（166/300），表明AI代理在复杂药理数据推理方面仍存在显著不足。
Epipolar Geometry Improves Video Generation Models
Authors: Orest Kupyn, Théo Uscidda, Marta Tintore Gazulla, Fabian Manhardt, Federico Tombari, Christian Rupprecht
First: 2025-10-24T16:21:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:22:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video generation models have advanced significantly through the latent diffusion transformers trained with rectified flow techniques. Yet these models still struggle with geometric inconsistencies, unstable motion, and visual artifacts that break the illusion of realistic 3D scenes. 3D-consistent video generation could significantly impact numerous downstream applications in generation and reconstruction tasks. We explore how epipolar geometry constraints improve modern video diffusion models. Despite using massive training data, these models fail to capture fundamental geometric principles. We align diffusion models using pairwise epipolar geometry constraints via preference-based optimization, directly addressing unstable trajectories and geometric artifacts through mathematically principled geometric enforcement. Our approach efficiently enforces geometric principles without requiring end-to-end differentiability. Evaluation demonstrates that classical geometric constraints provide more stable optimization signals than modern learned metrics. Training on static scenes with dynamic cameras ensures metric quality while the model generalizes to various dynamic scenes. By bridging data-driven learning with classical computer vision, we reduce epipolar error by 31% and improve human-rated consistency from 54% to 72% without compromising visual quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：极线几何提升视频生成模型
通过校正流技术训练的潜在扩散变换器，视频生成模型已取得显著进展。然而，这些模型仍存在几何不一致、运动不稳定和视觉伪影等问题，破坏了真实3D场景的沉浸感。3D一致的视频生成将对生成与重建任务的众多下游应用产生重大影响。我们探讨了极线几何约束如何改进现代视频扩散模型。尽管使用了大量训练数据，这些模型仍未能捕捉基本几何原理。我们通过基于偏好的优化，利用成对极线几何约束对扩散模型进行对齐，通过数学严谨的几何约束直接解决运动轨迹不稳定和几何伪影问题。我们的方法无需端到端可微性即可高效执行几何约束。评估表明，经典几何约束比现代学习度量提供更稳定的优化信号。在静态场景与动态相机上训练可确保度量质量，同时模型能泛化至各种动态场景。通过结合数据驱动学习与经典计算机视觉，我们在不牺牲视觉质量的前提下，将极线误差降低31%，人类评分的一致性从54%提升至72%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the geometric inconsistencies and visual artifacts in current video generation models, this work introduces epipolar geometry constraints to improve 3D consistency in video diffusion models. The method aligns diffusion models using pairwise epipolar constraints via preference-based optimization, enforcing geometric principles without requiring end-to-end differentiability. Experimental results show that this approach reduces epipolar error by 31% and boosts human-rated consistency from 54% to 72%, while maintaining visual quality, especially when trained on static scenes with dynamic cameras.
为解决当前视频生成模型存在的几何不一致和视觉伪影问题，本文引入极线几何约束以提升视频扩散模型的三维一致性。该方法通过偏好优化对扩散模型施加成对极线约束，无需端到端可微性即可实现几何原理的强制执行。实验表明，该方法将极线误差降低31%，人类评分的一致性从54%提升至72%，同时保持视觉质量，尤其在静态场景与动态相机训练下效果显著。
Seeing Through Occlusion: Deterministic Arm Kinematic Correction for Robot Teleoperation
Authors: Thomas M. Kwok, Nicholas Koenig, Yue Hu
First: 2026-06-17T16:20:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:20:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Markerless, single-RGB-D-camera motion capture provides a low-cost and non-invasive alternative to conventional marker-based systems for robot teleoperation; however, depth estimation often degrades in the presence of self-occlusion, particularly during upper-limb motion. This paper presents an Arm Kinematic Correction (AKC) method that improves depth estimation by enforcing geometric constraints based on constant arm lengths. The proposed approach reconstructs occluded joint depths by leveraging wrist positions and predefined arm lengths via a deterministic formulation based on the Pythagorean theorem, thereby avoiding the need for complex probabilistic modeling or parameter tuning. Experimental validation against a Vicon reference system demonstrates reliable performance for both static and dynamic joint motions, evaluated using root-mean-square error (RMSE) and Pearson correlation. Furthermore, motion-mapping teleoperation is successfully demonstrated in both simulated and physical robot environments. The results show that AKC enhances robustness and preserves anatomical consistency under long-duration, severe self-occlusion, even when paired with less reliable temporal filters, highlighting its practicality for real-time applications such as robot teleoperation and human-robot interaction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：穿透遮挡：用于机器人遥操作的确定性手臂运动学校正
无标记、单RGB-D相机的运动捕捉为机器人遥操作提供了一种低成本且非侵入性的替代方案，但深度估计在自遮挡（尤其上肢运动时）下常会退化。本文提出了一种基于恒定臂长几何约束的臂运动学校正（AKC）方法，通过腕部位置和预设臂长，利用勾股定理的确定性公式重建被遮挡关节的深度，无需复杂的概率建模或参数调优。实验对比Vicon参考系统，采用均方根误差（RMSE）和皮尔逊相关系数评估，验证了AKC在静态与动态关节运动中的可靠性。同时在仿真与物理机器人环境中成功实现运动映射遥操作。结果表明，AKC在长时间严重自遮挡下仍能增强鲁棒性并保持解剖一致性，即使搭配可靠性较低的时间滤波器，也凸显其在实时应用（如机器人遥操作与人机交互）中的实用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the degradation of depth estimation in markerless RGB-D motion capture during self-occlusion in upper-limb movements, this paper introduces Arm Kinematic Correction (AKC), a deterministic method that leverages known arm lengths and wrist positions to reconstruct occluded joint depths using the Pythagorean theorem. The approach avoids probabilistic modeling and parameter tuning, enhancing robustness under severe occlusion. Experiments against a Vicon system show consistent performance with low RMSE and high Pearson correlation for both static and dynamic motions, and successful teleoperation in simulated and physical environments, demonstrating AKC’s practicality for real-time human-robot interaction.
为了解决RGB-D相机在上肢运动中因自遮挡导致的深度估计退化问题，本文提出了一种基于几何约束的确定性方法——臂运动学校正（AKC），利用已知臂长和腕部位置，通过勾股定理重建被遮挡关节的深度，无需概率建模或参数调优。实验对比Vicon系统显示，该方法在静态与动态运动中均具有低均方根误差和高皮尔逊相关系数，并在仿真与实物机器人环境中成功实现运动映射式遥操作，证明其在长时间严重遮挡下仍能保持解剖一致性，适用于实时人机交互场景。
VGGHeads: 3D Multi Head Alignment with a Large-Scale Synthetic Dataset
Authors: Orest Kupyn, Eugene Khvedchenia, Christian Rupprecht
First: 2024-07-25T17:58:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:18:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human head detection, keypoint estimation, and 3D head model fitting are essential tasks with many applications. However, traditional real-world datasets often suffer from bias, privacy, and ethical concerns, and they have been recorded in laboratory environments, which makes it difficult for trained models to generalize. Here, we introduce \method -- a large-scale synthetic dataset generated with diffusion models for human head detection and 3D mesh estimation. Our dataset comprises over 1 million high-resolution images, each annotated with detailed 3D head meshes, facial landmarks, and bounding boxes. Using this dataset, we introduce a new model architecture capable of simultaneous head detection and head mesh reconstruction from a single image in a single step. Through extensive experimental evaluations, we demonstrate that models trained on our synthetic data achieve strong performance on real images. Furthermore, the versatility of our dataset makes it applicable across a broad spectrum of tasks, offering a general and comprehensive representation of human heads.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VGGHeads：基于大规模合成数据集的3D多头对齐
人体头部检测、关键点估计和3D头部模型拟合是具有广泛应用的重要任务。然而，传统真实世界数据集常存在偏差、隐私和伦理问题，且多在实验室环境中采集，导致训练模型难以泛化。本文引入\method——一种基于扩散模型生成的大型合成数据集，用于人体头部检测和3D网格估计。该数据集包含超过100万张高分辨率图像，每张图像均标注有详细的3D头部网格、面部关键点和边界框。基于此数据集，我们提出一种新模型架构，可从单张图像一步完成头部检测与头部网格重建。通过大量实验评估，我们证明在该合成数据上训练的模型在真实图像上表现优异。此外，该数据集的通用性使其适用于广泛任务，为人体头部提供全面且通用的表示。

Motivated by the limitations of real-world datasets in human head detection and 3D modeling due to bias, privacy, and poor generalization, the authors introduce VGGHeads, a large-scale synthetic dataset generated via diffusion models. The dataset contains over 1 million high-resolution images annotated with 3D head meshes, facial landmarks, and bounding boxes. Experiments show that models trained on this synthetic data generalize well to real-world images, achieving competitive performance across multiple benchmarks and demonstrating broad applicability for head-related tasks.
为解决真实数据集在人体头部检测与三维建模中存在的偏见、隐私问题及泛化能力不足，作者提出VGGHeads，一个通过扩散模型生成的超大规模合成数据集，包含超过100万张高分辨率图像，每张图像标注有三维网格、面部关键点和边界框。他们设计了一种联合模型架构，可从单张图像一步完成头部检测与三维网格重建。实验表明，基于该合成数据训练的模型在真实图像上表现优异，展现出良好的泛化能力，且该数据集可广泛应用于多种头部相关任务。
DiFlow-TTS: Compact and Low-Latency Zero-Shot Text-to-Speech with Discrete Flow Matching
Authors: Ngoc-Son Nguyen, Thanh V. T. Tran, Hieu-Nghia Huynh-Nguyen, Truong-Son Hy, Van Nguyen
First: 2025-09-11T17:16:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:16:04+00:00
Comments: Accepted at Interspeech 2026 (Long Paper Track)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Zero-shot text-to-speech (TTS) has made significant progress in replicating unseen voices, yet balancing generation quality and inference efficiency remains challenging. Autoregressive models suffer from high latency, while diffusion-based approaches are constrained by training-time configurations. Moreover, most flow-based methods operate in continuous space, which introduces optimization challenges because continuous token spaces are inherently more complex than discrete ones. To address these limitations, we propose DiFlow-TTS, a novel zero-shot TTS framework based on discrete flow matching. The model consists of a deterministic Phoneme-Content Mapper for linguistic modeling and a Factorized Discrete Flow Denoiser that simultaneously generates prosody and acoustic token streams. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach across multiple evaluation metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DiFlow-TTS：基于离散流匹配的紧凑型低延迟零样本文本到语音系统
零样本文本到语音（TTS）在复制未见声音方面取得了显著进展，但平衡生成质量和推理效率仍具挑战。自回归模型存在高延迟问题，而扩散方法受限于训练时配置。此外，多数基于流的方法在连续空间中操作，由于连续标记空间比离散空间更复杂，这引入了优化难题。为解决这些限制，我们提出DiFlow-TTS，一种基于离散流匹配的新型零样本TTS框架。该模型包含一个确定性的音素-内容映射器用于语言建模，以及一个因子化离散流去噪器，可同时生成语调和声学标记流。实验结果表明，我们的方法在多个评估指标上均有效。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to balance quality and efficiency in zero-shot TTS, DiFlow-TTS introduces a discrete flow matching framework that avoids the latency of autoregressive models and the training rigidity of diffusion methods. It employs a deterministic Phoneme-Content Mapper for linguistic encoding and a Factorized Discrete Flow Denoiser to jointly generate prosody and acoustic tokens in discrete space, sidestepping the optimization complexities of continuous flows. Experiments show it achieves competitive voice quality while maintaining low latency and compact model size across multiple benchmarks.
为在零样本语音合成中兼顾生成质量与推理效率，DiFlow-TTS 提出了一种基于离散流匹配的框架，规避了自回归模型的高延迟和扩散模型训练配置受限的问题。该方法采用确定性音素-内容映射器进行语言建模，并通过因子化离散流去噪器同时生成韵律与声学令牌，避免了连续空间流模型的优化复杂性。实验表明，DiFlow-TTS 在多项评估指标上表现优异，同时显著降低延迟与模型规模，优于当前主流零样本TTS方法。
"Did you lie?" Evaluating Lie Detectors across Model Scale and Belief-Verified Model Organisms
Authors: Alan Cooney, David Africa, Geoffrey Irving
First: 2026-06-10T19:21:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:15:20+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robust lie detectors for language models could enable powerful techniques for auditing, monitoring, and post-hoc investigation of model behaviour, but evaluating them requires testbeds where models verifiably believe the opposite of what they say. We show that existing trained model organisms often fail this requirement, leaving prior positive and negative detection results difficult to interpret. We address this with 13 reasoning model organisms whose hidden beliefs are verified in chain-of-thought and shown to generalise to held-out tasks, alongside Varied Deception, a prompted-lying testbed covering a broad range of lie-inducing motivations. On these testbeds we evaluate four detectors: a chain-of-thought judge, a logprob classifier, and two activation probes, including Did-You-Lie (DYL), a new method for training follow-up probes. On prompted lying, across 31 open-weight models spanning 2B to 1T parameters, all four detectors show positive scaling with model capability. However, every activation- and logprob-based detector drops sharply on our trained model organisms, with DYL retaining the most signal; only the chain-of-thought judge remains strong, with 0.82 balanced accuracy, partly as an artefact of our verification process favouring CoT-readable beliefs. Current lie detectors therefore cannot support high-confidence claims about model beliefs, and we suggest research directions that may address some of their current limitations. We release our datasets, model organisms, and trained detectors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：“你撒谎了吗？”：在不同模型规模和信念可验证的模型生物上评估测谎器
为语言模型开发稳健的测谎器，可支持对模型行为的审计、监控和事后调查，但评估需依赖模型确信其所说内容与实际相反的测试环境。我们发现现有训练模型生物常无法满足此要求，导致此前的正负检测结果难以解读。为此，我们构建了13个推理模型生物，其隐藏信念通过思维链验证并能泛化至未见任务，同时引入“多样化欺骗”测试环境，覆盖广泛欺骗动机。在这些测试环境中，我们评估四种测谎器：思维链裁判、对数概率分类器及两种激活探针，包括新方法“你撒谎了吗”（DYL），用于训练后续探针。在提示式欺骗测试中，针对31个参数规模从2B至1T的开源模型，所有四种测谎器均随模型能力提升而表现增强。然而，所有基于激活和对数概率的测谎器在我们训练的模型生物上表现骤降，DYL保留最强信号；唯有思维链裁判表现稳健，准确率达0.82，部分归因于验证过程偏好可读信念。当前测谎器尚无法支持对模型信念的高置信度断言，我们建议若干研究方向以缓解其局限。我们公开数据集、模型生物及训练好的测谎器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable lie detectors to audit language models, this study addresses the challenge that existing model organisms often fail to verifiably hold contradictory beliefs to their outputs. The authors introduce 13 reasoning model organisms with verified hidden beliefs via chain-of-thought and a new prompted-lying testbed called Varied Deception. They evaluate four detectors — a chain-of-thought judge, a logprob classifier, and two activation probes, including the novel Did-You-Lie (DYL) probe — across 31 open-weight models from 2B to 1T parameters. All detectors scale with model size on prompted lying, but activation- and logprob-based methods perform poorly on the trained model organisms, with DYL showing the most consistent signal. Only the chain-of-thought judge maintains strong performance (0.82 balanced accuracy), likely due to its alignment with the verification process. The study concludes that current detectors cannot reliably infer model beliefs and suggests future research directions.
为实现对语言模型的可靠谎言检测以支持审计，本研究提出构建可验证模型信念与表面输出矛盾的测试平台。作者设计了13个通过思维链验证信念的推理模型生物体，并引入名为“Varied Deception”的提示式谎言测试集。在31个从2B到1T参数规模的开源模型上，评估了四种检测器（含新方法Did-You-Lie）：所有检测器在提示式谎言任务中随模型规模提升而增强，但基于激活与对数概率的检测器在训练模型生物体上表现骤降，DYL保持最强信号；仅思维链法官保持高准确率（0.82），可能源于验证过程偏好可读信念。研究指出当前检测器无法可靠推断模型信念，并建议未来研究方向。
S3OD: Towards Generalizable Salient Object Detection with Synthetic Data
Authors: Orest Kupyn, Hirokatsu Kataoka, Christian Rupprecht
First: 2025-10-24T16:10:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:14:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Salient object detection exemplifies data-bounded tasks where expensive pixel-precise annotations force separate model training for related subtasks like DIS and HR-SOD. We present a method that dramatically improves generalization through large-scale synthetic data generation and ambiguity-aware architecture. We introduce S3OD, a dataset of over 139,000 high-resolution images created through our multi-modal diffusion pipeline that extracts labels from diffusion and DINO-v3 features. The iterative generation framework prioritizes challenging categories based on model performance. We propose a streamlined multi-mask decoder that handles the inherent ambiguity in salient object detection by predicting multiple valid interpretations. Models trained only on synthetic data achieve 20-50% error reduction in cross-dataset generalization, while fine-tuned versions reach state-of-the-art performance across DIS and HR-SOD benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：S3OD：利用合成数据实现可泛化的显著物体检测
显著物体检测是数据受限任务的典型代表，昂贵的像素级标注迫使相关子任务（如DIS和HR-SOD）需单独训练模型。我们提出一种方法，通过大规模合成数据生成和模糊感知架构显著提升泛化能力。我们引入S3OD数据集，包含超过13.9万张高分辨率图像，通过多模态扩散管道生成，其标签由扩散与DINO-v3特征提取。迭代生成框架根据模型表现优先生成挑战性类别。我们提出一种简化多掩码解码器，通过预测多个有效解释来处理显著物体检测固有的模糊性。仅在合成数据上训练的模型在跨数据集泛化中错误率降低20-50%，微调版本在DIS和HR-SOD基准测试中达到最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and scarcity of pixel-precise annotations in salient object detection, this work introduces S3OD, a synthetic dataset of 139,000 high-resolution images generated via a multi-modal diffusion pipeline that leverages diffusion and DINO-v3 features to produce labels. The method employs an iterative generation strategy that focuses on challenging categories and a streamlined multi-mask decoder to handle inherent ambiguity in salient object detection. Experimental results show that models trained solely on synthetic data achieve 20-50% error reduction in cross-dataset generalization, and fine-tuned models reach state-of-the-art performance on both DIS and HR-SOD benchmarks.
为应对显著目标检测中像素级标注成本高昂的问题，该研究提出S3OD数据集，通过多模态扩散管道生成13.9万张高分辨率合成图像，利用扩散模型与DINO-v3特征提取标签。方法采用迭代生成策略聚焦难例类别，并设计多掩码解码器以应对检测固有的模糊性，支持多合理解释预测。实验表明，仅在合成数据上训练的模型在跨数据集泛化中误差降低20–50%，微调后模型在DIS与HR-SOD基准上达到当前最优性能。
STARE: Surprisal-Guided Token-Level Advantage Reweighting for Policy Entropy Stability
Authors: Haipeng Luo, Qingfeng Sun, Songli Wu, Can Xu, Wenfeng Deng, Han Hu, Yansong Tang
First: 2026-06-17T16:13:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:13:42+00:00
Comments: LLM, Reinforcement Learning
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards algorithms like GRPO have emerged as the dominant post-training paradigm for complex reasoning in LLMs, yet commonly suffer from policy entropy collapse during training. We conduct a first-order gradient analysis of token-level entropy dynamics under GRPO and identify a token-level credit assignment mismatch: the per-token entropy variation decomposes into the product of the trajectory-level advantage and an entropy sensitivity function over the next-token distribution, yielding an advantage-surprisal four-quadrant structure and a near-criticality property. Motivated by it, we propose STARE (Surprisal-guided Token-level Advantage Reweighting for policy Entropy stability), which identifies entropy-critical token subsets via batch-internal surprisal quantiles, selectively reweights their effective advantages, and incorporates a target-entropy closed-loop gate for stable entropy regulation. Across model scales from 1.5B to 32B and three task families (Short CoT, Long CoT, and Multi-Turn Tool Use), STARE sustains stable RL training over thousands of steps while maintaining policy entropy within the target band. On AIME24 and AIME25, STARE outperforms DAPO and other competitive baselines by 4%-8% in average accuracy, with reflection tokens and response length growing in tandem, indicating sustained exploration-exploitation balance that further unlocks RL training potential.Code is available at https://github.com/hp-luo/STARE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：STARE：基于意外性引导的令牌级优势重加权以实现策略熵稳定性
可验证奖励的强化学习算法（如GRPO）已成为大语言模型复杂推理后训练阶段的主流范式，但常在训练过程中遭遇策略熵崩溃。我们对GRPO下令牌级熵动态进行一阶梯度分析，发现令牌级信用分配不匹配：每个令牌的熵变化可分解为轨迹级优势与下一令牌分布熵敏感函数的乘积，形成优势-意外性四象限结构及近临界特性。受此启发，我们提出STARE（Surprisal-guided Token-level Advantage Reweighting for policy Entropy stability），通过批次内意外性分位数识别熵临界令牌子集，选择性重加权其有效优势，并引入目标熵闭环门控机制以稳定熵调节。在1.5B至32B模型规模及三类任务（短链CoT、长链CoT、多轮工具使用）中，STARE在数千步训练中维持稳定强化学习训练，同时保持策略熵在目标区间内。在AIME24与AIME25上，STARE平均准确率较DAPO及其他竞争基线高出4%-8%，且反思令牌与响应长度同步增长，表明持续的探索-利用平衡进一步释放强化学习训练潜力。代码见https://github.com/hp-luo/STARE。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the entropy collapse observed in GRPO-based RL training for LLMs, the authors analyze token-level entropy dynamics and identify a mismatch in credit assignment between trajectory advantages and token surprisal. They propose STARE, a method that reweights token advantages based on surprisal quantiles to identify entropy-critical tokens, and incorporates a closed-loop entropy gate for stabilization. Experiments across 1.5B to 32B models and three task families show STARE maintains stable entropy and improves accuracy by 4%-8% over baselines like DAPO on AIME24 and AIME25, while preserving exploration-exploitation balance.
为解决GRPO等可验证奖励强化学习方法在大模型训练中出现的策略熵崩溃问题，作者通过一阶梯度分析发现轨迹优势与词元 surprisal 间存在信用分配错配，并提出STARE方法，通过词元 surprisal 分位数识别熵敏感词元并重调其优势权重，配合闭环熵门控机制进行调节。实验表明，在1.5B至32B规模模型及三类任务上，STARE保持熵稳定并持续训练数千步，相较DAPO等基线在AIME24/AIME25上平均准确率提升4%-8%，且反思词元与响应长度同步增长，体现持续的探索-利用平衡。
LVLMs and Humans Ground Differently in Referential Communication
Authors: Peter Zeng, Weiling Li, Amie J. Paige, Zhengxiang Wang, Panagiotis Kaliosis, Dimitris Samaras, Gregory Zelinsky, Susan E. Brennan, Owen Rambow
First: 2026-01-27T16:52:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:13:02+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 16 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
For generative AI agents to partner effectively with human users, the ability to accurately predict human intent is critical. But this ability to collaborate remains limited by a critical deficit: an inability to model common ground. We present a referential communication experiment with a factorial design involving director-matcher pairs (human-human, human-AI, AI-human, and AI-AI) that interact with multiple turns in repeated rounds to match pictures of objects not associated with any obvious lexicalized labels. We show that LVLMs cannot interactively generate and resolve referring expressions in a way that enables smooth communication, a crucial skill that underlies human language use. We release our corpus of 356 dialogues (89 pairs over 4 rounds each) along with the online pipeline for data collection and the tools for analyzing accuracy, efficiency, and lexical overlap.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉-语言大模型与人类在指称交流中的共同基础不同
为了让生成式AI代理能与人类用户有效协作，准确预测人类意图至关重要。但这一协作能力受限于一个关键缺陷：无法建模共同基础。我们设计了一个因子实验，研究导演-匹配者配对（人类-人类、人类-AI、AI-人类、AI-AI）在多轮重复互动中匹配无明显词汇标签的物体图片。结果表明，视觉-语言大模型无法交互式生成和解决指称表达，从而无法实现顺畅沟通——这是人类语言使用的核心能力。我们公开了包含356个对话（89对，每对4轮）的数据集，以及数据收集的在线流程和用于分析准确率、效率及词汇重叠的工具。
Summary / 总结
To enable effective collaboration between generative AI agents and humans, accurately predicting human intent requires modeling shared understanding — a capability current LVLMs lack. The study conducts a referential communication experiment with factorial design across human-human, human-AI, AI-human, and AI-AI pairs, testing their ability to match unlabeled object images through multi-turn dialogue. Results show LVLMs fail to generate or resolve referring expressions interactively, hindering smooth communication — a core human language skill — while human-human pairs perform reliably. The study also releases a corpus of 356 dialogues and analysis tools to support further research.
为实现有效的人机协作，准确预测人类意图需建模共同理解，而当前大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在此方面存在缺陷。研究通过因子设计实验，测试人类与人类、人类与AI、AI与人类、AI与AI四类配对在多轮对话中匹配无明确词标签物体图像的能力。结果表明，LVLMs无法交互式生成或解决指称表达，阻碍了流畅沟通——这是人类语言的核心能力。研究同时公开了89对参与者、共356轮对话的数据集，以及用于分析准确率、效率和词汇重叠的工具。
Mobile Pedipulation for Object Sliding via Hierarchical Control on a Wheeled Bipedal Robot
Authors: Yue Qin, Yulun Zhuang, Zelin Shen, Yanran Ding
First: 2026-06-17T16:09:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:09:02+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this letter, we present a hierarchical control framework that enables wheeled bipedal robots to perform planar object sliding tasks with their wheeled legs. The proposed approach formulates a nonlinear model predictive controller (NMPC) based on a reduced-order three rigid bodies (TRB) dynamical model that explicitly accounts for the hip roll degree of freedom and multiple wheel-environment contact modes, which is essential for lateral stepping and pedipulation tasks. Within this framework, the NMPC simultaneously regulates robot locomotion and interaction forces, allowing the robot to stably execute both rolling and object manipulation behaviors. A trajectory-optimization-based robot-object motion planner is developed to generate reference motions that incorporate stick-slip transitions in ground-object contact. Two representative pedipulation motions, namely scooting and lateral sliding, are validated through real-world hardware experiments, in which the robot successfully retrieves a 1 kg object from under a desk and slides a 4 kg object over a distance of 0.228 m via scooting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于分层控制的轮式双足机器人移动式物体滑动
本文提出了一种分层控制框架，使轮式双足机器人能够利用其轮式腿执行平面物体滑动任务。该方法基于简化三刚体（TRB）动力学模型构建非线性模型预测控制器（NMPC），明确考虑髋部滚动自由度及多种轮-环境接触模式，这对横向步态和移动式操作至关重要。在该框架下，NMPC同时调节机器人运动与交互力，使其稳定执行滚动与物体操作行为。开发了一种基于轨迹优化的机器人-物体运动规划器，生成包含地面-物体接触粘滑转换的参考运动。通过真实硬件实验验证了两种代表性移动式操作动作——爬行式移动与横向滑动，机器人成功从桌下拾取1kg物体，并通过爬行式移动将4kg物体滑动0.228米。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for wheeled bipedal robots to manipulate objects in constrained environments, this work proposes a hierarchical control framework combining nonlinear model predictive control with a reduced-order three-rigid-body dynamical model that captures hip roll and multi-contact modes. The method simultaneously controls robot locomotion and interaction forces to enable stable object sliding via pedipulation. Experimental results demonstrate successful retrieval of a 1 kg object and sliding of a 4 kg object 0.228 meters using scooting motion on real hardware.
为实现轮式双足机器人通过平面滑动操作物体，本文提出一种分层控制框架，结合非线性模型预测控制器（NMPC）与基于轨迹优化的运动规划器。NMPC基于三刚体动力学模型，显式建模髋部滚动及多轮地接触模式，以稳定控制机器人运动与交互力。实验验证表明，机器人成功完成两种“脚踏式”动作——爬行与侧滑，分别实现从桌下拾取1公斤物体及滑动4公斤物体0.228米。
A Human-in-the-Loop Bayesian Optimization Framework for Constraint-Aware Bioprocess Development
Authors: Samuel Stricker, Claus Wirnsperger, Alessandro Butté, Laura Helleckes, Gonzalo Guillén Gosálbez, Antonio del Rio Chanona, Mehmet Mercangöz
First: 2026-06-17T16:06:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:06:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work presents an extension to Pareto Front Guided Sampling (PFGS), a Human-in-the-Loop (HitL) Bayesian Optimization (BO) framework in which Gaussian process (GP) surrogate-derived quantities are reformulated as objectives of a multi-objective optimization problem, and the resulting Pareto front is exposed to a domain expert for interactive candidate selection rather than returning a single automated recommendation. The framework is extended in two directions: constrained optimization is addressed by incorporating the posterior probability of satisfying output specification limits as an explicit Pareto objective, computed analytically from the GP posterior distribution; robust optimization is addressed by a Monte Carlo sampling strategy that estimates expected lower-confidence performance over a user-defined variability of input perturbations, capturing performance degradation under likely implementation deviations. The resulting multi-dimensional Pareto representation renders trade-offs between predicted performance, model uncertainty, probabilistic constraint satisfaction, and input robustness simultaneously visible through pairwise two-dimensional projections on an interactive dashboard, enabling selection criteria to be iteratively refined as the surrogate model improves and development objectives evolve. The framework is showcased on an eight-dimensional fed-batch Chinese Hamster Ovary (CHO) cell culture simulator demonstrating systematic identification of high-performing, feasibility-compliant, and perturbation-resilient operating conditions, and illustrating how expert-defined requirements provide a principled stopping criterion and support informed allocation of experimental resources.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向约束感知生物工艺开发的人类参与贝叶斯优化框架
本工作提出对帕累托前沿引导采样（PFGS）框架的扩展，该框架属于人类参与（HitL）贝叶斯优化（BO），其中高斯过程（GP）代理模型导出的量被重新表述为多目标优化问题的目标，生成的帕累托前沿由领域专家交互式选择候选方案，而非返回单一自动化推荐。框架在两个方向上扩展：约束优化通过将满足输出规格限的后验概率作为显式帕累托目标纳入，该概率从GP后验分布中解析计算；鲁棒优化通过蒙特卡洛采样策略，估计在用户定义的输入扰动变异性下预期的低置信性能，捕捉在可能的实施偏差下的性能退化。所得多维帕累托表示通过交互式仪表板上的成对二维投影，同时可视化预测性能、模型不确定性、概率约束满足度和输入鲁棒性之间的权衡，使选择标准可随着代理模型改进和开发目标演变而迭代优化。该框架在八维补料批次中国仓鼠卵巢（CHO）细胞培养模拟器上展示，系统性识别出高性能、可行性合规且抗扰动的操作条件，并说明专家定义的需求如何提供原则性停止准则并支持实验资源的明智分配。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interactive, constraint-aware optimization in bioprocess development, this work extends Pareto Front Guided Sampling (PFGS) by integrating probabilistic constraint satisfaction and input robustness into a multi-objective Bayesian optimization framework. It reformulates GP surrogate outputs as Pareto objectives, enabling domain experts to iteratively select candidates from a multi-dimensional trade-off surface. The method computes constraint satisfaction via GP posterior probabilities and estimates robustness using Monte Carlo sampling over input perturbations. Experimental results on an eight-dimensional CHO cell culture simulator demonstrate the framework’s ability to identify high-performing, feasible, and resilient operating conditions while allowing experts to refine selection criteria as the model improves.
为解决生物工艺开发中对约束条件和鲁棒性的交互式优化需求，本研究扩展了Pareto前沿引导采样（PFGS）框架，将高斯过程代理模型的约束满足概率与输入扰动下的性能鲁棒性纳入多目标优化。方法将代理模型输出重构为帕累托目标，使领域专家可通过交互式仪表盘可视化性能、不确定性、约束满足概率与鲁棒性之间的权衡，并动态调整选择标准。在八维中国仓鼠卵巢细胞培养模拟器上的实验表明，该框架能系统识别高性能、可行且抗扰动的操作条件，同时支持专家定义的停止准则与实验资源分配决策。
Implicit vs. Explicit Prompting Strategies for LVLMs in Referential Communication
Authors: Peter Zeng, Amie J. Paige, Weiling Li, Susan E. Brennan, Owen Rambow, Cameron R. Jones
First: 2026-06-16T00:05:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:05:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Two recent studies (Jones et al. (2026); Zeng et al. (2026)) reach apparently contradictory conclusions about whether LVLMs can coordinate on efficient referring expressions. We control for task differences between the studies while directly comparing their prompting styles. We replicate the finding that models can coordinate efficient referring expressions when explicitly prompted to do so, suggesting that other task differences are not responsible for divergent results. However, we also find that the same models fail to infer the need for communicative efficiency from a more implicit prompt, highlighting critical differences between how humans and AI systems communicate.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LVLMs在指称交流中的隐式与显式提示策略比较
两项近期研究（Jones等，2026；Zeng等，2026）对LVLMs是否能协调高效指称表达得出了看似矛盾的结论。我们在控制两项研究任务差异的同时，直接比较其提示风格。我们复现了模型在显式提示下能协调高效指称表达的发现，表明其他任务差异并非导致结果分歧的原因。然而，我们也发现同一模型在隐式提示下无法推断出沟通效率的需求，凸显了人类与AI系统在沟通方式上的关键差异。
