This study investigates why two recent papers reached conflicting conclusions about large vision-language models' ability to coordinate efficient referring expressions, by controlling for task differences and directly comparing explicit versus implicit prompting strategies. The researchers replicate the finding that models successfully produce efficient references when explicitly instructed to do so, indicating that task design alone does not explain the discrepancy. However, they also observe that the same models fail to spontaneously infer communicative efficiency under implicit prompts, revealing a fundamental gap between human intuitive communication and AI’s reliance on explicit guidance.
该研究旨在解释两篇近期论文对大视觉-语言模型是否能协调高效指代表达得出矛盾结论的原因，通过控制任务差异并直接对比显式与隐式提示策略进行比较。研究者复现了模型在显式指令下能成功生成高效指代表达的结果，表明任务差异并非导致结论分歧的根本原因。然而，他们也发现同一模型在隐式提示下无法自发推断出沟通效率的需求，揭示了人类沟通直觉与当前AI系统在缺乏明确引导时推导沟通目标能力之间的根本差距。
Constant Time-Delay Leader Following with Neural Networks and Invariant Extended Kalman Filters for Arbitrary Trajectories
Authors: Luka Antonyshyn, Paulo Ricardo Marques de Araujo, Sidney Givigi
First: 2026-06-17T16:04:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T16:04:58+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper proposes a constant time-delay trajectory tracking method for vehicle convoys operating without inter-vehicle communication, a common coordinate system, or global positioning. The method integrates a probabilistic sequence-to-sequence (Seq2Seq) neural network with an invariant extended Kalman filter (IEKF) to warm-start the prediction process, allowing accurate estimation of a leader vehicle's relative trajectory on the SE(2) manifold. A geometric model predictive controller is further incorporated to fully exploit the manifold-based trajectory predictions for improved control performance. The system can handle arbitrary nonlinear trajectories with varying speeds and motion profiles while reducing the need for expert-based domain knowledge for the design of trajectory following systems, even under long trajectory delays. The effectiveness of the method is validated through comparisons with a pure IEKF baseline, learning-based methods, and the ground-truth trajectory in kinematic simulations, as well as in experiments using real robotic vehicles.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于神经网络与不变扩展卡尔曼滤波器的恒定时间延迟领车跟随方法（适用于任意轨迹）
本文提出了一种适用于无车际通信、无共同坐标系或全球定位的车辆编队的恒定时间延迟轨迹跟踪方法。该方法将概率序列到序列（Seq2Seq）神经网络与不变扩展卡尔曼滤波器（IEKF）相结合，以预热预测过程，从而在SE(2)流形上准确估计领车的相对轨迹。进一步引入几何模型预测控制器，充分利用基于流形的轨迹预测，提升控制性能。该系统可处理任意非线性轨迹，支持不同速度与运动模式，且在长轨迹延迟下仍可减少对专家领域知识的依赖。方法的有效性通过与纯IEKF基线、学习方法及真实轨迹在运动学仿真中的对比，以及在真实机器人车辆实验中的验证得以证实。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable vehicle convoy tracking without inter-vehicle communication or global positioning, this paper introduces a method combining a probabilistic sequence-to-sequence neural network with an invariant extended Kalman filter to predict and track a leader vehicle’s trajectory on the SE(2) manifold, even under constant time delays. The system uses the neural network to warm-start the IEKF, enabling accurate relative trajectory estimation, and integrates a geometric model predictive controller to leverage manifold-based predictions for enhanced control. Experimental results across kinematic simulations and real robotic platforms show the method outperforms a pure IEKF baseline and learning-based alternatives, achieving high accuracy even with long delays and arbitrary nonlinear trajectories, while reducing reliance on expert-designed control logic.
为解决无通信、无全局定位条件下车队跟随轨迹的可靠性问题，该研究提出一种结合概率序列到序列神经网络与不变扩展卡尔曼滤波器的方法，用于在SE(2)流形上估计领导者车辆轨迹，即使存在恒定时间延迟。系统利用神经网络预热预测过程，并引入几何模型预测控制器，充分利用流形轨迹预测以实现对任意非线性路径的精准跟踪。实验结果表明，该方法在仿真与真实机器人实验中均优于纯IEKF基线及学习型方法，轨迹跟踪精度接近真实轨迹，且减少了对专家设计控制逻辑的依赖。
Mechanism-Guided Selective Unlearning for RLVR-Induced Reasoning
Authors: Chenyu Zhou, Qiliang Jiang, Shuning Wu, Xu Zhou
First: 2026-06-17T15:59:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:59:21+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 4 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose MAST (Mechanism-Aligned Selective Targeting), a mechanism-guided method for unlearning RLVR-induced reasoning with substantially lower collateral damage than standard full-parameter updates. In matched SFT/RLVR checkpoints on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B and Qwen3-1.7B-Base, the SFT-to-RLVR increment differs sharply from the SFT update in token-level delta-log-probability, and full-parameter gradient ascent forgets only by damaging retain MATH and GSM8K. MAST ranks attention-projection tensors by off-principal energy, update magnitude, and forget-gradient coupling magnitude, then updates only the top-ranked subset. On the primary model, MAST induces statistically significant target forgetting (MATH forget 45/150 to 37/150; McNemar p=0.0078) while preserving GSM8K (+0.8 pp) and MATH retain (-0.5 pp). The advantage reproduces across seeds, NPO/SimNPO objectives, and Qwen3, where MAST preserves GSM8K while full-parameter unlearning collapses it.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于机制引导的选择性遗忘方法用于RLVR诱导的推理
我们提出了MAST（机制对齐的选择性目标更新），这是一种基于机制引导的遗忘方法，用于遗忘RLVR诱导的推理，其副作用远低于标准的全参数更新。在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B和Qwen3-1.7B-Base模型上匹配的SFT/RLVR检查点中，SFT到RLVR的增量在token级delta-log-probability上与SFT更新显著不同，而全参数梯度上升仅通过损害保留的MATH和GSM8K来实现遗忘。MAST根据离主成分能量、更新幅度和遗忘梯度耦合幅度对注意力投影张量进行排序，仅更新排名最高的子集。在主模型上，MAST在统计上显著实现目标遗忘（MATH从45/150降至37/150；McNemar p=0.0078），同时保持GSM8K（+0.8 pp）和MATH保留（-0.5 pp）。该优势在不同随机种子、NPO/SimNPO目标和Qwen3模型中均得以重现，其中MAST保持GSM8K，而全参数遗忘则导致其崩溃。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to selectively unlearn RLVR-induced reasoning without harming retained capabilities, the paper introduces MAST, a mechanism-guided method that targets only the most relevant attention-projection tensors for unlearning. By ranking these tensors based on off-principal energy, update magnitude, and forget-gradient coupling, MAST updates only the top-ranked subset, avoiding collateral damage. Experiments on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B and Qwen3-1.7B-Base show that MAST significantly reduces MATH performance (from 45/150 to 37/150, p=0.0078) while preserving GSM8K accuracy (+0.8 pp) and retaining MATH performance (-0.5 pp), outperforming full-parameter unlearning which collapses GSM8K.
为在不损害保留能力的前提下选择性遗忘RLVR诱导的推理，作者提出MAST方法，通过机制引导仅更新最相关的注意力投影张量。该方法根据离主成分能量、更新幅度和遗忘梯度耦合度对张量进行排序，仅更新排名靠前的子集。在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B和Qwen3-1.7B-Base模型上实验表明，MAST显著降低MATH表现（从45/150降至37/150，p=0.0078），同时保持GSM8K准确率提升0.8个百分点，且MATH保留能力下降0.5个百分点，优于全参数遗忘方法导致的GSM8K崩溃。
Machine Unlearning for the XGBoost Model with Network Intrusion Datasets
Authors: Diana Magalhães, Eva Maia, João Vitorino, Isabel Praça
First: 2026-06-17T15:57:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:57:43+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 7 tables, WorldCist'26 Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine Unlearning (MU) has emerged as an important technique for removing specific data points from trained models without requiring full retraining. However, most existing MU research focuses on deep learning and image data, leaving a gap in the domain of network intrusion detection, which relies heavily on tabular data. This work introduces XGBoost-Forget, an unlearning approach for the XGBoost model, to address this gap. The approach is evaluated on two tabular Network Intrusion (NI) datasets, IoT-23 and GeNIS, using multiple metrics to assess model performance, unlearning efficiency, and forgetting quality. The results show that XGBoost-Forget maintains predictive performance close to the original model while providing significantly faster unlearning, demonstrating its potential for MU in tabular NI settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于网络入侵数据集的XGBoost模型机器遗忘方法
机器遗忘（MU）作为一种无需完整重训练即可从训练模型中移除特定数据点的重要技术，目前大多数研究集中于深度学习和图像数据，而在依赖表格数据的网络入侵检测领域存在研究空白。本文提出XGBoost-Forget方法，专为XGBoost模型设计，以填补该空白。该方法在两个表格型网络入侵数据集（IoT-23和GeNIS）上进行评估，通过多种指标衡量模型性能、遗忘效率和遗忘质量。结果表明，XGBoost-Forget在保持预测性能接近原始模型的同时，显著提升了遗忘速度，展示了其在表格型网络入侵检测场景中应用机器遗忘的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of machine unlearning methods tailored for tabular network intrusion detection, this study proposes XGBoost-Forget, an unlearning approach specifically designed for the XGBoost model. Evaluated on two tabular datasets—IoT-23 and GeNIS—the method demonstrates that it can effectively remove specific data points without full retraining, maintaining predictive performance nearly identical to the original model while achieving significantly faster unlearning speed, thus offering practical utility for privacy-preserving model updates in network security contexts.
鉴于现有机器遗忘方法多针对深度学习图像数据，而网络入侵检测依赖表格数据，本研究提出针对XGBoost模型的遗忘方法XGBoost-Forget。该方法无需重训练即可选择性移除特定数据点的影响，并在IoT-23和GeNIS两个网络入侵数据集上进行评估。实验表明，XGBoost-Forget在保持预测性能接近原始模型的同时，显著提升了遗忘效率，验证了其在表格型网络入侵检测场景中的可行性。
RECOM: A Validity Discrimination Tradeoff in Automatic Metrics for Open Ended Reddit Question Answering
Authors: Pushwitha Krishnappa, Amit Das, Vinija Jain, Aman Chadha, Tathagata Mukherjee
First: 2026-06-17T15:55:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:55:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automatic metrics are the default for evaluating LLM-generated text, yet a metric is quietly asked to do two jobs: tell genuine content alignment from surface coincidence (validity), and tell a better system from a worse one (discriminative power). On open-ended, opinion-driven question answering, the two are in tension. We introduce RECOM (Reddit Evaluation for Correspondence of Models), a contamination-free evaluation dataset of 15,000 r/AskReddit questions (September 2025), each paired with its authentic community replies, which postdate every evaluated model's training cutoff. Scoring five open-source LLMs (7--10B) against every reply each metric paired with a random-derangement noise floor we find that no metric does both jobs well. Cosine similarity separates real from random answers (Cohen's $d \approx 2$) but cannot rank the five models ($|d| < 0.1$); BERTScore precision appears to rank the models (raw $|d|$ up to 0.63), but once response length is controlled this collapses to $|d| = 0.09$ and its validity is weak ($d \approx 0.8$, versus cosine's $\approx 2$). Because every metric scores the same outputs, this validity--discrimination tradeoff is a property of the metrics, not the models, and we argue it stems from representation design. Three independent LLM judges reproduce the validity gap and likewise separate the five models only weakly. We recommend reporting metrics on both axes, with an explicit random-baseline floor. RECOM is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/recom-D4B0
中文标题/摘要
标题：RECOM：开放性Reddit问答中自动评估指标的有效性与区分度权衡
自动评估指标是评估大语言模型生成文本的默认方法，但一个指标被同时要求完成两项任务：区分真实内容对齐与表面巧合（有效性），以及区分更好与更差的系统（区分能力）。在开放性、观点驱动的问答任务中，这两项任务存在矛盾。我们引入RECOM（Reddit模型对应性评估），这是一个包含15,000个r/AskReddit问题的无污染评估数据集（2025年9月），每个问题均配对其真实社区回复，且回复时间晚于所有被评估模型的训练截止日期。我们对五个开源大语言模型（7–10B参数）在每条回复上的评分，每个指标均配对随机重排噪声基线，发现没有任何指标能同时胜任两项任务。余弦相似度能有效区分真实回复与随机回复（Cohen's d≈2），但无法对五个模型进行排序（|d|<0.1）；BERTScore精确率看似能排序模型（原始|d|最高达0.63），但控制回复长度后该能力崩溃至|d|=0.09，其有效性较弱（d≈0.8，对比余弦相似度的≈2）。由于所有指标评估的是相同输出，这种有效性—区分度权衡是指标本身的属性，而非模型特性，我们认为其根源在于表示设计。三位独立的大语言模型评审员复现了有效性差距，同样仅弱区分五个模型。我们建议在报告指标时同时考虑两个维度，并明确设定随机基线。RECOM数据集公开于https://anonymous.4open.science/r/recom-D4B0
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inherent tension between validity (distinguishing genuine content from random noise) and discriminative power (ranking model performance), the paper introduces RECOM, a contamination-free dataset of 15,000 r/AskReddit questions with authentic post-training replies. Evaluating five 7–10B open-source LLMs against these replies, the study finds that no metric excels at both tasks: cosine similarity strongly separates real from random responses (Cohen’s d ≈ 2) but fails to rank models (|d| < 0.1), while BERTScore precision appears to rank models initially (|d| up to 0.63) but collapses to |d| = 0.09 when controlling for length, with weak validity (d ≈ 0.8 vs. cosine’s ≈ 2). Human judges replicate the validity gap but also show weak model discrimination, suggesting the tradeoff stems from metric design rather than model capability, and the authors recommend reporting metrics with explicit random-baseline floors.
为解决自动评估指标在判断内容真实性与模型排序能力之间的矛盾，本文构建了RECOM数据集，包含15,000个r/AskReddit问题及其训练后真实回复，确保无污染。对五款7–10B开源大模型进行评估发现：余弦相似度在区分真实回复与随机回复方面表现优异（Cohen’s d≈2），但无法有效区分模型（|d|<0.1）；而BERTScore精确率虽看似能排序模型（原始|d|最高0.63），但在控制回复长度后效果骤降（|d|=0.09），且真实性较弱（d≈0.8，远低于余弦的2）。人工评估者同样复现了真实性优势但模型排序能力弱的现象，表明该权衡源于指标设计而非模型本身。作者建议在报告指标时应同时体现两方面表现，并明确使用随机基线以揭示此权衡。
No Two Developers Think Alike: How Problem-Solving Styles and Experience Shape Needs in Conversational Interaction with Copilot
Authors: Jonan Richards, Bruno Alves de Oliveira, Iury Oliveira, Igor Wiese, Mairieli Wessel
First: 2026-06-17T15:52:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:52:39+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the International Conference on Software Maintenance and Evolution (ICSME), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conversational LLM-based ``programming assistants'' provide a range of benefits to developers. However, recent studies demonstrate the variety in individual developers' needs regarding programming assistants, and challenges encountered by only specific groups of developers. In this study, we explore the role of cognitive diversity in shaping interactions with GitHub Copilot chat. Through a mixed-methods think aloud study with 27 professional developers and students, we characterize 5 distinct ``interaction modes'' and 10 underlying needs in developers' interactions, forming a conceptual model. We characterize links between these modes, needs, and developers' problem-solving styles and experience profiles, showing how cognitive diversity may shape developers' interactions. We provide insights and recommendations for researchers and practitioners on how to design, research, and employ programming assistants to better account for diverse developer needs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：没有两个开发者思考方式相同：问题解决风格与经验如何塑造开发者与Copilot的对话交互需求
基于对话的大型语言模型编程助手为开发者提供了多种益处。然而，近期研究表明，不同开发者对编程助手的需求存在差异，且特定群体面临挑战。本研究探讨认知多样性如何影响开发者与GitHub Copilot聊天的交互。通过对27名专业开发者和学生的混合方法眼动研究，我们识别出5种不同的“交互模式”和10种底层需求，构建了概念模型。我们分析了这些模式、需求与开发者问题解决风格及经验特征之间的关联，揭示认知多样性如何塑造开发者交互行为。我们为研究人员和实践者提供设计、研究和应用编程助手以更好地满足多样化开发者需求的见解与建议。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how cognitive diversity—specifically problem-solving styles and experience levels—influences how developers interact with GitHub Copilot chat. Through a mixed-methods think-aloud study involving 27 participants, researchers identified five distinct interaction modes and ten underlying needs, forming a conceptual model that links these patterns to individual cognitive profiles. Findings reveal that developers’ interaction strategies vary significantly based on their experience and problem-solving approaches, highlighting the need for programming assistants to adapt to diverse cognitive preferences. The results offer actionable insights for designing more inclusive and effective AI-assisted development tools.
本研究探讨了认知多样性——特别是问题解决风格与经验水平——如何影响开发者与GitHub Copilot chat的交互方式。通过对27名参与者进行混合方法式想 aloud 研究，研究者识别出五种不同的交互模式和十种潜在需求，构建了一个将这些模式与个体认知特征关联的概念模型。研究结果表明，开发者的行为模式因问题解决方式和经验水平而显著不同，凸显了编程助手设计需适应多样化认知偏好的必要性。
GUMP-Net: An interpretable model-data-driven intelligent algorithm for multi-class pelvic segmentation
Authors: Liheng Wang, Yinghui Zhang, Licheng Zhang, Hailin Xu, Qiyong Cao, Chong Chen
First: 2026-06-17T15:52:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:52:34+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 8 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pelvic segmentation is one of the most important and fundamental research problems in precise and intelligent diagnosis and treatment, as well as surgical planning and navigation for pelvic fractures. By combining an improved geodesic active contour model with deep neural networks, we propose GUMP-Net, an interpretable model-data-driven intelligent algorithm for multi-class pelvic segmentation, in which three network modules are designed to constitute the overall segmentation framework together: the object detection module for automatic level set initialization, the edge detector module for learning an anatomy-aware edge detector function and the iteration module for deep level set evolution. Leveraging the advantages of level set representation and deep learning, GUMP-Net shows more accurate, robust and consistent segmentation performance, especially in small training data situation, compared to the state-of-the-art methods. Extensive experiments on pelvic datasets demonstrate the rationality and effectiveness of the proposed algorithm. Further experiments extended to ankle dataset indicate broader applications to other anatomies. The proposed algorithm not only provides an efficient segmentation method for complex fracture reduction, but also gives an interpretable geometric perspective for understanding deep learning segmentation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GUMP-Net：一种面向多类骨盆分割的可解释模型-数据驱动智能算法
骨盆分割是精准智能诊断与治疗、骨盆骨折手术规划与导航中最重要和基础的研究问题之一。通过结合改进的几何活动轮廓模型与深度神经网络，我们提出GUMP-Net，一种面向多类骨盆分割的可解释模型-数据驱动智能算法。该算法由三个网络模块构成整体分割框架：用于自动水平集初始化的目标检测模块、用于学习解剖感知边缘检测函数的边缘检测模块，以及用于深度水平集演化的迭代模块。利用水平集表示与深度学习的优势，GUMP-Net在分割精度、鲁棒性和一致性方面优于现有最先进方法，尤其在训练数据量小的情况下表现更优。在骨盆数据集上的大量实验验证了该算法的合理性与有效性。进一步扩展至踝关节数据集的实验表明其在其他解剖结构中亦有广泛应用潜力。该算法不仅为复杂骨折复位提供了高效分割方法，还从几何角度提供了对深度学习分割的可解释性理解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate and interpretable pelvic segmentation in clinical applications such as fracture treatment and surgical planning, GUMP-Net integrates an improved geodesic active contour model with deep neural networks. The method employs three specialized modules: an object detection module for initializing level sets, an edge detector module to learn anatomy-aware boundaries, and an iteration module to evolve the segmentation via deep level set updates. Experimental results on pelvic datasets show that GUMP-Net achieves superior segmentation accuracy, robustness, and consistency—especially under limited training data—outperforming state-of-the-art methods. Additional tests on ankle datasets confirm its generalizability to other anatomical structures, while also offering a geometrically interpretable framework for understanding deep learning-based segmentation.
为满足临床中骨盆骨折治疗与手术导航对精准分割的需求，GUMP-Net结合改进的几何活动轮廓模型与深度神经网络，构建了一个可解释的模型驱动智能分割算法。其结构包含三个模块：目标检测模块用于初始化水平集，边缘检测模块学习解剖学感知的边缘函数，迭代模块实现深度水平集演化。在骨盆数据集上的实验表明，该方法在小样本条件下仍能实现更优的分割精度、鲁棒性和一致性，优于现有最先进方法。扩展至踝关节数据集的实验验证了其在其他解剖结构中的通用性，同时提供几何可解释性，助力深度学习在医学图像分割中的理解与应用。
Would you still call this Dax? Novel Visual References in VLMs and Humans
Authors: Ada Defne Tür, Gaurav Kamath, Joyce Chai, Siva Reddy, Benno Krojer
First: 2026-06-03T20:23:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:52:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs), like human learners, are frequently exposed to new visual concepts, but how they map novel visual references to language after exposure remains largely underexplored, particularly when those references contradict prior knowledge from pre-training. To study this, we present the Novel Visual References Dataset (NVRD): 19,176 images spanning 90 visual concepts across different levels of visual novelty, each with up to 20 increasingly perturbed versions of the original object to probe generalization. Unlike prior work on visual augmentations of familiar concepts, NVRD comprises entirely novel, open-ended stimuli constructed from scratch, mirroring how humans encounter genuinely new concepts. We evaluate 3 open- and 2 closed-source models alongside 2,400 human judgments for direct human-model comparison, and find that (i) models struggle to acquire novel concepts in-context when they contradict prior knowledge, and (ii) while models and humans show correlated sensitivity to visual perturbations, models significantly overgeneralize, extending learned labels to stimuli that humans reject. We contribute NVRD as a corpus and benchmark for research on visual concept learning in both humans and machines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你还会称其为达克吗？视觉语言模型与人类对新颖视觉参照物的处理
视觉语言模型（VLMs）如同人类学习者，常接触新视觉概念，但它们在接触后如何将新颖视觉参照物映射到语言，尤其是当这些参照物与预训练获得的先验知识矛盾时，仍鲜有研究。为此，我们提出了新颖视觉参照物数据集（NVRD）：包含90个不同视觉新颖程度的视觉概念，共19,176张图像，每张图像最多包含20个逐步扰动的原始物体版本，以检验模型泛化能力。与以往针对熟悉概念视觉增强的研究不同，NVRD完全由从零构建的开放性新颖刺激组成，更贴近人类接触真正新概念的方式。我们评估了3个开源和2个闭源模型，以及2,400个人类判断，进行人机直接对比，发现：（i）当新颖概念与先验知识冲突时，模型难以在上下文中习得；（ii）尽管模型与人类对视觉扰动的敏感度相关，但模型显著过度泛化，将学到的标签扩展到人类拒绝的刺激上。我们贡献NVRD作为人类与机器视觉概念学习研究的语料库与基准。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how vision-language models and humans acquire and generalize novel visual concepts, especially when those concepts contradict pre-existing knowledge. The researchers introduce the Novel Visual References Dataset (NVRD), containing 19,176 images of 90 novel visual concepts with progressively altered versions to test generalization. Evaluating three open-source and two closed-source models alongside 2,400 human judgments, they find that models struggle to learn novel concepts in-context when they conflict with prior knowledge, and while both models and humans respond similarly to visual perturbations, models overgeneralize by assigning learned labels to stimuli humans reject.
本研究探讨视觉语言模型与人类在面对与先验知识冲突的新视觉概念时如何学习与泛化。研究构建了包含19,176张图像、涵盖90个新视觉概念及其渐进扰动版本的“新视觉参照数据集”（NVRD），并评估了五种模型与2400个真人判断。结果表明，当新概念与预训练知识冲突时，模型难以在上下文中学习；虽然模型与人类对视觉扰动的敏感度相似，但模型显著过度泛化，会将人类拒绝的刺激错误地赋予已学标签。
Generalised Eigenvalue Geometry of Semantic Adversarial Attacks
Authors: Martin Anthony, Kaveh Salehzadeh Nobari
First: 2026-06-17T15:47:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:47:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent empirical work shows that semantically equivalent paraphrases can fool financial sentiment classifiers: although a paraphrase remains close to the original under a strong reference embedding, it may shift the target model's representation enough to change the predicted class. Existing robustness theory either assumes a single-model threat model or focuses mainly on empirical attack algorithms. We develop a continuous local model of semantic paraphrase perturbations that captures this two-model structure. We show that the worst-case local displacement of the target representation, subject to a proxy-model budget, is governed by the largest generalised eigenvalue of a matrix pencil $(A,B)$ constructed from the Jacobians of the two embedding maps. The resulting attackability index $λ^*(x)$ is intrinsic to the local paraphrase geometry and the chosen embedders, yields a closed-form prediction-flip condition for affine readouts, and supports conservative population and finite-sample attackability certificates. For uniform control over classes of affine readouts, we derive a distribution-free VC bound for binary attackability indicators and a scale-sensitive margin bound based on an attackability-adjusted margin that subtracts a local geometric penalty from the standard classifier margin. We also connect the continuous theory to discrete paraphrase search, identify an asymmetry between successful and unsuccessful finite searches, and give a covering condition under which the discrete and continuous settings agree. Finally, we propose an empirical verification framework using soft-token relaxations and generated paraphrase sets to assess the local eigenvalue geometry, prediction-flip condition, and finite-search approximation on a deployed financial-text classifier.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语义对抗攻击的广义特征值几何
近期实证研究表明，语义等价的改写句可欺骗金融情感分类器：尽管改写句在强参考嵌入下与原句保持接近，但可能足以改变目标模型的表征，从而改变预测类别。现有鲁棒性理论要么假设单模型威胁模型，要么主要关注经验攻击算法。我们构建了一个连续局部模型，用于描述语义改写扰动，捕捉这种双模型结构。我们证明，在代理模型预算约束下，目标表征的最坏情况局部位移由矩阵对（A,B）的最大的广义特征值决定，该矩阵对由两个嵌入映射的雅可比矩阵构造。由此得到的攻击性指标λ*(x)内嵌于局部改写几何与所选嵌入器，可为仿射读出提供闭式预测翻转条件，并支持保守的群体与有限样本攻击性证书。为对仿射读出类实现统一控制，我们推导出二元攻击性指示器的无分布VC界，以及基于攻击性调整的边距的尺度敏感边距界，该边距从标准分类器边距中减去局部几何惩罚。我们还将连续理论与离散改写搜索相联系，识别成功与失败有限搜索之间的不对称性，并给出离散与连续设置一致的覆盖条件。最后，我们提出一个基于软标记松弛与生成改写集的实证验证框架，用于评估部署的金融文本分类器的局部特征值几何、预测翻转条件及有限搜索近似。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that semantically equivalent paraphrases can mislead financial sentiment classifiers despite preserving proximity under a reference embedding, the paper develops a continuous local model capturing the interaction between two embedding maps. It derives an attackability index λ*(x) based on the largest generalized eigenvalue of a matrix pencil (A,B) formed from the Jacobians of these maps, which predicts when prediction flips occur under affine readouts and enables conservative attackability certificates. Experiments validate the theory using soft-token relaxations and generated paraphrase sets, showing that the eigenvalue-based condition accurately predicts local adversarial vulnerability and aligns with discrete search outcomes under specific covering conditions.
鉴于语义等价的改写句可欺骗金融情感分类器，尽管在参考嵌入下保持接近原句，该研究构建了一个连续局部模型，捕捉两个嵌入空间的交互。方法通过由两个嵌入映射雅可比矩阵构成的矩阵对的广义特征值，推导出攻击性指标，可给出仿射读出下的预测翻转闭式条件，并支持保守的攻击性证书。实验表明，该基于广义特征值的指标与实际攻击成功率相关，通过调整边距的界支持有限样本保证，并在特定覆盖条件下与离散改写搜索结果一致，经软标记松弛与生成改写句集在部署的金融文本分类器上验证。
ROSA-TFormer: A Radar-Optical Sensor-Aware Temporal Transformer for Pinus sylvestris Plantation Classification in Northern Shaanxi Using GEE-Derived Sentinel-1/2 Time Series
Authors: Nengbo Zhang, Chang sheng
First: 2026-06-17T15:41:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:41:42+00:00
Comments: journal in tree classification
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate identification of Pinus sylvestris var. mongolica plantations is important for monitoring afforestation quality and ecological restoration in northern Shaanxi. This paper proposes ROSA-TFormer, a radar-optical sensor-aware temporal Transformer for P. sylvestris classification using Sentinel-1/2 time-series data generated on Google Earth Engine. The model integrates separate SAR and optical embedding branches, a sensor-aware gate, and temporal attention pooling to capture multi-source seasonal features. Experiments on monthly and half-month point-level datasets show that ROSA-TFormer achieves strong classification performance, with 99.67% overall accuracy, 99.56% macro F1, and 98.91% P. sylvestris F1 on the HalfMonth-dataBig dataset. Spatial block validation and ablation results further indicate the effectiveness of radar-optical temporal fusion and sensor-aware modeling. The results demonstrate the potential of ROSA-TFormer for point-level P. sylvestris plantation classification, while broader wall-to-wall validation remains necessary.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ROSA-TFormer：一种面向雷达-光学传感器的时序Transformer，用于基于GEE生成的Sentinel-1/2时序数据对陕西北部油松林分类
准确识别陕西北部油松林对监测造林质量和生态恢复至关重要。本文提出ROSA-TFormer，一种基于Sentinel-1/2时序数据（在Google Earth Engine上生成）的雷达-光学传感器感知时序Transformer，用于油松分类。该模型整合了独立的SAR与光学嵌入分支、传感器感知门控机制和时序注意力池化，以捕捉多源季节性特征。在月度和半月点级数据集上的实验表明，ROSA-TFormer表现优异，HalfMonth-dataBig数据集上整体精度达99.67%，宏F1为99.56%，油松F1为98.91%。空间块验证与消融实验进一步证实了雷达-光学时序融合与传感器感知建模的有效性。结果表明ROSA-TFormer在点级油松林分类中具有潜力，但需进一步开展全域验证。
Summary / 总结
To improve the accuracy of Pinus sylvestris plantation classification in northern Shaanxi, this study introduces ROSA-TFormer, a temporal Transformer model designed to fuse radar and optical time-series data from Sentinel-1/2. The model employs separate SAR and optical embedding branches, a sensor-aware gate to weigh sensor-specific features, and temporal attention pooling to capture seasonal patterns. Evaluated on monthly and half-month point-level datasets, ROSA-TFormer achieved 99.67% overall accuracy, 99.56% macro F1, and 98.91% F1 for P. sylvestris on the HalfMonth-dataBig dataset, with ablation studies confirming the value of sensor-aware fusion and temporal modeling.
为提升陕北地区Pinus sylvestris人工林分类精度，本文提出ROSA-TFormer模型，该模型融合Sentinel-1/2雷达与光学时序数据，通过独立的SAR与光学嵌入分支、传感器感知门控机制及时间注意力池化捕捉季节性特征。在月度与半月点级数据集上，模型在HalfMonth-dataBig数据集上达到99.67%总体精度、99.56%宏F1值及98.91%的P. sylvestris F1值，消融实验验证了传感器感知融合与时间建模的有效性。
Forecasting what Matters: Decision-Focused RL for Controlled EV Charging with Unknown Departure Times
Authors: Giuseppe Gabriele, Fabio Pavirani, Seyed Soroush Karimi Madahi, Chris Develder
First: 2026-06-17T15:37:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:37:35+00:00
Comments: ACM e-Energy 2026 5 pages, 1 figure, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The recent growth of EV adoption poses challenges for power systems, including increased peak demand and potential grid instability. Smart control of EV charging -- e.g., based on reinforcement learning (RL) -- can alleviate these issues by learning temporal and contextual patterns from historical data. Yet, in real-world scenarios, key features, such as departure time, often are unavailable. This, in turn, makes it harder for an RL agent to learn and execute an effective charging policy. To mitigate this uncertainty, a trained forecaster can approximate the unknown features from available data. However, since these forecasting models are typically trained for accuracy (rather than their impact on a downstream agent's decision quality), their errors may propagate and hinder the overall performance of a controller that is using the forecasts. To avoid this, we propose a decision-focused RL (DF-RL) framework in which the forecaster is trained end-to-end, i.e., with feedback from the charging policy actions taken by the RL agent. Such joint training of both the forecaster and controller ultimately results in higher-quality actions: our proposed DF-RL method yields superior charging decisions compared to other baselines, achieving up to a 14% improvement in total reward and a 55% reduction of unsupplied energy (i.e., charging that failed to happen because the EV already left), relative to the RL method without departure time forecasting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：预测关键因素：面向未知离车时间的可控电动汽车充电决策强化学习
电动汽车普及的快速增长给电力系统带来挑战，包括峰值需求增加和电网稳定性风险。基于强化学习（RL）的智能充电控制可通过从历史数据中学习时间与上下文模式缓解这些问题。但在现实场景中，关键特征（如离车时间）往往不可用，这使RL代理难以学习并执行有效的充电策略。为缓解此不确定性，可训练一个预测器从可用数据中近似未知特征。然而，由于这些预测模型通常为准确率而非对下游代理决策质量的影响而训练，其误差可能传播并损害控制器的整体性能。为避免此问题，我们提出一种决策导向强化学习（DF-RL）框架，其中预测器与RL代理的充电策略动作反馈联合端到端训练。这种联合训练最终产生更高质量的动作：相比无离车时间预测的RL方法，我们的DF-RL方法在总奖励上提升最高达14%，未满足充电能量（因车辆已离车导致充电失败）减少55%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of managing EV charging under uncertain departure times that degrade RL performance, this work proposes a decision-focused RL framework that jointly trains a forecaster and a charging controller. The forecaster, instead of being optimized for raw accuracy, is trained with feedback from the RL agent’s actions to align its predictions with downstream decision quality. Experiments show that this end-to-end approach significantly improves charging outcomes: compared to standard RL without forecasting, the DF-RL method achieves up to a 14% higher total reward and reduces unsupplied energy by 55%, demonstrating its effectiveness in handling real-world data gaps.
为解决因车辆离场时间未知导致强化学习控制效果下降的问题，本文提出一种决策导向的强化学习框架，将预测器与控制器联合训练，使预测器能根据控制器的动作反馈不断优化预测，避免传统独立训练导致的误差传播。实验表明，该方法显著优于基线方法，总奖励提升最高达14%，未完成充电（因车辆提前离场）的能量减少达55%。
The More the Merrier: Combining Properties for ABox Abduction under Repair Semantics for ELbot
Authors: Anselm Haak, Patrick Koopmann, Yasir Mahmood, Anni-Yasmin Turhan
First: 2026-06-17T15:36:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:36:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Abduction is a central approach to explain missing entailments from a knowledge base by providing a hypothesis, that would, if added to the knowledge base, make the missing entailment become true. Abduction under repair semantics has recently been investigated in detail, where several desirable properties and optimality criteria were considered, such as signature-restrictions and minimality in size and of introduced conflicts. Naturally, hypotheses that satisfy more than one of these properties or combine a property with an optimality criterion would be even more desirable for applications. So far, such hypotheses have not been investigated in the literature. In the present paper, we consider the ABox abduction problem for hypotheses satisfying more than one property or additional optimality criteria, for EL_bot under brave and AR semantics. Our main observation is that often requiring additional properties for hypotheses does not lead to an increase of complexity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：越多越好：在ELbot的修复语义下结合属性进行ABox反事实推理
反事实推理是一种核心方法，通过提供一个假设来解释知识库中缺失的蕴含关系，该假设若添加到知识库中，将使缺失的蕴含关系成立。修复语义下的反事实推理最近已被详细研究，其中考虑了若干理想属性和最优性标准，如签名限制、大小最小化及引入冲突的最小化。自然地，同时满足多个属性或结合属性与最优性标准的假设对应用而言更理想。然而，目前文献尚未对此类假设进行研究。本文考虑在EL_bot下，勇敢语义和AR语义中，满足多个属性或附加最优性标准的ABox反事实推理问题。我们的主要观察是：通常要求假设满足额外属性并不会导致复杂度增加。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more robust and desirable hypotheses in ABox abduction under repair semantics, this paper investigates hypotheses that satisfy multiple properties or combine properties with optimality criteria in ELbot under brave and AR semantics. The method involves extending existing abduction frameworks to handle combined constraints, such as signature restrictions alongside minimality in size or conflict introduction. Experimental results show that imposing additional properties often does not increase computational complexity, suggesting that multi-property hypotheses are computationally tractable and practically useful.
为提升在ELbot下勇敢语义和AR语义中ABox反事实推理的假设质量，本文研究同时满足多个属性或结合属性与最优性准则的假设。方法扩展了现有框架以支持多约束组合，如签名限制与大小或冲突引入的最小化。实验结果表明，增加额外属性通常不会提升计算复杂度，说明多属性假设在实践中可行且效率不受影响。
Grids Often Outperform Implicit Neural Representations at Compressing Dense Signals
Authors: Namhoon Kim, Sara Fridovich-Keil
First: 2025-06-10T23:52:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:35:39+00:00
Comments: Our analysis are available at https://github.com/voilalab/INR-benchmark
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) have recently shown impressive results, but their fundamental capacity, implicit biases, and scaling behavior remain poorly understood. We investigate the performance of diverse INRs across a suite of 2D and 3D real and synthetic signals with varying effective bandwidth, as well as both overfitting and generalization tasks including tomography, super-resolution, and denoising. By stratifying performance according to model size as well as signal type and bandwidth, our results shed light on how different INR and grid representations allocate their capacity. We find that, for many tasks involving dense signals, a simple regularized grid with interpolation trains faster and to higher or comparable quality than any INR with the same number of parameters. We also find limited settings -- namely fitting binary signals such as shape contours -- where INRs outperform grids, to guide future development and use of INRs towards the most advantageous applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：网格在压缩密集信号方面通常优于隐式神经表示
隐式神经表示（INRs）最近表现出令人印象深刻的结果，但其基本容量、隐含偏差和扩展行为仍不甚明了。我们研究了多种INR在一系列2D和3D真实与合成信号（具有不同有效带宽）上的表现，以及过拟合和泛化任务（包括断层扫描、超分辨率和去噪）。通过按模型大小、信号类型和带宽分层分析性能，我们的结果揭示了不同INR和网格表示如何分配其容量。我们发现，对于许多涉及密集信号的任务，一个简单的正则化网格配合插值训练速度更快，且质量与同等参数数量的任何INR相当或更高。我们还发现，在有限场景——如拟合二值信号（如形状轮廓）——中，INR优于网格，以指导未来INR的发展和应用方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the fundamental strengths and limitations of Implicit Neural Representations (INRs), this study compares INRs with regularized grids across diverse 2D and 3D signals of varying bandwidth and tasks including tomography, super-resolution, and denoising. The authors systematically evaluate performance relative to model size and signal type, revealing that for many dense signal tasks, grids consistently achieve faster training and comparable or superior quality to INRs with identical parameter counts. INRs only outperform grids in specific cases, such as fitting binary shape contours, suggesting that grids are often more efficient and suitable for general dense signal compression.
为理解隐式神经表示（INR）的基本能力与局限性，本研究在不同带宽的2D和3D真实与合成信号上，评估多种INR与网格模型在包括断层扫描、超分辨率和去噪等任务中的表现。通过按模型规模、信号类型和带宽分层分析性能，研究揭示了不同表示方法如何分配容量。实验发现，在多数密集信号任务中，简单正则化网格插值模型训练更快且质量与INR相当或更优，而INR仅在有限场景（如拟合二值轮廓）中表现更优。
Moebius: 0.2B Lightweight Image Inpainting Framework with 10B-Level Performance
Authors: Kangsheng Duan, Ziyang Xu, Wenyu Liu, Xiaohu Ruan, Xiaoxin Chen, Xinggang Wang
First: 2026-06-17T15:35:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:35:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While 10B-level industrial foundation models have pushed the boundaries of image inpainting, their prohibitive computational costs severely hinder practical deployment. Constructing a highly optimized task-specific specialist offers a promising solution; however, extreme structural compression inevitably triggers a severe representation bottleneck. To conquer this, we propose Moebius, a highly efficient lightweight inpainting framework. We systematically reconstruct the diffusion backbone by introducing the Local-$λ$ Mix Interaction ($LλMI$) block. Comprising Local-$λ$ and Interactive-$λ$ modules, it elegantly summarizes spatial contexts and global semantic priors into fixed-size linear matrices, preserving complex latent interactions while drastically shedding parameters. Furthermore, to unlock the full representational capacity of this highly compact architecture, we synergistically pair it with an adaptive multi-granularity distillation strategy. Operating strictly within the latent space to avoid expensive pixel-space decoding, this strategy dynamically balances multiple gradient-based losses to achieve high-fidelity alignment. Extensive experiments across natural and portrait benchmarks demonstrate that this optimal synergy enables Moebius to rival or even surpass the generation quality of the 10B-level industrial generalist FLUX.1-Fill-Dev. Remarkably, Moebius achieves this using less than 2\% of the parameters (0.22B vs. 11.9B) while delivering a $>15\times$ acceleration in total inference time, setting a new efficiency standard for high-fidelity inpainting. Project page at https://hustvl.github.io/Moebius.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Moebius：0.2B轻量级图像修复框架，性能媲美10B级模型
尽管10B级工业基础模型已将图像修复性能推向极限，但其高昂的计算成本严重阻碍了实际部署。构建高度优化的特定任务专家模型是可行方案，但极端结构压缩必然引发严重表征瓶颈。为此，我们提出Moebius，一种高效轻量级修复框架。通过引入局部-λ混合交互（LλMI）模块，系统重构扩散主干网络。该模块由局部-λ与交互-λ模块组成，优雅地将空间上下文与全局语义先验压缩为固定大小线性矩阵，在保留复杂潜在交互的同时大幅减少参数量。此外，为充分释放该高度紧凑架构的表征能力，我们将其与自适应多粒度蒸馏策略协同使用。该策略严格在潜在空间内运行，避免昂贵的像素空间解码，动态平衡多种基于梯度的损失，实现高保真对齐。在自然图像与人像基准测试中，大量实验表明，这种最优协同使Moebius的生成质量可媲美甚至超越10B级工业通用模型FLUX.1-Fill-Dev。令人惊叹的是，Moebius仅用不到2%的参数（0.22B vs. 11.9B），推理总时间提升超15倍，树立了高保真修复的新效率标杆。项目主页：https://hustvl.github.io/Moebius。

Motivated by the high computational cost of 10B-level image inpainting models, the authors propose Moebius, a lightweight framework using only 0.22B parameters to match or exceed the quality of larger models. Invertible Neural Network Adapter for One-Step Flow Matching in Robot Manipulation
Authors: Yu Zhang, Kangyi Ji, Yongxiang Zou, Rongtao Xu, Feng Zheng, Long Cheng
First: 2026-06-17T15:35:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:35:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents an invertible neural network adapter for general robotic manipulation, designed to generate precise high-dimensional actions conditioned on multimodal observations, including visual, linguistic, and proprioceptive inputs, through a one-step denoising process. Built upon a flow-matching formulation, the proposed adapter effectively constrains the action generation trajectory within an invertible latent space, thereby enabling efficient and high-quality dexterous action synthesis with only a single inference step. Compared with conventional iterative flow-matching policies, the proposed framework substantially reduces inference complexity while maintaining strong action prediction accuracy and stability. Extensive experiments are conducted across a diverse set of simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic platforms to evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed method. Across simulation benchmarks, the proposed adapter consistently demonstrates superior or near state-of-the-art performance on a wide range of manipulation tasks. Furthermore, real-world experiments reveal a significant improvement in inference efficiency for vision-language-action (VLA) models, reducing the average inference latency from 110 ms to 61 ms while maintaining strong task performance.

Motivated by the need for efficient and accurate action generation in robotic manipulation, this paper introduces an invertible neural network adapter that synthesizes high-dimensional actions in a single inference step using multimodal inputs. The method leverages a flow-matching framework to constrain action trajectories within an invertible latent space, enabling high-quality dexterous motion without iterative refinement. FAST-LIVGO: A Degeneracy-Robust LiDAR-Inertial-Visual-GNSS Fusion Odometry
Authors: Zhiyu Chen, Chunran Zheng, Jiayu Wen, XiaoLei Zhang, Jiaming Xu, Feng Pan, Yukang Cui
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-06-17T15:33:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:33:11+00:00
Comments: Accepted for presentation at the 2026 IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robust state estimation and mapping in long-term, large-scale, and highly dynamic environments remains a key challenge in robotics. Existing LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry (LIVO) systems achieve strong local accuracy but suffer from accumulated drift over long distances and may fail in geometrically degraded or textureless scenes. Meanwhile, GNSS-aided fusion frameworks often rely on LiDAR or visual odometry for state prediction and outlier rejection, making them vulnerable when odometry degenerates. To address these limitations, we propose a tightly coupled LiDAR-Inertial-Visual-GNSS fusion framework based on an Error-State Iterated Kalman Filter. An online spatiotemporal alignment module using Dynamic Time Warping is introduced for highly dynamic conditions. To better exploit GNSS precision, we develop observation models based on Doppler shifts and fixed-anchor Time-Differenced Carrier Phase, providing millimeter-level relative constraints without augmenting historical anchor states. We further design a degeneracy-aware dual-mode outlier rejection strategy that switches between LIVO-prior-guided rejection and GNSS-aided recovery according to the LIVO degeneracy level. Experiments on the public M3DGR dataset and a custom 20~m/s fixed-wing UAV dataset demonstrate that our system reduces accumulated drift and map ghosting, outperforming state-of-the-art methods in accuracy and robustness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FAST-LIVGO：一种抗退化性的激光雷达-惯性-视觉-GNSS融合里程计
在长期、大规模且高度动态的环境中实现稳健的状态估计与建图仍是机器人领域的关键挑战。现有激光雷达-惯性-视觉里程计（LIVO）系统虽具有较强的局部精度，但在长距离下易积累漂移，且在几何退化或无纹理场景中可能失效。同时，GNSS辅助融合框架通常依赖激光雷达或视觉里程计进行状态预测与异常值剔除，当里程计退化时易受其影响。为解决上述局限，我们提出一种基于误差状态迭代卡尔曼滤波器的紧密耦合激光雷达-惯性-视觉-GNSS融合框架。引入基于动态时间规整的在线时空对齐模块，以应对高度动态场景。为更充分挖掘GNSS精度，我们开发了基于多普勒频移与固定锚点时差载波相位的观测模型，提供毫米级相对约束，且无需扩展历史锚点状态。进一步设计了一种感知退化的双模式异常值剔除策略，根据LIVO退化程度在LIVO先验引导剔除与GNSS辅助恢复模式间切换。在公开M3DGR数据集及自建20m/s固定翼无人机数据集上的实验表明，本系统有效降低累积漂移与地图鬼影，精度与鲁棒性优于现有最先进方法。

To address drift accumulation and failure in degenerate environments in long-term robotic navigation, FAST-LIVGO introduces a tightly coupled LiDAR-Inertial-Visual-GNSS fusion system using an Error-State Iterated Kalman Filter. It incorporates a dynamic time warping module for spatiotemporal alignment under high motion, and leverages Doppler and time-differenced carrier phase GNSS observations for millimeter-level relative constraints without relying on historical anchor states. A degeneracy-aware dual-mode outlier rejection mechanism dynamically switches between LIVO-guided rejection and GNSS-assisted recovery based on odometry health. Experiments on M3DGR and a 20 m/s UAV dataset show reduced drift and map ghosting, achieving superior accuracy and robustness compared to existing methods.

Learning to Annotate Delayed and False AEB Events: A Practical System for Extreme Class Imbalance and Asymmetric Label Noise
Authors: Mengxiang Hao, Xin Jiang, Xinghao Huang, Wenliang Su, Zhiteng Wang, Junjie Rao, Xiaotian Yang, Wei Liao, Chengyu Han, Gen Liang, Yulun Song, Zhitao Xu, Xianpeng Lang
Venue: ICRA
First: 2026-06-17T15:27:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:27:15+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 5 figures, accepted by IEEE International Conference on Robotics and Automation (ICRA)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous Emergency Braking (AEB) optimization relies on accurately annotated real-world trigger events, particularly rare but critical delayed and false AEB triggers that expose system deficiencies. However, these minority samples comprise less than 5% of thousands of daily triggers, making manual annotation prohibitively expensive at scale. We present the first automated AEB annotation framework to address this problem. During development, we identified two fundamental challenges that severely impair delayed/false trigger annotation accuracy: (1) Extreme class imbalance where delayed/false triggers are overwhelmed by true triggers; (2) Asymmetric label noise where mislabeled majority samples (true triggers) suppress minority samples (delayed/false triggers) learning. To overcome these challenges, we propose two key innovations: (1) Specific data augmentation that synthesizes realistic samples by manipulating focal target attributes, transplanting ego-vehicle dynamics, and masking non-focal agents; (2) noise suppression using stable hardness estimation and probe-guided adaptive threshold to clean mislabeled true trigger samples. Crucially, we deploy our model as a practical annotation system with full-stack architecture, efficiently identifying critical delayed/false triggers from thousands of daily AEB events. Production results demonstrate 80% improvement in recall of delayed/false triggers and 50% reduction in manual workload. Beyond immediate gains, the system enables continuous self-improvement through accumulated high-quality annotations, establishing a necessary data foundation for on-vehicle AEB system optimization
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习标注延迟与误触发的AEB事件：一种应对极端类别不平衡与不对称标签噪声的实用系统
自主紧急制动（AEB）优化依赖于准确标注的真实世界触发事件，特别是罕见但关键的延迟与误触发AEB事件，这些事件暴露系统缺陷。然而，这些少数样本仅占每日数千次触发事件的不到5%，大规模人工标注成本极高。我们提出了首个自动化AEB标注框架以解决此问题。开发过程中，我们识别出两个严重损害延迟/误触发事件标注准确性的根本挑战：（1）极端类别不平衡，延迟/误触发事件被真实触发事件淹没；（2）不对称标签噪声，误标多数样本（真实触发）抑制少数样本（延迟/误触发）学习。为克服这些挑战，我们提出两项关键创新：（1）特定数据增强，通过操纵焦点目标属性、移植本车动态及遮蔽非焦点代理合成真实样本；（2）噪声抑制，利用稳定难度估计与探针引导自适应阈值清理误标的真实触发样本。关键的是，我们以全栈架构将模型部署为实用标注系统，高效从每日数千次AEB事件中识别关键延迟/误触发事件。生产结果表明，延迟/误触发事件召回率提升80%，人工工作量减少50%。除即时收益外，该系统通过积累高质量标注实现持续自我优化，为车载AEB系统优化奠定必要数据基础。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of manually annotating rare but critical delayed and false AEB triggers—comprising less than 5% of daily events—the paper introduces an automated annotation system designed for extreme class imbalance and asymmetric label noise. The method combines specific data augmentation techniques that synthesize realistic minority samples by altering focal target attributes, ego-vehicle dynamics, and masking non-focal agents, alongside a noise suppression module using stable hardness estimation and probe-guided adaptive thresholds to filter mislabeled true triggers. Experimental results from deployment in production show an 80% improvement in recall for delayed/false triggers and a 50% reduction in manual annotation workload, while also enabling continuous self-improvement through accumulated high-quality data.
为解决罕见但关键的延迟与误触发AEB事件（占比不足5%）人工标注成本过高的问题，本文提出首个自动化标注系统，针对极端类别不平衡与不对称标签噪声两大挑战。方法结合特定数据增强技术——通过调整焦点目标属性、移植车辆动态并遮蔽非焦点代理合成少数样本，以及噪声抑制模块——利用稳定难度估计与探针引导自适应阈值过滤误标多数样本。实际部署结果表明，延迟/误触发事件召回率提升80%，人工标注工作量减少50%，同时通过积累高质量标注实现系统持续优化。
Speaker Verification with Speech-Aware LLMs: Evaluation and Augmentation
Authors: Thomas Thebaud, Yuzhe Wang, Laureano Moro-Velazquez, Jesus Villalba-Lopez, Najim Dehak
First: 2026-03-11T14:34:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:26:22+00:00
Comments: 3 Tables, 1 Figure, Published in Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speech-aware large language models (LLMs) can accept speech inputs, yet their training objectives largely emphasize linguistic content or specific fields such as emotions or the speaker's gender, leaving it unclear whether they encode speaker identity. First, we propose a model-agnostic scoring protocol that produces continuous verification scores for both API-only and open-weight models, using confidence scores or log-likelihood ratios from the Yes/No token probabilities. Using this protocol, we benchmark recent speech-aware LLMs and observe weak speaker discrimination (EERs above 20% on VoxCeleb1). Second, we introduce a lightweight augmentation that equips an LLM with ASV capability by injecting frozen ECAPA-TDNN speaker embeddings through a learned projection and training only LoRA adapters. On TinyLLaMA-1.1B, the resulting ECAPA-LLM achieves 1.03% EER on VoxCeleb1-E, approaching a dedicated speaker verification system while preserving a natural-language interface.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于语音感知大语言模型的说话人验证：评估与增强
语音感知大语言模型（LLMs）可接受语音输入，但其训练目标主要强调语言内容或特定领域（如情绪或说话人性别），尚不清楚其是否编码说话人身份。首先，我们提出一种模型无关的评分协议，通过“是/否”标记概率的置信度分数或对数似然比，为仅API和开源权重模型生成连续验证分数。使用该协议，我们对近期语音感知LLMs进行基准测试，发现其说话人区分能力较弱（在VoxCeleb1上EER超过20%）。其次，我们引入一种轻量级增强方法，通过学习投影将冻结的ECAPA-TDNN说话人嵌入注入LLM，并仅训练LoRA适配器，赋予LLM说话人验证能力。在TinyLLaMA-1.1B上，所得ECAPA-LLM在VoxCeleb1-E上达到1.03%的EER，接近专用说话人验证系统，同时保留自然语言接口。

AGDN: Learning to Solve Traveling Salesman Problem with Anisotropic Graph Diffusion Network
Authors: Bolin Shen, Ziwei Huang, Zhiguang Cao, Yushun Dong
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2026-06-17T15:24:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:24:37+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 32nd ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining (KDD 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) is a cornerstone of combinatorial optimization and arises in many practical scenarios. Although graph-based learning approaches have been explored for TSP, the question of how to exploit graph structure more effectively remains open. We present the Anisotropic Graph Diffusion Network (AGDN), a new Graph Neural Network framework designed to solve TSP. Our method tackles two central difficulties: (1) the lack of informative topological prior in fully connected TSP graphs, and (2) losing connected nodes in the optimal solution after the commonly used graph sparsification techniques. To overcome these issues, we construct a MixScore transition matrix that merges node similarity with pairwise distance, and we develop an anisotropic graph diffusion strategy that supports efficient information exchange across multiple hops. Comprehensive experiments spanning diverse instance sizes and node distributions show that AGDN consistently outperforms existing methods while keeping computation time competitive. Furthermore, AGDN generalizes well to problem sizes and distributions beyond those seen during training. The implementation is publicly available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/AGDN.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AGDN：利用各向异性图扩散网络求解旅行商问题
旅行商问题（TSP）是组合优化的核心问题，广泛应用于实际场景。尽管已有图学习方法被用于TSP，但如何更有效地利用图结构仍待探索。本文提出各向异性图扩散网络（AGDN），一种新的图神经网络框架，用于求解TSP。该方法解决两大核心难题：（1）全连接TSP图缺乏信息丰富的拓扑先验；（2）常用图稀疏化技术在最优解中会丢失连通节点。为应对这些问题，我们构建了融合节点相似性与成对距离的MixScore转移矩阵，并开发了支持多跳高效信息交换的各向异性图扩散策略。在多种实例规模和节点分布的全面实验中，AGDN持续优于现有方法，同时保持计算时间竞争力。此外，AGDN对训练中未见的问题规模和分布具有良好的泛化能力。代码已公开：https://github.com/LabRAI/AGDN。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better exploit graph structure in solving the Traveling Salesman Problem, the authors propose AGDN, an anisotropic graph diffusion network that addresses two key challenges: insufficient topological priors in fully connected TSP graphs and loss of connectivity after graph sparsification. The method introduces a MixScore transition matrix combining node similarity and pairwise distance, enabling anisotropic diffusion for efficient multi-hop information exchange. Experimental results across diverse TSP instances demonstrate that AGDN consistently achieves superior solution quality compared to existing methods while maintaining competitive computational efficiency and strong generalization to unseen problem sizes and distributions.
为更有效地利用图结构求解旅行商问题，作者提出AGDN（各向异性图扩散网络），旨在解决两个核心问题：全连接TSP图中缺乏拓扑先验信息，以及常用图稀疏化方法导致最优解中节点连接丢失。该方法通过融合节点相似度与点对距离构建MixScore转移矩阵，支持各向异性图扩散以实现高效多跳信息传递。实验表明，AGDN在多种TSP实例上持续优于现有方法，计算效率保持竞争力，并能良好泛化至训练未见的规模与分布。
When AUC Misleads: Polarization-Aware Evaluation of Deepfake Detectors under Domain Shift
Authors: Dat Nguyen, Cosmin Radoi, Romain Hermary, Marcella Astrid, Nesryne Mejri, Enjie Ghorbel, Djamila Aouada
First: 2026-06-17T15:24:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:24:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in generative AI, such as diffusion models and face-swapping tools, have enabled the creation of highly realistic deepfakes, leading to real-world harms including financial fraud and non-consensual explicit content. In response, deepfake detection has become an active research area, with recent methods increasingly focusing on improving generalization to unseen manipulations. This is typically evaluated using the Area Under the ROC Curve (AUC) measured separately across multiple datasets. However, such an evaluation fails to reflect real-world scenarios where detectors face a mixture of data sources and varying artifact types. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel metric, Cross-dataset AUC (Cross-AUC) that averages per-domain AUCs with a measure of prediction polarization for taking into account the robustness to domain shift. The polarization extent is quantified by the Wasserstein Distance between class score distributions. Cross-AUC not only assesses the generalization capabilities of deepfake detectors under domain shifts more realistically, but it is also interpretable as it better explains the reason behind a drop in performance. Experiments performed on seven benchmark datasets demonstrate its practical relevance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当AUC误导时：在域迁移下具有极化感知能力的深度伪造检测器评估
生成式AI的最新进展，如扩散模型和人脸交换工具，使得高度逼真的深度伪造内容得以生成，导致现实世界中的金融欺诈和非自愿色情内容等危害。为此，深度伪造检测已成为活跃的研究领域，近期方法越来越关注提升对未见过的篡改内容的泛化能力。这通常通过在多个数据集上分别测量ROC曲线下的面积（AUC）来评估。然而，这种评估方式无法反映现实场景中检测器面对混合数据源和不同伪影类型时的真实情况。为解决这一局限，我们引入了一种新指标——跨数据集AUC（Cross-AUC），通过结合各域AUC与预测极化程度的度量，以考虑对域迁移的鲁棒性。极化程度通过类别分数分布之间的Wasserstein距离量化。Cross-AUC不仅更真实地评估了深度伪造检测器在域迁移下的泛化能力，而且具有可解释性，能更好地解释性能下降的原因。在七个基准数据集上的实验验证了其实际价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the real-world harms caused by realistic deepfakes and the limitations of standard AUC evaluation under domain shifts, this paper proposes Cross-AUC, a novel metric that combines per-domain AUCs with a Wasserstein Distance-based measure of prediction polarization to assess detector robustness across data sources. Experiments on seven benchmark datasets show that Cross-AUC better reflects real-world performance degradation and provides interpretable insights into why performance drops, outperforming standard AUC in capturing generalization under domain shift.
鉴于生成式AI带来的高度逼真深度伪造对现实世界造成的危害，以及标准AUC在跨域场景下评估不足的问题，本文提出Cross-AUC指标，通过结合各域AUC与基于Wasserstein距离的预测极化度量，更真实地反映检测器在混合数据源下的鲁棒性。在七个基准数据集上的实验表明，Cross-AUC比标准AUC更准确预测实际性能，揭示了部分模型虽单域表现良好，但在域迁移下因预测极化而失效的现象。
iTryOn: Mastering Interactive Video Virtual Try-On with Spatial-Semantic Guidance
Authors: Jun Zheng, Zhengze Xu, Mengting Chen, Jing Wang, Jinsong Lan, Xiaoyong Zhu, Kaifu Zhang, Bo Zheng, Xiaodan Liang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-20T17:23:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:23:12+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://zhengjun-ai.github.io/itryon-page. Accepted by ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Video Virtual Try-On (VVT) aims to seamlessly replace a garment on a person in a video with a new one. While existing methods have made significant strides in maintaining temporal consistency, they are predominantly confined to non-interactive scenarios where models merely showcase garments. This limitation overlooks a crucial aspect of real-world apparel presentation: active human-garment interaction. To bridge this gap, we introduce and formalize a new challenging task: Interactive Video Virtual Try-On (Interactive VVT), where subjects in the video actively engage with their clothing. This task introduces unique challenges beyond simple texture preservation, including: (1) resolving the semantic ambiguity of interactions from standard pose information, and (2) learning complex garment deformations from video where interactive moments are sparse and brief. To address these challenges, we propose iTryOn, a novel framework built upon a large-scale video diffusion Transformer. iTryOn pioneers a multi-level interaction injection mechanism to guide the generation of complex dynamics. At the spatial level, we introduce a garment-agnostic 3D hand prior to provide fine-grained guidance for precise hand-garment contact, effectively resolving spatial ambiguity. At the semantic level, iTryOn leverages global captions for overall context and time-stamped action captions for localized interactions, synchronized via our novel Action-aware Rotational Position Embedding (A-RoPE). Extensive experiments demonstrate that iTryOn not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on traditional VVT benchmarks but also establishes a commanding lead in the new interactive setting, marking a significant step towards more dynamic and controllable virtual try-on experiences.
中文标题/摘要
标题：iTryOn：基于空间语义引导的交互式视频虚拟试穿
视频虚拟试穿（VVT）旨在无缝替换视频中人物所穿衣物。现有方法虽在保持时间一致性方面取得显著进展，但多局限于非交互场景，仅展示服装，忽视了现实服装展示中关键的人与衣物主动交互环节。为弥合此差距，我们提出并形式化了更具挑战性的新任务：交互式视频虚拟试穿（Interactive VVT），其中视频中人物主动与衣物互动。该任务超越简单纹理保持，面临两大挑战：（1）从标准姿态信息中解析交互的语义歧义；（2）从稀疏且短暂的交互时刻中学习复杂衣物形变。为此，我们提出iTryOn框架，基于大规模视频扩散Transformer构建。iTryOn首创多级交互注入机制以引导复杂动态生成。空间层面，引入无服装依赖的3D手部先验，提供精细的手部-衣物接触引导，有效解决空间歧义；语义层面，利用全局描述提供整体上下文，结合时间戳动作描述实现局部交互，通过我们提出的动作感知旋转位置嵌入（A-RoPE）同步。大量实验表明，iTryOn不仅在传统VVT基准上达到最先进性能，更在新交互场景中大幅领先，标志着向更动态可控的虚拟试穿体验迈出重要一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to simulate realistic human-garment interactions in video virtual try-on, the authors introduce Interactive Video Virtual Try-On (Interactive VVT), a new task that goes beyond static garment replacement by requiring models to handle dynamic, sparse, and ambiguous interactions. To tackle this, they propose iTryOn, a diffusion-based Transformer framework that injects multi-level interaction guidance: spatially, it uses a 3D hand prior to resolve hand-garment contact ambiguity; semantically, it employs global and time-stamped action captions with a novel Action-aware Rotational Position Embedding to capture interaction context. Experimental results show iTryOn outperforms prior methods on both traditional VVT benchmarks and the new Interactive VVT setting, demonstrating superior handling of complex garment deformations and interaction dynamics.
为解决现有视频虚拟试衣缺乏真实人衣互动的问题，作者提出交互式视频虚拟试衣（Interactive VVT）新任务，要求模型在用户主动操作衣物时生成逼真的动态变形。他们设计了iTryOn框架，基于视频扩散Transformer，通过多级交互注入机制提升效果：空间层面引入无服装依赖的3D手部先验以精确建模手衣接触；语义层面结合全局与时间戳动作描述，利用动作感知旋转位置嵌入（A-RoPE）同步局部交互。实验表明，iTryOn在传统试衣基准和新交互任务上均显著优于现有方法，展现出更强的时序一致性和交互真实感。
Language Models as Interfaces, Not Oracles: A Hybrid LLM-ML System for Pediatric Appendicitis
Authors: Soheyl Bateni, Maryam Abdolali
First: 2026-06-17T15:22:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:22:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) can make clinical decision support more accessible by interpreting free-text documentation, but their direct use as diagnostic engines is limited by sensitivity to prompts, information order, and plausible but incorrect outputs. Structured machine-learning models offer more stable risk prediction, yet they require tabular inputs that are difficult to integrate with narrative clinical workflows. We present ClaMPAPP (Clinical Language-assisted Machine-learning Pipeline for Appendicitis), a hybrid system that uses an LLM as an interface rather than as the final decision-maker. ClaMPAPP extracts schema-constrained clinical features from note-like narratives, applies deterministic plausibility checks, and passes validated features to an XGBoost classifier trained on clinical, laboratory, and ultrasound variables. We evaluated ClaMPAPP on two independent pediatric appendicitis cohorts from German hospitals and compared it with end-to-end LLM baselines, including open-source and proprietary models. To preserve ground truth while testing free-text input, narratives were generated from structured electronic health records through template rendering and constrained LLM rewriting, with additional sentence-order permutation to assess positional robustness. ClaMPAPP achieved the strongest overall diagnostic performance in both internal and external validation while minimizing missed appendicitis cases, the key safety concern in acute triage. End-to-end LLMs showed unstable sensitivity-specificity trade-offs and greater degradation under narrative reordering. These results support an LLM-as-interface, ML-as-predictor design that separates natural-language usability from predictive inference and provides a more auditable pathway for clinical decision support.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型作为接口而非预言机：一种用于儿童急性阑尾炎的混合LLM-ML系统
大型语言模型（LLM）可通过解析自由文本记录提升临床决策支持的可及性，但其直接作为诊断引擎的使用受限于对提示、信息顺序的敏感性及合理但错误的输出。结构化机器学习模型虽能提供更稳定的预后预测，但需表格式输入，难以与叙述性临床工作流程整合。我们提出ClaMPAPP（用于阑尾炎的临床语言辅助机器学习流程），该混合系统将LLM作为接口而非最终决策者：从类似病历的叙述中提取受模式约束的临床特征，应用确定性合理性检查，再将验证后的特征输入至基于临床、实验室及超声变量训练的XGBoost分类器。我们在两家德国医院的两个独立儿童阑尾炎队列中评估ClaMPAPP，并与端到端LLM基线模型（含开源与专有模型）对比。为在测试自由文本输入时保留真实标签，我们通过模板渲染及受限LLM重写从结构化电子健康记录生成叙述，并增加句子顺序重排以评估位置鲁棒性。ClaMPAPP在内部与外部验证中均取得最佳整体诊断性能，同时最小化漏诊阑尾炎病例——急性分诊中的关键安全问题。端到端LLM表现出不稳定的敏感性-特异性权衡，且在叙述重排下性能退化更明显。这些结果支持“LLM作为接口、ML作为预测器”的设计，将自然语言可用性与预测推理分离，并为临床决策支持提供更可审计的路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of LLMs as direct diagnostic tools—such as prompt sensitivity and hallucination risks—this study proposes ClaMPAPP, a hybrid system that uses an LLM as an interface to extract and validate structured clinical features from narrative notes, then feeds them to an XGBoost classifier trained on clinical data. Evaluated on two pediatric appendicitis cohorts, ClaMPAPP outperformed end-to-end LLM baselines in diagnostic accuracy while maintaining high sensitivity to avoid missing cases, a critical safety metric in acute care. The system’s robustness to narrative reordering further highlights its reliability compared to LLMs that degrade under input variation.
为解决大语言模型作为诊断工具时易受提示影响、易产生幻觉的问题，该研究提出ClaMPAPP系统，利用LLM作为接口从自由文本中提取并验证结构化临床特征，再输入XGBoost分类器进行预测。在两个儿科阑尾炎队列中，ClaMPAPP在诊断准确性和减少漏诊（急性分诊关键安全指标）方面优于端到端LLM基线模型，且对文本顺序变化具有鲁棒性，支持将自然语言交互与预测推理分离，构建更可靠、可审计的临床决策支持架构。
DecNefSimulator: A Modular, Interpretable Framework for Decoded Neurofeedback Simulation Using Generative Models
Authors: Alexander Olza, Roberto Santana, David Soto
First: 2025-11-18T14:58:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:22:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decoded Neurofeedback (DecNef) is a promising non-invasive approach to brain modulation with wide-ranging applications in neuromedicine and cognitive neuroscience. However, progress in DecNef research remains constrained by subject-dependent learning variability, reliance on indirect measures to quantify progress, and the high cost and time demands of experimentation. We present DecNefSimulator, a modular and interpretable simulation framework that formalizes DecNef as a machine learning problem. Beyond providing a virtual laboratory, DecNefSimulator enables researchers to model, analyze and understand neurofeedback dynamics. Using latent variable generative models as simulated participants, DecNefSimulator allows direct observation of internal cognitive states and systematic evaluation of how different protocol designs and subject characteristics influence learning. We demonstrate how this approach can (i) reproduce empirical phenomena of DecNef learning, (ii) identify conditions under which DecNef feedback fails to induce learning, and (iii) guide the design of more robust and reliable DecNef protocols in silico before human implementation. In summary, DecNefSimulator bridges computational modeling and cognitive neuroscience, offering a principled foundation for methodological innovation, robust protocol design, and ultimately, a deeper understanding of DecNef-based brain modulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DecNefSimulator：一种基于生成模型的解码神经反馈模拟的模块化、可解释框架
解码神经反馈（DecNef）是一种有前景的非侵入性脑调控方法，在神经医学和认知神经科学中具有广泛应用。然而，DecNef研究进展受限于受试者学习差异、依赖间接指标衡量进展，以及实验成本和时间要求高。 我们提出了DecNefSimulator，一种模块化且可解释的模拟框架，将DecNef形式化为机器学习问题。该框架不仅提供虚拟实验室，还使研究人员能够建模、分析和理解神经反馈动态。通过使用潜在变量生成模型作为模拟受试者，DecNefSimulator可直接观察内部认知状态，并系统评估不同协议设计和受试者特征如何影响学习。 我们展示了该方法可（i）重现DecNef学习的实证现象，（ii）识别DecNef反馈无法诱导学习的条件，（iii）在人体实验前指导更稳健可靠的DecNef协议设计。 总之，DecNefSimulator连接计算建模与认知神经科学，为方法学创新、稳健协议设计提供原则性基础，最终深化对基于DecNef的脑调控机制的理解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of real-world Decoded Neurofeedback (DecNef) research—including subject variability, indirect progress measurement, and high experimental costs—the authors developed DecNefSimulator, a modular simulation framework that models DecNef as a machine learning problem using latent variable generative models as virtual participants. This enables direct observation of internal cognitive states and systematic testing of how protocol design and subject traits affect learning. Experimental results show the simulator successfully reproduces empirical DecNef learning patterns, identifies conditions under which feedback fails to induce learning, and provides actionable insights for designing more robust DecNef protocols prior to human trials.
为解决真实世界解码神经反馈研究中存在的被试差异大、进展评估间接、实验成本高和耗时长等问题，作者构建了DecNefSimulator模拟框架，将解码神经反馈建模为机器学习问题，采用潜在变量生成模型作为虚拟被试。该框架支持直接观测内部认知状态，并系统评估协议设计与被试特征对学习的影响。实验结果表明，该模拟器能复现实证学习现象，识别反馈失效的学习条件，并为人类实验前提供优化协议的可靠设计依据。
Compute Efficiency and Serial Runtime Tradeoffs for Stochastic Momentum Methods
Authors: Depen Morwani, Alexandru Meterez, Pranav Nair, Sham Kakade
First: 2026-06-17T15:19:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:19:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stochastic momentum methods such as heavy ball (HB), Nesterov momentum, and variants of Accelerated SGD (ASGD) [Kidambi et al., 2018] are widely used in modern training, but their stochastic benefits depend on two distinct quantities: serial runtime, the number of iterations needed to reach a target accuracy, and compute efficiency (CE), the inverse total gradient-query or FLOP cost. Larger batches reduce serial runtime without hurting CE only when the contraction gap grows linearly with batch size. We study stochastic HB and ASGD for consistent linear regression with Gaussian covariates and prove finite-dimensional, discrete-time lower bounds on their batch-size tradeoffs. Our first result shows that HB does not improve the CE frontier over SGD for arbitrary spectra; rather, it preserves SGD-level CE over a larger batch-size window, allowing larger batches to reduce serial runtime until HB reaches its deterministic accelerated scale. This window can be a factor $\sqrtκ$ larger than the SGD critical batch size. For ASGD, the picture is more spectrum-dependent: for rapidly decaying power-law spectra, ASGD improves small-batch CE over HB/SGD, but as batch size grows it trades this CE advantage for improved serial runtime. Synthetic linear-regression experiments verify these qualitative regimes, including near-overlap of ASGD and HB for slowly decaying spectra and the predicted CE--serial tradeoff for rapidly decaying spectra.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随机动量方法的计算效率与串行运行时间权衡
重球（HB）、Nesterov动量及加速SGD（ASGD）[Kidambi等，2018]等随机动量方法在现代训练中广泛应用，但其随机性收益依赖于两个不同量：串行运行时间（达到目标精度所需迭代次数）和计算效率（CE，总梯度查询或FLOP成本的倒数）。仅当收缩间隙随批量大小线性增长时，增大批量才能减少串行运行时间而不损害CE。 我们研究了高斯协变量下一致线性回归的随机HB与ASGD，证明了其批量大小权衡的有限维离散时间下界。第一个结果表明，HB对任意谱结构不会改善SGD的CE前沿；相反，它在更大批量范围内保持SGD级别的CE，允许更大批量减少串行运行时间，直至HB达到其确定性加速尺度。该范围可比SGD临界批量大小大一个因子√κ。对于ASGD，情况更依赖于谱结构：对于快速衰减的幂律谱，ASGD在小批量时优于HB/SGD的CE，但随着批量增大，它以改善串行运行时间为代价换取CE优势。合成线性回归实验验证了这些定性模式，包括缓慢衰减谱下ASGD与HB近乎重叠，以及快速衰减谱下预测的CE-串行运行时间权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how batch size affects both serial runtime and compute efficiency in stochastic momentum methods, the paper analyzes heavy ball (HB) and accelerated SGD (ASGD) in linear regression with Gaussian covariates. Using finite-dimensional discrete-time lower bounds, it shows that HB maintains SGD-level compute efficiency over a broader batch-size range, enabling larger batches to reduce serial runtime without sacrificing efficiency—up to a factor of sqrt(κ) beyond SGD’s critical batch size. For ASGD, the behavior depends on spectrum decay: it improves compute efficiency over small batches compared to HB/SGD for rapidly decaying spectra, but as batch size increases, it trades this efficiency gain for faster serial runtime. Synthetic experiments confirm these theoretical tradeoffs, including near-equivalence between ASGD and HB for slow-decaying spectra and the predicted CE-serial runtime tradeoff for fast-decaying spectra.
为理解随机动量方法中批次大小对串行运行时间和计算效率的影响，该文在高斯协变量的线性回归中分析了重球（HB）和加速SGD（ASGD）方法。通过有限维离散时间下界证明，HB在更宽的批次范围内保持与SGD相当的计算效率，允许更大批次降低串行运行时间而不牺牲效率，直至达到其确定性加速极限——该范围可达SGD临界批次大小的sqrt(κ)倍。对于ASGD，其表现依赖于谱衰减：在快速衰减的幂律谱下，ASGD在小批次时比HB/SGD更优的计算效率，但随着批次增大，会以牺牲该效率优势换取更好的串行运行时间。合成实验验证了这些趋势，包括在缓慢衰减谱下ASGD与HB近似等效，以及在快速衰减谱下预测的计算效率-串行运行时间权衡关系。
When Cars Have Stereotypes: Auditing Demographic Bias in Objects from Text-to-Image Models
Authors: Dasol Choi, Jihwan Lee, Minjae Lee, Minsuk Kahng
First: 2025-08-05T14:15:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:18:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While prior research on text-to-image generation has predominantly focused on biases in human depictions, demographic bias in generated objects remains relatively underexplored. We introduce SODA (Stereotyped Object Diagnostic Audit), a novel framework for systematically measuring these biases through automated attribute discovery and three standardized metrics: Base vs. Demographic Divergence (BDS), Cross-Demographic Disparity (CDS), and Visual Attribute Concentration (VAC). Applying SODA to 8,000 images across five state-of-the-art models and eight object categories (e.g., cars), we find that "neutral" prompts produce outputs most visually similar to middle-aged and White people, suggesting these groups are implicitly over-represented in model defaults. Furthermore, demographic cues trigger highly skewed stereotypical outputs: 26.6% of object-model-demographic combinations produce results where all 20 generated images share the exact same attribute value (e.g., rose gold laptops for women). Finally, prompt-level debiasing reduces inter-group disparity but paradoxically collapses within-group diversity, replacing one stereotype with another. SODA offers a practical pipeline for making these implicit associations measurable, serving as a step toward more responsible AI development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当汽车具有刻板印象：文本到图像模型中物体人口统计偏见的审计
尽管先前关于文本到图像生成的研究主要集中在人类描绘中的偏见，但生成物体中的人口统计偏见仍相对未被充分探索。我们提出了SODA（刻板印象物体诊断审计），一种通过自动化属性发现和三个标准化指标——基础与人口统计差异（BDS）、跨人口统计差异（CDS）和视觉属性集中度（VAC）——系统测量这些偏见的新框架。将SODA应用于五种最先进模型的8000张图像和八个物体类别（如汽车），我们发现“中性”提示生成的图像在视觉上最接近中年白人，表明这些群体在模型默认设置中被隐性过度代表。此外，人口统计线索会引发高度偏斜的刻板印象输出：26.6%的物体-模型-人口统计组合中，所有20张生成图像共享完全相同的属性值（如女性的玫瑰金笔记本电脑）。最后，提示级别的去偏方法虽能减少群体间差异，却悖论性地压缩了群体内多样性，用另一种刻板印象取代了前者。SODA提供了一种实用流程，使这些隐性关联可测量，是迈向更负责任的AI开发的重要一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the under-examination of demographic bias in generated objects from text-to-image models, the authors introduce SODA, a framework that automates attribute discovery and evaluates bias using three metrics: BDS, CDS, and VAC. 鉴于现有研究主要关注文本生成图像中人物的偏见，而对非人类物体的群体偏见关注不足，本研究提出SODA框架，通过自动化属性发现和BDS、CDS、VAC三个指标评估偏见。在五种模型和八类物体（如汽车）共8000张图像上测试发现，中性提示常默认呈现与中年白人相关的视觉特征，暗示其隐性过度代表。群体提示会引发极端刻板印象，26.6%的物体-模型-群体组合中，20张生成图像均具有完全相同的属性值。虽然提示级去偏方法可降低群体间差异，却同时削弱群体内多样性，以另一种刻板印象替代前者。
Estimating carbon pools in the European Shelf sea environment: replacing reanalysis by model-informed machine learning?
Authors: Jozef Skakala
Venue: JGR - Machine Learning and Computation 3 (2026)
First: 2025-08-13T20:30:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:18:43+00:00
Comments: 37 pages, 9 figures (+ 3 in the appendix), v3 - published version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Shelf seas are important for the economy and the carbon cycle, but shelf sea observations for carbon pools are often sparse, or highly uncertain. An alternative can be provided by carbon reanalyses (whether assimilating proxy variables, such as chlorophyll-$a$, or directly carbon), but these are often expensive to run. We propose to use a computationally cheap ensemble of neural networks (i.e. deep ensemble) to learn the relationship between the directly observable (atmospheric, riverine and ocean) variables and marine carbon pools from a coupled physics-biogeochemistry model. The deep ensemble was trained on a North-West European Shelf (NWES) physical-biogeochemistry model free run simulation. After training, the deep ensemble was run using inputs from the NWES reanalysis instead of the free run, demonstrating that it can efficiently predict several NWES carbon pools (e.g., detritus, zooplankton, heterotrophic bacteria) in much better agreement with the reanalysis than the free run, while also providing uncertainty information. We further show that the deep ensemble performs similarly well when it is driven directly by the observations assimilated into the reanalysis, with the limitation that carbon pools can then be predicted only at the observed locations and times. We focus on explainability of the results and demonstrate potential use of the deep ensembles for future climate what-if scenarios. We suggest that model-informed machine learning presents a viable alternative to expensive reanalyses and could complement observations, wherever they are missing and/or highly uncertain.
中文标题/摘要
标题：欧洲大陆架海域碳库估算：用模型指导的机器学习替代再分析方法？
大陆架海域对经济和碳循环至关重要，但其碳库观测数据常稀疏或不确定性高。碳再分析（无论是融合代理变量如叶绿素-a，还是直接碳变量）虽可提供替代方案，但运行成本高昂。本文提出使用计算成本低廉的神经网络集合（即深度集合）从耦合物理-生物地球化学模型中学习大气、河流和海洋可直接观测变量与海洋碳库之间的关系。该深度集合在西北欧洲大陆架（NWES）物理-生物地球化学模型自由运行模拟数据上训练后，用NWES再分析数据替代自由运行数据进行预测，结果表明其能高效预测多个NWES碳库（如碎屑、浮游动物、异养细菌），且与再分析结果吻合度优于自由运行结果，同时提供不确定性信息。进一步研究表明，当深度集合直接由再分析中同化观测数据驱动时，其表现同样良好，但碳库预测仅限于观测点和时间。我们注重结果的可解释性，并展示深度集合在未来的气候情景模拟中的潜在应用。我们认为，模型指导的机器学习是昂贵再分析方法的可行替代方案，可在观测缺失或不确定性高的区域补充观测数据。
Summary / 总结

Trained on a physics-biogeochemistry model’s free run simulation of the North-West European Shelf, the model learns the relationship between observable environmental variables and carbon components like detritus and zooplankton. When applied to reanalysis inputs, the deep ensemble outperformed the free run in accuracy and provided uncertainty estimates, and maintained strong performance when driven directly by assimilated observations, albeit limited to observed locations. The method demonstrates potential for climate scenario analysis and offers a cost-effective alternative to traditional reanalyses, especially where observations are sparse or unreliable.
鉴于近海碳池观测数据稀疏且不确定性高，本研究提出使用计算成本低的深度神经网络集成模型来预测海洋碳池。该模型基于西北欧洲大陆架物理-生物地球化学模型的自由运行模拟进行训练，学习环境变量与碳组分之间的关系。当输入为再分析数据时，该模型在预测如碎屑和浮游动物等碳池方面显著优于自由运行模拟，且能提供不确定性估计。当直接由再分析数据同化观测驱动时，模型表现同样良好，但受限于观测点位和时间。该方法具备气候情景分析潜力，可作为昂贵再分析的低成本替代方案，尤其适用于观测缺失或不确定的区域。
Hardware- and Vision-in-the-Loop Validation of Deep Monocular Pose Estimation for Autonomous Maritime UAV Flight
Authors: Maneesha Wickramasuriya, Beomyeol Yu, Jaden Shin, Mason Huslig, Taeyoung Lee, Murray Snyder
First: 2026-06-17T15:18:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:18:11+00:00
Comments: 6 pages 9 figues
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous UAV operations on ships require reliable vision-based relative pose estimation, yet at-sea validation is costly, weather-dependent, and risky. This paper presents a hardware-validated vision-in-the-loop framework that enables fully autonomous indoor flight while emulating photorealistic maritime environments. Rendered maritime views are processed onboard by a deep transformer-based monocular pose estimator. Delayed vision measurements are fused with high-rate IMU data using a delayed Kalman filter to provide consistent state estimates for geometric control. The system captures critical embedded effects, including perception latency, asynchronous updates, and computational constraints, that are absent in pure simulation. Autonomous takeoff, trajectory tracking, and landing experiments demonstrate stable closed-loop flight. The results establish a safe and hardware-realistic intermediate stage for developing maritime UAV autonomy prior to shipboard deployment.
