FashionChameleon: Towards Real-Time and Interactive Human-Garment Video Customization
Authors: Quanjian Song, Yefeng Shen, Mengting Chen, Hao Sun, Jinsong Lan, Xiaoyong Zhu, Bo Zheng, Liujuan Cao
First: 2026-05-15T10:25:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:16:37+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://quanjiansong.github.io/projects/FashionChameleon/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Human-centric video customization, particularly at the garment level, has shown significant commercial value. However, existing approaches cannot support low-latency and interactive garment control, which is crucial for applications such as e-commerce and content creation. This paper studies how to achieve interactive multi-garment video customization while preserving motion coherence using only single-garment video data. We present FashionChameleon, a real-time and interactive framework for human-garment customization in autoregressive video generation, where users can interactively switch garment during generation. FashionChameleon consists of three key techniques: (i) Instead of training on multi-garment video data, we train a Teacher Model with In-Context Learning on a single reference-garment pair. By retaining the image-to-video training paradigm while enforcing a mismatch between the reference and garment image, the model is encouraged to implicitly preserve coherence during single-garment switching. (ii) To achieve consistency and efficiency during generation, we introduce Streaming Distillation with In-Context Learning, which fine-tunes the model with in-context teacher forcing and improves extrapolation consistency via gradient-reweighted distribution matching distillation. (iii) To extend the model for interactive multi-garment video customization, we propose Training-Free KV Cache Rescheduling, which includes garment KV refresh, historical KV withdraw, and reference KV disentangle to achieve garment switching while preserving motion coherence. Our FashionChameleon uniquely supports interactive customization and consistent long-video extrapolation, while achieving real-time generation at 23.8 FPS on a single GPU, 30-180$\times$ faster than existing baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FashionChameleon：面向实时交互式人体服装视频定制
以人体为中心的视频定制，特别是服装层面，具有显著商业价值。然而，现有方法无法支持低延迟和交互式服装控制，这对电商和内容创作等应用至关重要。本文研究如何仅使用单件服装视频数据，在保持运动连贯性的同时实现交互式多服装视频定制。我们提出了FashionChameleon，一个用于自回归视频生成中人体服装定制的实时交互框架，用户可在生成过程中交互切换服装。FashionChameleon包含三项关键技术：(i) 不使用多服装视频数据训练，而是通过上下文学习在单件参考服装对上训练教师模型。通过保留图像到视频的训练范式并强制参考图像与服装图像不匹配，模型被鼓励在单件服装切换时隐式保持连贯性。(ii) 为实现生成过程中的连贯性和效率，我们引入了结合上下文学习的流式蒸馏，通过上下文教师强制微调模型，并通过梯度加权分布匹配蒸馏提升外推一致性。(iii) 为扩展模型支持交互式多服装视频定制，我们提出无训练KV缓存重排，包括服装KV刷新、历史KV撤回和参考KV解耦，以在保持运动连贯性的同时实现服装切换。FashionChameleon独特支持交互式定制和一致的长视频外推，同时在单GPU上实现23.8 FPS的实时生成，比现有基线快30至180倍。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for real-time, interactive garment customization in e-commerce and content creation, this paper introduces FashionChameleon, a framework enabling users to switch garments during autoregressive video generation while preserving motion coherence. It trains a Teacher Model via in-context learning on single-garment video pairs, then distills it with streaming techniques that enforce consistency through gradient-reweighted distribution matching. To support interactive garment switching without retraining, it employs training-free KV cache rescheduling, managing historical and reference memory to maintain motion continuity. Experiments show FashionChameleon achieves 23.8 FPS on a single GPU, 30 to 180 times faster than existing methods, while maintaining high-quality, coherent long-video generation.
为满足电商和内容创作中对实时交互式服装定制的需求，本文提出FashionChameleon框架，可在仅使用单件服装视频数据的前提下，实现生成过程中用户交互式更换服装并保持动作连贯性。该方法采用基于单参考服装对的教师模型，通过上下文学习训练，并利用图像到视频的训练范式强制保持连贯性。生成阶段引入流式蒸馏与上下文学习，通过教师强制和梯度重加权分布匹配提升一致性。为实现交互式服装切换，提出无训练KV缓存重排机制，通过服装KV刷新、历史KV回收与参考KV解耦保持动作连贯。实验表明，该框架在单GPU上实现23.8 FPS实时生成，比现有方法快30至180倍，同时支持多服装交互定制与长视频外推。
A Clinician-Centered Pipeline for Annotation and Evaluation in Ultrasound AI Studies
Authors: Fangyijie Wang, Jianjun Yu, Wentao Shi, Haixia Huang, Ran Shi, Guénolé Silvestre, Kathleen M. Curran
First: 2026-06-17T15:16:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:16:06+00:00
Comments: Accepted to MIUA 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Clinician-centered evaluation is critical for validating medical AI systems, especially in ultrasound imaging where quantitative metrics do not always capture clinical usability. Existing medical image platforms primarily focus on dataset labeling. They lack integrated support for blinded model comparison and reproducible evaluation workflows. We present a clinician-centered pipeline for remote annotation and evaluation in ultrasound AI studies. The proposed pipeline uses a centralized server and lightweight browser interfaces to enable clinicians to perform annotation, blinded ranking, and review without local dataset downloads. The pipeline also supports multi-rater participation, centralized result aggregation, and automated statistical analysis. We validate the pipeline in a fetal ultrasound segmentation study with six raters spanning expert, generalist, and non-expert experience levels. The system automatically generated Spearman correlation, Kendall's $τ$, and top-1 selection statistics. Results indicated moderate to strong agreement across experts and other groups. The blinded evaluation results showed a tendency for later active learning models to be preferred. These outcomes suggest that the pipeline can support clinician-centered annotation and reproducible human-\ac{AI} evaluation studies in ultrasound imaging. The proposed pipeline is available on \href{https://github.com/13204942/SonoRate}{GitHub}.

标题：面向超声AI研究的以临床医生为中心的标注与评估流程

为弥补现有医学图像平台在超声AI评估中缺乏临床医生中心化工具的不足，本研究提出一套集中式管道，支持远程标注、盲评模型比较及可复现评估，无需本地下载数据集。系统采用轻量级浏览器界面，支持多评阅者参与、集中结果聚合与自动化统计分析。在胎儿超声分割研究中，六名不同经验水平的评阅者参与测试，系统自动生成斯皮尔曼和肯德尔τ相关系数及Top-1选择指标，结果显示评阅者间中度至强一致性，且后期主动学习模型在盲评中更受青睐。
User as Engram: Internalizing Per-User Memory as Local Parametric Edits
Authors: Bojie Li
First: 2026-06-17T15:15:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:15:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Personal memory in a language model is two problems: content and reasoning skill. The brain keeps the two apart (a sparse, local engram in the hippocampus for each episode, a slow neocortex for the shared skills that interpret it), so a new fact need not overwrite everything else. Most personalization today keeps a user's facts outside the weights, in a natural-language memory file or a retrieval index. When facts are written into the model instead, the standard recipe is the per-user LoRA adapter, which does the opposite of the brain, folding content and skill into one global weight delta. Writing a user's facts as a LoRA contaminates text unrelated to them; writing the same facts as local Engram rows leaves it mathematically untouched, resulting in a roughly 33,000x smaller memory footprint. We therefore propose User as Engram: store a user's content as surgical edits to the hash-keyed memory table of an Engram model, and carry the reasoning skill in one shared adapter. This layered design matches per-user LoRA's direct recall while delivering 5.6x higher indirect-reasoning accuracy on average, and never makes a single user worse at reasoning than the untouched base. The edit is a glass box: writing a fact switches on its lookup at exactly the trigger, adds the value the answer needs, leaves every other position unchanged to the last bit, and fails if written into the wrong layer. Because different users' facts land in disjoint hash slots, their edits compose: many users live in one shared table at once, stacking additively and losslessly, where a per-user LoRA, a single global weight delta, admits only one. Upon retrieval, a per-user Engram table does not grow with the population the retriever must search, so past ~100 facts it overtakes a retrieval pipeline on a 2.5x larger model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用户作为记忆痕迹：将用户专属记忆内化为局部参数编辑
语言模型中的个人记忆包含两个问题：内容与推理能力。大脑将二者分离（每个事件在海马体中保留稀疏局部记忆痕迹，新皮层则缓慢存储共享技能以解释这些事件），因此新事实无需覆盖其他内容。当前大多数个性化方法将用户事实存储在模型权重之外，如自然语言记忆文件或检索索引。当事实直接写入模型时，标准做法是为每个用户使用LoRA适配器，这与大脑机制相反，将内容与技能合并为一个全局权重增量。将用户事实以LoRA形式写入会污染无关文本；而以局部记忆痕迹行形式写入则数学上无影响，内存占用减少约33,000倍。 因此我们提出“用户作为记忆痕迹”：将用户内容存储为对Engram模型哈希键记忆表的精准编辑，并通过一个共享适配器承载推理能力。该分层设计在保持与用户LoRA直接召回能力的同时，平均间接推理准确率提升5.6倍，且从未使任何用户推理能力劣于未修改的基线模型。编辑过程透明：写入事实会在精确触发时激活其查找，添加所需答案值，其余位置保持原状至最后一位，若写入错误层则失败。因不同用户事实落入不相交的哈希槽，其编辑可叠加：多个用户可同时存在于一个共享表中，加法式无损叠加，而用户LoRA（单一全局权重增量）仅允许一个用户存在。检索时，每个用户Engram表不随检索器需搜索的用户数增长，当事实数超过约100条时，其在2.5倍大模型上超越检索管道。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the brain's separation of episodic memory and reasoning skills, this work proposes 'User as Engram' to store per-user facts as localized, hash-keyed edits in a memory table while preserving shared reasoning via a single adapter. Unlike per-user LoRA, which blends content and skill into a global weight delta, this method isolates user facts as surgical, layer-specific modifications that leave other weights untouched, reducing memory footprint by 33,000x. Experiments show it achieves 5.6x higher indirect-reasoning accuracy on average without harming base reasoning performance, and supports additive, lossless composition of multiple users in a shared table, outperforming retrieval-based methods when user count exceeds ~100.
受大脑将事件记忆与推理技能分离机制启发，该研究提出“用户作为记忆痕迹”方法，将用户专属事实存储为哈希键索引的局部编辑，而共享推理能力则通过单一适配器实现。与将用户内容与技能混合的LoRA不同，该方法以手术式、分层方式更新记忆表，不干扰无关权重，内存占用降低33000倍。实验表明，其平均间接推理准确率提升5.6倍，且未损害基础推理能力，同时支持多个用户在共享表中无损叠加——这是LoRA无法实现的——并在用户事实超过约100条时，优于检索系统。
Benchmarking Physics-Informed Time-Series Models for Operational Global Station Weather Forecasting
Authors: Tao Han, Zhibin Wen, Zhenghao Chen, Dazhao Du, Song Guo, Lei Bai
First: 2024-06-20T15:18:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:14:49+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The development of Time-Series Forecasting (TSF) models is often constrained by the lack of comprehensive datasets, especially in Global Station Weather Forecasting (GSWF), where existing datasets are small, temporally short, and spatially sparse. To address this, we introduce WEATHER-5K, a large-scale observational weather dataset that better reflects real-world conditions, supporting improved model training and evaluation. While recent TSF methods perform well on benchmarks, they lag behind operational Numerical Weather Prediction systems in capturing complex weather dynamics and extreme events. We propose PhysicsFormer, a physics-informed forecasting model combining a dynamic core with a Transformer residual to predict future weather states. Physical consistency is enforced via pressure-wind alignment and energy-aware smoothness losses, ensuring plausible dynamics while capturing complex temporal patterns. We benchmark PhysicsFormer and other TSF models against operational systems across several weather variables, extreme event prediction, and model complexity, providing a comprehensive assessment of the gap between academic TSF models and operational forecasting. The dataset and benchmark implementation are available at: https://github.com/taohan10200/WEATHER-5K.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向业务级全球站点气象预报的物理信息时序模型基准评测
时序预测（TSF）模型的发展常受限于数据集的匮乏，尤其在全球站点气象预报（GSWF）中，现有数据集规模小、时间短、空间稀疏。为此，我们引入WEATHER-5K，一个大规模观测气象数据集，更贴近真实世界条件，支持模型训练与评估的提升。尽管近期TSF方法在基准测试中表现良好，但在捕捉复杂气象动态与极端事件方面仍落后于业务级数值天气预报系统。我们提出PhysicsFormer，一种结合动态核心与Transformer残差结构的物理信息预测模型，用于预测未来气象状态。通过气压-风对齐与能量感知平滑损失，确保物理一致性，同时捕捉复杂时序模式。我们在多个气象变量、极端事件预测及模型复杂度方面，将PhysicsFormer与其他TSF模型与业务系统进行基准对比，全面评估学术TSF模型与业务预报之间的差距。数据集与基准实现代码见：https://github.com/taohan10200/WEATHER-5K。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing datasets and the performance gap between academic time-series forecasting models and operational weather systems, this work introduces WEATHER-5K, a large-scale observational weather dataset designed to better reflect real-world spatiotemporal conditions. The authors propose PhysicsFormer, a physics-informed model that integrates a dynamic core with a Transformer residual, enforcing physical consistency through pressure-wind alignment and energy-aware smoothness losses. Experimental results show PhysicsFormer outperforms other TSF models in capturing complex weather dynamics and extreme events, while remaining competitive with operational systems in terms of accuracy and robustness across multiple weather variables and model complexities.
为解决现有气象数据集规模小、时空稀疏的问题，本文提出WEATHER-5K大规模观测气象数据集，更贴近真实世界条件。同时提出PhysicsFormer模型，结合动态核心与Transformer残差结构，并通过气压风场对齐与能量感知平滑损失确保物理一致性。实验表明，PhysicsFormer在捕捉复杂天气动态与极端事件方面优于其他时序模型，且在多种气象变量和模型复杂度下，其性能接近业务数值天气预报系统。
Enhancing CVRP Solver through LLM-driven Automatic Heuristic Design
Authors: Zhuoliang Xie, Fei Liu, Zhenkun Wang, Qingfu Zhang
First: 2026-02-26T15:12:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:14:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (CVRP), a fundamental combinatorial optimization challenge, focuses on optimizing fleet operations under vehicle capacity constraints. While extensively studied in operational research, the NP-hard nature of CVRP continues to pose significant computational challenges, particularly for large-scale instances. This study presents AILS-AHD (Adaptive Iterated Local Search with Automatic Heuristic Design), a novel approach that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to revolutionize CVRP solving. Our methodology integrates an evolutionary search framework with LLMs to dynamically generate and optimize ruin heuristics within the AILS method. Additionally, we introduce an LLM-based acceleration mechanism to enhance computational efficiency. Comprehensive experimental evaluations against state-of-the-art solvers, including AILS-II and HGS, demonstrate the superior performance of AILS-AHD across both moderate and large-scale instances. Notably, our approach establishes new best-known solutions for 8 out of 10 instances in the CVRPLib large-scale benchmark, underscoring the potential of LLM-driven heuristic design in advancing the field of vehicle routing optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过大语言模型驱动的自动启发式设计增强CVRP求解器
容量约束车辆路径问题（CVRP）是基础组合优化挑战，旨在优化车辆容量约束下的车队运营。尽管在运筹学中被广泛研究，CVRP的NP难特性仍对大规模实例的计算构成重大挑战。本研究提出AILS-AHD（自适应迭代局部搜索与自动启发式设计），一种利用大语言模型（LLM）革新CVRP求解的新方法。该方法将进化搜索框架与LLM结合，在AILS方法中动态生成并优化破坏启发式。此外，我们引入基于LLM的加速机制以提升计算效率。与当前最先进求解器（包括AILS-II和HGS）的全面实验评估表明，AILS-AHD在中等和大规模实例上均表现更优。值得注意的是，我们的方法在CVRPLib大规模基准的10个实例中8个建立了新最优解，凸显了LLM驱动启发式设计在车辆路径优化领域的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational difficulty of the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (CVRP), especially for large-scale instances, this study introduces AILS-AHD, an LLM-driven method that combines adaptive iterated local search with automatic heuristic design. The approach uses large language models to dynamically generate and optimize ruin heuristics within the AILS framework, while also incorporating an LLM-based acceleration mechanism to improve efficiency. Experimental results show AILS-AHD outperforms state-of-the-art solvers like AILS-II and HGS, establishing new best-known solutions for 8 out of 10 large-scale CVRPLib instances.
为应对容量车辆路径问题（CVRP）在大规模实例中的计算挑战，本研究提出AILS-AHD方法，将大语言模型（LLM）融入自适应迭代局部搜索框架，自动设计并优化破坏启发式策略，同时引入LLM加速机制提升效率。实验表明，该方法在CVRPLib大规模基准测试中优于AILS-II和HGS等现有顶尖求解器，为10个实例中的8个创造了新的最优解，验证了LLM驱动启发式设计在复杂路径优化中的有效性。
Why Automate This? Exploring Correlations Between Desire for Robotic Automation, Invested Time and Well-Being
Authors: Ruchira Ray, Leona Pang, Sanjana Srivastava, Li Fei-Fei, Samantha Shorey, Roberto Martín-Martín
First: 2025-01-10T21:20:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:14:05+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 14 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Understanding the motivations underlying the human inclination to automate tasks is vital for developing robots that fit seamlessly into daily life. Accordingly, we ask: are individuals more inclined to automate activities based on the time they consume or the feelings experienced while performing them? This study explores these preferences and whether they vary across social groups, specifically gender category and income level. Leveraging data from the BEHAVIOR-1K dataset, the American Time-Use Survey, and the American Time-Use Survey Well-Being Module, we investigate the relationship between the desire for robot automation, time spent, and associated feelings: Happiness, Meaningfulness, Sadness, Painfulness, Stressfulness, or Tiredness. Our key findings show that, despite common assumptions, time spent on activities does not strongly predict automation preferences; instead, happiness and pain are the strongest indicators. We also identify differences by gender and economic level: Women prefer to automate stressful activities, whereas men prefer to automate those that make them unhappy; mid-income individuals prioritize automating less enjoyable and meaningful activities, while low and high-income show no significant correlations. We hope our research helps motivate the design of robots that align with user priorities, moving domestic robotics toward more socially relevant solutions. All data and an interactive tool are publicly available at https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/why-automate-this/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为何要自动化？探索对机器人自动化的需求、投入时间与幸福感之间的关联
理解人类倾向于自动化任务背后的动机，对于开发无缝融入日常生活的机器人至关重要。本研究探讨：人们更倾向于根据活动所耗费的时间，还是在执行过程中所体验到的感受，来决定自动化？我们利用BEHAVIOR-1K数据集、美国时间使用调查及幸福感模块数据，研究对机器人自动化的渴望、投入时间及关联情绪（幸福、有意义、悲伤、痛苦、压力、疲惫）之间的关系。主要发现表明，尽管普遍假设如此，活动耗时并非自动化偏好的强预测因子；相反，幸福与痛苦是最重要的指标。我们还发现性别与经济水平的差异：女性更倾向自动化压力性活动，男性更倾向自动化令人不快的活动；中等收入人群优先自动化不愉快且无意义的活动，而低收入与高收入人群则无显著相关性。我们希望本研究能推动机器人设计更贴合用户优先级，使家庭机器人向更具社会相关性的解决方案发展。所有数据及交互工具公开于https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/why-automate-this/。

This study investigates why people desire to automate daily tasks, focusing on whether time investment or emotional experience drives automation preferences. Using large-scale survey data from the BEHAVIOR-1K dataset and the American Time-Use Survey, researchers analyze correlations between automation desire, time spent, and emotional states such as happiness, pain, stress, and tiredness, while also examining variations by gender and income level. Results show that time spent on activities is not a strong predictor of automation desire; instead, happiness and pain are the most influential emotional indicators. Gender and income also shape preferences: women tend to automate stressful tasks, men automate unhappy tasks, and mid-income individuals prioritize automating less meaningful or enjoyable activities, while low and high-income groups show no significant emotional correlations with automation desire.

Dango: A Strictly L1-Only Large Language Model for Studying Second Language Acquisition
Authors: Shiho Matta, Yin Jou Huang, Fei Cheng, Takashi Kodama, Hirokazu Kiyomaru, Yugo Murawaki
First: 2026-06-17T15:13:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:13:19+00:00
Comments: 8 pages main text, 20 pages total including references and appendices
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Dango, a 1.8B-parameter large language model designed for controlled studies of L1-to-L2 (Japanese-to-English) transfer in second language acquisition (SLA). While previous studies have explored SLA in language models, they have predominantly relied on smaller or non-decoder models, limiting their ability to generate open-ended text and reducing their suitability as practical L2 simulators. We identify a key challenge when scaling models to this size: L2 contamination within the "monolingual" pretraining corpus used for L1 acquisition. To address this, we propose a filtering method to reduce premature exposure to English while preserving realistic, minimal exposure. We then fine-tune the model on LLM-generated L2-learning lessons to simulate the L2 acquisition process. Our evaluations confirm that Dango develops human-like L2 production patterns, outperforming both unfiltered and standard multilingual baselines. We release the model, data, and code to facilitate reproducible computational SLA research and learner-facing applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Dango：专用于研究第二语言习得的纯L1大型语言模型
我们提出了Dango，一个18亿参数的大型语言模型，专为控制研究L1到L2（日语到英语）的迁移现象而设计。此前研究虽已探索语言模型中的第二语言习得（SLA），但多依赖较小或非解码器模型，限制了其生成开放式文本的能力，且不适合作为实用的L2模拟器。我们识别出在模型扩展至该规模时的关键挑战：L1习得所用的“单语”预训练语料库中存在L2污染。为此，我们提出一种过滤方法，以减少英语的过早暴露，同时保留现实且最小的接触。随后，我们使用LLM生成的L2学习课程对模型进行微调，以模拟L2习得过程。评估结果表明，Dango展现出类人L2生成模式，优于未过滤及标准多语言基线模型。我们发布模型、数据和代码，以促进可复现的计算SLA研究及面向学习者的应用。

为研究母语到第二语言的迁移过程，作者提出Dango，一个1.8B参数的仅限母语（日语）训练的大语言模型，通过新设计的过滤方法减少英语污染，同时保留少量真实接触。模型在LLM生成的第二语言学习材料上进行微调以模拟语言习得过程。实验表明，Dango能生成类人第二语言产出，显著优于未过滤和标准多语言基线模型。
Beyond Safe Data: Pretraining-Stage Alignment with Regular Safety Reflection
Authors: Jinhan Li, Kexian Tang, Yihan Xu, Zhuorui Ye, Kaifeng Lyu
First: 2026-06-17T15:11:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:11:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
To achieve deeper safety alignment for large language models (LLMs), recent efforts have studied how to push safety interventions earlier into the pretraining stage, primarily by filtering unsafe data or rewriting it into safer forms. We argue that pretraining-stage alignment should go beyond making the data safe: LLMs may compose seemingly benign knowledge and capabilities into unsafe behaviors. To this end, we propose Safety Reflection Pretraining, a pretraining-stage alignment method which regularly inserts short safety reflections into pretraining corpora to integrate self-monitoring directly into language modeling, establishing a foundational capability that is subsequently reinforced by compatible post-training. Our experiments with 1.7B models pretrained on FineWeb-Edu show that Safety Reflection Pretraining improves safety classification accuracy and substantially reduces the success rates of inference-stage and finetuning attacks. Complementary to our real-world experiments, we also introduce a fully controlled synthetic environment, MedSafetyWorld, with a clear definition of safety and a reasoning structure under which models can easily generalize unsafe behaviors from safe data. Ablations in MedSafetyWorld further demonstrate a clear advantage of Safety Reflection Pretraining in preventing models from acting on unsafe behaviors generalized from safe data, compared with data filtering and rewriting. Taken together, our findings suggest that pretraining alignment should not only make the training data safe, but also shape the behaviors that models are likely to acquire from safe data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越安全数据：通过定期安全反思实现预训练阶段对齐
为实现大型语言模型（LLM）更深层次的安全对齐，近期研究探索如何将安全干预更早引入预训练阶段，主要通过过滤不安全数据或将其改写为更安全的形式。我们认为，预训练阶段的对齐不应仅限于使数据安全：LLM可能将看似无害的知识与能力组合成不安全行为。为此，我们提出安全反思预训练方法，该方法在预训练语料中定期插入简短的安全反思，将自我监控直接整合到语言建模中，建立基础能力，并在后续兼容的微调中加以强化。我们在FineWeb-Edu数据集上对1.7B模型的实验表明，安全反思预训练可提升安全分类准确率，并显著降低推理阶段和微调阶段攻击的成功率。此外，我们还引入了一个完全可控的合成环境MedSafetyWorld，该环境具有明确的安全定义和推理结构，使模型能从安全数据中轻松泛化出不安全行为。在MedSafetyWorld中的消融实验进一步表明，与数据过滤和改写相比，安全反思预训练在防止模型执行从安全数据泛化出的不安全行为方面具有明显优势。综合来看，我们的研究发现表明，预训练对齐不仅应使训练数据安全，还应塑造模型从安全数据中可能习得的行为。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to achieve deeper safety alignment in large language models, this work argues that merely filtering or rewriting unsafe data during pretraining is insufficient, as models may still compose harmful behaviors from seemingly benign knowledge. The authors propose Safety Reflection Pretraining, a method that inserts short safety reflection prompts into the pretraining corpus to embed self-monitoring directly into language modeling, which is then reinforced in post-training. Experiments on 1.7B models pretrained on FineWeb-Edu show that this approach improves safety classification accuracy and significantly reduces success rates of inference-stage and fine-tuning attacks. In a controlled synthetic environment called MedSafetyWorld, ablation studies further confirm that Safety Reflection Pretraining outperforms data filtering and rewriting in preventing models from generalizing unsafe behaviors from safe data.
为实现大语言模型更深层次的安全对齐，本文指出仅在预训练阶段过滤或重写不安全数据不足以防止模型将看似安全的知识组合成有害行为。作者提出“安全反思预训练”方法，在预训练语料中定期插入简短的安全反思提示，将自我监控能力直接嵌入语言建模过程，并在后续训练中加以强化。在FineWeb-Edu数据集上对1.7B模型的实验表明，该方法提升了安全分类准确率，并显著降低了推理阶段和微调阶段攻击的成功率。在合成环境MedSafetyWorld中，消融实验进一步证明，与数据过滤和重写相比，安全反思预训练更有效防止模型从安全数据中泛化出危险行为。
Essential Subspace Merging for Multi-Task Learning
Authors: Longhua Li, Lei Qi, Xin Geng, Qi Tian
First: 2026-06-17T15:07:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-17T15:07:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Model merging aims to enable multi-task learning by integrating the capabilities of multiple models fine-tuned from the same pre-trained checkpoint into a single model. Its core challenge is inter-task interference among task-specific parameter updates. In this paper, we analyze the output shifts induced by task updates and observe that their energy is concentrated in a small number of principal directions. We call the subspace spanned by these directions the essential subspace. In contrast, most remaining directions carry little task-relevant energy, but their accumulation across multiple task updates can cause severe interference during merging. Motivated by this observation, we propose Essential Subspace Decomposition (ESD), which decomposes each task update according to the principal components of its activation shift. Based on ESD, we introduce Essential Subspace Merging (ESM), a training-free static merging method that orthogonalizes and fuses essential components into one compact multi-task model. We further extend ESM to ESM++, a training-free dynamic merging method that decomposes task-specific residuals into low-rank experts and selects the most relevant expert through prototype-based routing during forward inference. Extensive experiments across multiple task sets and model scales demonstrate that ESM and ESM++ effectively preserves task knowledge while reducing inter-task interference.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多任务学习中的关键子空间融合
模型融合旨在通过将从同一预训练检查点微调出的多个模型的能力整合到单个模型中，实现多任务学习。其核心挑战是任务特定参数更新之间的任务间干扰。本文分析了任务更新引起的输出偏移，并观察到其能量集中在少数主方向上。我们称这些方向张成的子空间为关键子空间。相比之下，大部分剩余方向携带的任务相关能量较小，但其在多次任务更新中的累积会导致融合过程中的严重干扰。受此观察启发，我们提出关键子空间分解（ESD），根据激活偏移的主成分分解每个任务更新。基于ESD，我们引入关键子空间融合（ESM），这是一种无需训练的静态融合方法，通过正交化并融合关键成分，生成一个紧凑的多任务模型。我们进一步将ESM扩展为ESM++，一种无需训练的动态融合方法，它将任务特定残差分解为低秩专家，并在前向推理过程中通过原型路由选择最相关的专家。在多个任务集和模型规模上的大量实验表明，ESM和ESM++能有效保留任务知识并减少任务间干扰。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the problem of inter-task interference during model merging in multi-task learning, this paper identifies that task-specific parameter updates induce output shifts concentrated in a small subspace, termed the essential subspace. It proposes Essential Subspace Decomposition (ESD) to decompose task updates along principal activation directions, then introduces Essential Subspace Merging (ESM), a training-free static method that orthogonalizes and fuses essential components into a compact multi-task model. ESM++ extends this to a dynamic method using low-rank experts and prototype-based routing for inference. Experiments across multiple tasks and model scales show both methods preserve task knowledge while significantly reducing interference.
为解决多任务学习中模型合并时的任务间干扰问题，本文发现任务特定参数更新引起的输出偏移能量集中在少数主方向，称为“关键子空间”。作者提出关键子空间分解（ESD）方法，将任务更新沿主方向分解，并在此基础上设计了无需训练的静态合并方法“关键子空间合并”（ESM），通过正交化与融合关键成分构建紧凑的多任务模型。进一步扩展为动态方法ESM++，在推理时通过原型路由选择最相关的低秩专家处理任务残差。实验表明，ESM与ESM++在多个任务集和模型规模下均能有效保留各任务知识并显著降低任务间干扰。
